@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("aa_svd_compressor")
class AASVDCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        tokenizer: Any | None = None,
        rank: int = 288,
        ema_beta: float = 0.9,
        max_error: float = 0.50,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        calibration_texts: list[str] | None = None,
        device: str | torch.device | None = None,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        target_attention_only: bool = True,
        force_rank: bool = False,
        lora_rank: int = 0,  # AASVD-FIX-03
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,  # AASVD-FIX-03
        cov_dtype: str = "float32",  # AASVD-FIX-09
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="aasvd", **kwargs)
        cfg = kwargs.get("comp_config", kwargs.get("cfg"))
        # AASVD-FIX-02
        self.comp_config = cfg

        # AASVD-FIX-04: use _cfg_get everywhere
        rank = _cfg_get(cfg, "aasvd_rank", _cfg_get(cfg, "rank", rank))
        ema_beta = float(_cfg_get(cfg, "aasvd_ema_beta", _cfg_get(cfg, "ema_beta", ema_beta)))
        tokenizer = _cfg_get(cfg, "tokenizer", tokenizer)
        calibration_texts = _cfg_get(cfg, "calibration_texts", calibration_texts)
        max_error = float(_cfg_get(cfg, "aasvd_max_error", _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error)))
        fallback_to_original = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "aasvd_fallback_to_original",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original),
            )
        )
        target_attention_only = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "aasvd_target_attention_only",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only),
            )
        )
        lora_rank = int(_cfg_get(cfg, "aasvd_lora_rank", _cfg_get(cfg, "lora_rank", lora_rank)))
        lora_alpha = float(
            _cfg_get(cfg, "aasvd_lora_alpha", _cfg_get(cfg, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha))
        )
        cov_dtype = str(_cfg_get(cfg, "cov_dtype", cov_dtype))

        # override from kwargs (highest priority after explicit args)
        lora_rank = int(kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank))
        lora_alpha = float(kwargs.get("lora_alpha", lora_alpha))
        cov_dtype = str(kwargs.get("cov_dtype", cov_dtype))

        # force_rank resolution
        force_rank = bool(
            force_rank
            or kwargs.get("force_rank", False)
            or (cfg is not None and _cfg_get(cfg, "force_rank", False))
            or getattr(getattr(model, "config", None), "force_rank", False)
        )
        self.force_rank = force_rank
        self.use_strs = bool(kwargs.get("use_strs", not force_rank)) and not force_rank
        # AASVD-FIX-10: dynamic STRS candidates are computed per-layer

        # ---- tokenizer auto-resolve ----
        if tokenizer is None:
            tokenizer = getattr(model, "tokenizer", None) or getattr(
                getattr(model, "config", None), "tokenizer", None
            )
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        if self.tokenizer is None:
            mp = getattr(getattr(model, "config", None), "_name_or_path", None)
            if mp:
                try:
                    from transformers import AutoTokenizer

                    self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(mp)
                    if getattr(self.tokenizer, "pad_token", None) is None:
                        self.tokenizer.pad_token = self.tokenizer.eos_token
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("Auto-tokenizer load failed for %s: %s", mp, e)
                    self.tokenizer = None

        # ---- basic params ----
        self.rank = max(1, int(rank))
        self.ema_beta = float(max(0.0, min(ema_beta, 0.999)))
        self.max_error = float(max_error)
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(fallback_to_original)
        self.target_attention_only = bool(target_attention_only)
        self.calibration_texts = calibration_texts or DEFAULT_CALIBRATION_CORPUS
        self.target_modules = target_modules
        self.lora_rank = max(0, int(lora_rank))
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        # AASVD-FIX-03
        self.lora_r = self.lora_rank  # legacy alias

        # AASVD-FIX-09
        self.cov_dtype_name = str(cov_dtype).lower()
        if self.cov_dtype_name not in ("float32", "float16"):
            self.cov_dtype_name = "float32"
        self.cov_dtype = torch.float32 if self.cov_dtype_name == "float32" else torch.float16

        # AASVD-FIX-08: single source of truth for device
        self.device = (
            torch.device(device)
            if device is not None
            else next(model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
        )

        # ---- state ----
        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.layer_ranks: dict[str, int] = {}

        # AASVD-FIX-14
        self._compressed_params: int = 0
        try:
            self._original_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        except Exception:
            self._original_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        # AASVD-FIX-11
        self._orig_storage_bytes: int = 0
        self._comp_storage_bytes: int = 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Target selection
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        # AASVD-FIX-13: skip already compressed
        if isinstance(module, SVDLinear):
            return False
        if any(
            name.endswith(sfx) for sfx in (".base_layer", ".lora_A", ".lora_B", ".lora_dropout")
        ):
            return False

        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
        target_cls = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()

        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(module, nn.Linear)
            or isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
            or "conv1d" in cls_name
            or "conv1d" in target_cls
            or hasattr(module, "nf")
            or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            or "loralinear" in cls_name
            or hasattr(module, "base_layer")
        )
        if not is_linear_like:
            return False

        name_lower = name.lower()
        if any(
            skip in name_lower
            for skip in (
                "lm_head",
                "classifier",
                "head",
                "output_layer",
                "score",
                "pooler",
                "embedding",
                "layernorm",
                "rmsnorm",
            )
        ):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            ffn_patterns = (
                "ffn",
                "mlp",
                "lin1",
                "lin2",
                "intermediate",
                "gate_proj",
                "up_proj",
                "down_proj",
                "c_fc",
            )
            if any(p in name_lower for p in ffn_patterns):
                return False
            if "output.dense" in name_lower and "attention" not in name_lower:
                return False
            attn_patterns = (
                "attn",
                "attention",
                "q_proj",
                "k_proj",
                "v_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "out_lin",
                "q_lin",
                "k_lin",
                "v_lin",
                "c_attn",
                "c_proj",
                "query",
                "key",
                "value",
            )
            if not any(p in name_lower for p in attn_patterns):
                return False
        else:
            try:
                if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
                    return False
            except Exception:
                pass

        if self.target_modules is not None:
            return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)
        return True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # AASVD-FIX-10: dynamic STRS candidates
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _build_strs_candidates(r_floor: int, r_ceiling: int) -> list[int]:
        if r_ceiling < r_floor:
            return [r_ceiling]
        cands = set()
        n = max(4, min(10, int(math.log2(max(2, r_ceiling // max(1, r_floor)))) + 2))
        for i in range(n):
            t = i / (n - 1) if n > 1 else 0
            r = int(round(r_floor * (r_ceiling / max(1, r_floor)) ** t))
            cands.add(max(r_floor, min(r, r_ceiling)))
        cands.add(r_floor)
        cands.add(r_ceiling)
        return sorted(cands)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress_linear(
        self,
        layer: nn.Module,
        rank: int | None = None,
        cov: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        cov_factors: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[SVDLinear | None, float, int]:
        try:
            target_mod = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
            cls_name = layer.__class__.__name__.lower()
            target_cls = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = (
                "conv1d" in cls_name
                or "conv1d" in target_cls
                or hasattr(layer, "nf")
                or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            )
            weight_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "weight", getattr(layer, "weight", None))
            if weight_tensor is None:
                return None, float("inf"), 0

            raw_w = weight_tensor.data
            if is_conv1d:
                in_feat, out_feat = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
                W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
            else:
                out_feat, in_feat = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
                W = raw_w.float()

            r_default = rank if rank is not None else self.rank
            r = get_dynamic_compression_rank(
                in_feat,
                out_feat,
                model_or_family=self.model,
                default_rank=r_default,
                comp_config=self.comp_config,
            )
            max_allowed_rank = max(
                1,
                min(
                    in_feat,
                    out_feat,
                    int(0.75 * (in_feat * out_feat) / (in_feat + out_feat)),
                ),
            )
            r = min(r, max_allowed_rank)
            W_norm = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()

            # ---- covariance preconditioning ----
            cov_sqrt = cov_inv_sqrt = None
            if cov_factors is not None:
                cov_sqrt, cov_inv_sqrt = cov_factors
            elif cov is not None and cov.shape == (in_feat, in_feat):
                try:
                    cov_fp32 = cov.to(self.device, torch.float32)
                    cov_sym = 0.5 * (cov_fp32 + cov_fp32.T)
                    eigvals, eigvecs = torch.linalg.eigh(cov_sym)
                    eps = 1e-6 * torch.max(eigvals).clamp(min=1e-12) + 1e-8
                    eigvals_reg = eigvals.clamp(min=eps)
                    cov_sqrt = eigvecs @ torch.diag(torch.sqrt(eigvals_reg)) @ eigvecs.T
                    cov_inv_sqrt = eigvecs @ torch.diag(1.0 / torch.sqrt(eigvals_reg)) @ eigvecs.T
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("AASVD covariance sqrt failed for %s: %s", e, e)
                    cov_sqrt, cov_inv_sqrt = None, None

            W_weighted = W @ cov_sqrt if cov_sqrt is not None else W

            W_w_dev = W_weighted.to(self.device, torch.float32)
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W_w_dev, full_matrices=False)

            # ---- STRS / fixed rank ----
            if not self.use_strs:
                selected_r = min(r, len(S))
                sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:selected_r], min=1e-12))
                best_Ur = U[:, :selected_r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
                best_Vr = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:selected_r, :]
                if cov_inv_sqrt is not None:
                    best_Vr = best_Vr @ cov_inv_sqrt
                best_err = float(
                    torch.norm(W - best_Ur @ best_Vr, p="fro").item() / max(W_norm, 1e-12)
                )
            else:
                r_floor = max(1, min(r, max_allowed_rank, len(S)))
                r_ceiling = max_allowed_rank
                cand_ranks = self._build_strs_candidates(r_floor, r_ceiling)
                cand_ranks = [c for c in cand_ranks if c <= len(S)]

                best_err = float("inf")
                selected_r = cand_ranks[0] if cand_ranks else r_floor
                best_Ur = best_Vr = None

                for test_r in cand_ranks:
                    sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:test_r], min=1e-12))
                    U_cand = U[:, :test_r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
                    V_cand = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:test_r, :]
                    if cov_inv_sqrt is not None:
                        V_cand = V_cand @ cov_inv_sqrt
                    W_cand = U_cand @ V_cand
                    test_err = float(torch.norm(W - W_cand, p="fro").item() / max(W_norm, 1e-12))
                    if test_err < best_err:
                        best_err = test_err
                        selected_r = test_r
                        best_Ur = U_cand
                        best_Vr = V_cand
                    if test_err <= self.max_error:
                        break

                if best_Ur is None or best_err > self.max_error:
                    # Fallback to break-even rank
                    be_r = max(
                        1,
                        min(
                            int(0.75 * (in_feat * out_feat) / (in_feat + out_feat)),
                            len(S),
                        ),
                    )
                    selected_r = be_r
                    sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:be_r], min=1e-12))
                    best_Ur = U[:, :be_r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
                    best_Vr = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:be_r, :]
                    if cov_inv_sqrt is not None:
                        best_Vr = best_Vr @ cov_inv_sqrt
                    best_err = float(
                        torch.norm(W - best_Ur @ best_Vr, p="fro").item() / max(W_norm, 1e-12)
                    )

            dtype = weight_tensor.dtype
            device = weight_tensor.device
            bias_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "bias", getattr(layer, "bias", None))

            svd_layer = SVDLinear(
                in_features=in_feat,
                out_features=out_feat,
                rank=selected_r,
                bias=bias_tensor is not None,
                dtype=dtype,
                device=device,
                lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
                lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            )
            svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(
                best_Ur.to(device=device, dtype=dtype),
                best_Vr.to(device=device, dtype=dtype),
            )
            if bias_tensor is not None and svd_layer.bias is not None:
                svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(bias_tensor.data.to(svd_layer.bias.dtype))

            svd_layer.reconstruction_error = best_err
            svd_layer.rank = selected_r
            return svd_layer, best_err, selected_r
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("AASVD factorization failed: %s", e)
            return None, float("inf"), 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        self.model.eval()
        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.layer_errors = {}
        self.layer_ranks = {}
        self._orig_storage_bytes = 0
        self._comp_storage_bytes = 0

        covariances: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        # AASVD-FIX-06: track per-layer update count
        update_counts: dict[str, int] = {}

        if self.tokenizer is None:
            logger.info("AASVD: no tokenizer; using unweighted SVD.")
        else:
            hooks = []

            def make_hook(layer_name: str):
                nonlocal covariances, update_counts

                def hook(module, inp, outp):
                    if not inp or inp[0] is None:
                        return
                    x = inp[0].detach()
                    # AASVD-FIX-05
                    with _autocast_off(x):
                        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                        cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
                        target_cls = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
                        is_conv1d = (
                            "conv1d" in cls_name
                            or "conv1d" in target_cls
                            or hasattr(module, "nf")
                            or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
                        )
                        wt = getattr(target_mod, "weight", getattr(module, "weight", None))
                        if wt is None:
                            return
                        in_f = wt.shape[0] if is_conv1d else wt.shape[1]

                        x_fp32 = x.float()
                        x_flat = x_fp32.reshape(-1, in_f)
                        # AASVD-FIX-12
                        valid = x_flat.abs().amax(dim=-1) > 0
                        if valid.sum() == 0:
                            return
                        x_active = x_flat[valid]
                        n_tokens = max(1, x_active.size(0))
                        batch_cov = (x_active.t() @ x_active / n_tokens).cpu()
                        if self.cov_dtype == torch.float16:
                            batch_cov = batch_cov.half()

                        # AASVD-FIX-06: EMA with first-observation handling
                        if layer_name not in covariances:
                            covariances[layer_name] = batch_cov
                            update_counts[layer_name] = 1
                        else:
                            beta = self.ema_beta
                            covariances[layer_name] = (
                                beta * covariances[layer_name] + (1.0 - beta) * batch_cov
                            )
                            update_counts[layer_name] += 1

                return hook

            for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
                if self._is_target_module(name, module):
                    hooks.append(module.register_forward_hook(make_hook(name)))

            try:
                inputs = self.tokenizer(
                    self.calibration_texts,
                    return_tensors="pt",
                    padding=True,
                    truncation=True,
                    max_length=512,
                )
                inputs = {k: v.to(self.device) for k, v in inputs.items() if hasattr(v, "to")}
                with torch.no_grad():
                    self.model(**inputs)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("AASVD calibration forward failed: %s", e)
            finally:
                for h in hooks:
                    h.remove()

        # ---- Precompute covariance square roots ----
        cov_factor_cache: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]] = {}
        for name, cov in covariances.items():
            try:
                cov_dev = cov.to(self.device, torch.float32)
                cov_sym = 0.5 * (cov_dev + cov_dev.T)
                eigvals, eigvecs = torch.linalg.eigh(cov_sym)
                eps = 1e-6 * torch.max(eigvals).clamp(min=1e-12) + 1e-8
                eigvals_reg = eigvals.clamp(min=eps)
                cov_sqrt = eigvecs @ torch.diag(torch.sqrt(eigvals_reg)) @ eigvecs.T
                cov_inv_sqrt = eigvecs @ torch.diag(1.0 / torch.sqrt(eigvals_reg)) @ eigvecs.T
                cov_factor_cache[name] = (cov_sqrt, cov_inv_sqrt)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("AASVD covariance factor failed for %s: %s", name, e)

        # ---- Compress each target ----
        for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
            if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                continue

            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            try:
                for p in target_mod.parameters():
                    self._orig_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
            except Exception:
                pass

            cov_factors = cov_factor_cache.get(name)
            cov = covariances.get(name) if cov_factors is None else None

            svd_layer, err, r = self.compress_linear(
                module,
                rank=self.rank,
                cov=cov,
                cov_factors=cov_factors,
            )
            self.layer_errors[name] = err
            self.layer_ranks[name] = r

            too_big = (err > self.max_error) and not self.use_strs
            if svd_layer is None or too_big:
                if self.fallback_to_original:
                    logger.warning(
                        "AASVD: %s (err=%.4f > %.4f); falling back to original.",
                        name,
                        err,
                        self.max_error,
                    )
                    self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                    self._comp_storage_bytes += (
                        self._orig_storage_bytes
                    )  # approximation; corrected below
                    continue
                logger.warning(
                    "AASVD: %s exceeds max_error but fallback is False.",
                    name,
                )
                if svd_layer is None:
                    self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                    self._comp_storage_bytes += self._orig_storage_bytes
                    continue

            if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                module.base_layer = svd_layer
                for attr in ("lora_A", "lora_B"):
                    sub = getattr(module, attr, None)
                    if sub is None:
                        continue
                    params = sub.parameters() if hasattr(sub, "parameters") else iter([sub])
                    for p in params:
                        if hasattr(p, "requires_grad"):
                            p.requires_grad = True
            else:
                replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)
            self.compressed_layers.append(name)

            try:
                for p in svd_layer.parameters():
                    self._comp_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Freeze fallback layers
        for name, mod in self.model.named_modules():
            if name in self.fallback_layers:
                target = getattr(mod, "base_layer", mod)
                for p in target.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False

        # ---- Free memory ----
        covariances.clear()
        cov_factor_cache.clear()
        del covariances
        del cov_factor_cache
        if torch.cuda.is_available():
            with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
                torch.cuda.empty_cache()

        try:
            self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        except Exception:
            self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        avg_err = (
            sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
            / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
            if self.compressed_layers
            else 0.0
        )

        # AASVD-FIX-11: proper storage accounting
        # comp_storage was approximated for fallback layers; recompute honestly.
        comp_bytes_real = 0
        for name, mod in self.model.named_modules():
            if name in self.compressed_layers or name in self.fallback_layers:
                target = getattr(mod, "base_layer", mod)
                try:
                    for p in target.parameters():
                        comp_bytes_real += p.numel() * p.element_size()
                except Exception:
                    pass
        self._comp_storage_bytes = comp_bytes_real

        storage_ratio = (
            self._orig_storage_bytes / self._comp_storage_bytes
            if self._comp_storage_bytes > 0
            else 1.0
        )
        effective_ratio = (
            self._comp_storage_bytes / self._orig_storage_bytes
            if self._orig_storage_bytes > 0
            else 1.0
        )

        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        stats.update(
            {
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "use_strs": self.use_strs,
                "rank": self.rank,
                "lora_rank": self.lora_rank,
                "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
                "orig_storage_bytes": self._orig_storage_bytes,
                "comp_storage_bytes": self._comp_storage_bytes,
                "storage_compression_ratio": round(storage_ratio, 3),
                "storage_reduction_pct": round(100.0 * (1.0 - effective_ratio), 2),
                "method": "aasvd_activation_aware_svd",
            }
        )
        logger.info(
            "AASVD: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, storage_ratio=%.2fx",
            len(self.compressed_layers),
            len(self.fallback_layers),
            avg_err,
            storage_ratio,
        )
        return self.model, stats

Base class for all compressors. Not a nn.Module to avoid parameter storage.

Expand source code 
def compress_linear(
    self,
    layer: nn.Module,
    rank: int | None = None,
    cov: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    cov_factors: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
) -> tuple[SVDLinear | None, float, int]:
    try:
        target_mod = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
        cls_name = layer.__class__.__name__.lower()
        target_cls = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = (
            "conv1d" in cls_name
            or "conv1d" in target_cls
            or hasattr(layer, "nf")
            or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        )
        weight_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "weight", getattr(layer, "weight", None))
        if weight_tensor is None:
            return None, float("inf"), 0

        raw_w = weight_tensor.data
        if is_conv1d:
            in_feat, out_feat = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
        else:
            out_feat, in_feat = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.float()

        r_default = rank if rank is not None else self.rank
        r = get_dynamic_compression_rank(
            in_feat,
            out_feat,
            model_or_family=self.model,
            default_rank=r_default,
            comp_config=self.comp_config,
        )
        max_allowed_rank = max(
            1,
            min(
                in_feat,
                out_feat,
                int(0.75 * (in_feat * out_feat) / (in_feat + out_feat)),
            ),
        )
        r = min(r, max_allowed_rank)
        W_norm = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()

        # ---- covariance preconditioning ----
        cov_sqrt = cov_inv_sqrt = None
        if cov_factors is not None:
            cov_sqrt, cov_inv_sqrt = cov_factors
        elif cov is not None and cov.shape == (in_feat, in_feat):
            try:
                cov_fp32 = cov.to(self.device, torch.float32)
                cov_sym = 0.5 * (cov_fp32 + cov_fp32.T)
                eigvals, eigvecs = torch.linalg.eigh(cov_sym)
                eps = 1e-6 * torch.max(eigvals).clamp(min=1e-12) + 1e-8
                eigvals_reg = eigvals.clamp(min=eps)
                cov_sqrt = eigvecs @ torch.diag(torch.sqrt(eigvals_reg)) @ eigvecs.T
                cov_inv_sqrt = eigvecs @ torch.diag(1.0 / torch.sqrt(eigvals_reg)) @ eigvecs.T
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("AASVD covariance sqrt failed for %s: %s", e, e)
                cov_sqrt, cov_inv_sqrt = None, None

        W_weighted = W @ cov_sqrt if cov_sqrt is not None else W

        W_w_dev = W_weighted.to(self.device, torch.float32)
        U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W_w_dev, full_matrices=False)

        # ---- STRS / fixed rank ----
        if not self.use_strs:
            selected_r = min(r, len(S))
            sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:selected_r], min=1e-12))
            best_Ur = U[:, :selected_r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
            best_Vr = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:selected_r, :]
            if cov_inv_sqrt is not None:
                best_Vr = best_Vr @ cov_inv_sqrt
            best_err = float(
                torch.norm(W - best_Ur @ best_Vr, p="fro").item() / max(W_norm, 1e-12)
            )
        else:
            r_floor = max(1, min(r, max_allowed_rank, len(S)))
            r_ceiling = max_allowed_rank
            cand_ranks = self._build_strs_candidates(r_floor, r_ceiling)
            cand_ranks = [c for c in cand_ranks if c <= len(S)]

            best_err = float("inf")
            selected_r = cand_ranks[0] if cand_ranks else r_floor
            best_Ur = best_Vr = None

            for test_r in cand_ranks:
                sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:test_r], min=1e-12))
                U_cand = U[:, :test_r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
                V_cand = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:test_r, :]
                if cov_inv_sqrt is not None:
                    V_cand = V_cand @ cov_inv_sqrt
                W_cand = U_cand @ V_cand
                test_err = float(torch.norm(W - W_cand, p="fro").item() / max(W_norm, 1e-12))
                if test_err < best_err:
                    best_err = test_err
                    selected_r = test_r
                    best_Ur = U_cand
                    best_Vr = V_cand
                if test_err <= self.max_error:
                    break

            if best_Ur is None or best_err > self.max_error:
                # Fallback to break-even rank
                be_r = max(
                    1,
                    min(
                        int(0.75 * (in_feat * out_feat) / (in_feat + out_feat)),
                        len(S),
                    ),
                )
                selected_r = be_r
                sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:be_r], min=1e-12))
                best_Ur = U[:, :be_r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
                best_Vr = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:be_r, :]
                if cov_inv_sqrt is not None:
                    best_Vr = best_Vr @ cov_inv_sqrt
                best_err = float(
                    torch.norm(W - best_Ur @ best_Vr, p="fro").item() / max(W_norm, 1e-12)
                )

        dtype = weight_tensor.dtype
        device = weight_tensor.device
        bias_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "bias", getattr(layer, "bias", None))

        svd_layer = SVDLinear(
            in_features=in_feat,
            out_features=out_feat,
            rank=selected_r,
            bias=bias_tensor is not None,
            dtype=dtype,
            device=device,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
        )
        svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(
            best_Ur.to(device=device, dtype=dtype),
            best_Vr.to(device=device, dtype=dtype),
        )
        if bias_tensor is not None and svd_layer.bias is not None:
            svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(bias_tensor.data.to(svd_layer.bias.dtype))

        svd_layer.reconstruction_error = best_err
        svd_layer.rank = selected_r
        return svd_layer, best_err, selected_r
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("AASVD factorization failed: %s", e)
        return None, float("inf"), 0

@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("adasvd_compressor")
class AdaSVDCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        rank: int | None = None,
        target_param_ratio: float = 0.7,
        num_iters: int = 10,
        max_error: float = 0.50,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        target_attention_only: bool = True,
        min_rank: int = 16,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        tokenizer: Any | None = None,
        calibration_texts: list[str] | None = None,
        use_weighted_als: bool = False,
        use_mean_shift: bool = True,
        use_gating_d: bool = True,
        device: str | torch.device | None = None,
        comp_config: Any = None,
        max_calib_tokens: int = 4096,
        fused_qkv_error_threshold: float = 0.30,
        gelu_layer_patterns: tuple[str, ...] | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="adasvd", **kwargs)
        cfg = comp_config or kwargs.get(
            "comp_config", kwargs.get("cfg", getattr(model, "config", None))
        )

        # ---- CLI overrides ----
        cli_rank = None
        cli_use_gating_d = None
        cli_use_weighted_als = None
        for idx, arg in enumerate(sys.argv):
            if arg in ("--rank", "--adasvd_rank") and idx + 1 < len(sys.argv):
                try:
                    cli_rank = int(sys.argv[idx + 1])
                except ValueError:
                    pass
            elif arg.startswith(("--rank=", "--adasvd_rank=")):
                try:
                    cli_rank = int(arg.split("=")[1])
                except ValueError:
                    pass
            elif arg in ("--adasvd_use_gating_d", "--use_gating_d") and idx + 1 < len(sys.argv):
                cli_use_gating_d = sys.argv[idx + 1].lower() in ("true", "1", "yes")
            elif arg.startswith(("--adasvd_use_gating_d=", "--use_gating_d=")):
                cli_use_gating_d = arg.split("=")[1].lower() in ("true", "1", "yes")
            elif arg in (
                "--adasvd_use_weighted_als",
                "--use_weighted_als",
            ) and idx + 1 < len(sys.argv):
                cli_use_weighted_als = sys.argv[idx + 1].lower() in ("true", "1", "yes")
            elif arg.startswith(("--adasvd_use_weighted_als=", "--use_weighted_als=")):
                cli_use_weighted_als = arg.split("=")[1].lower() in ("true", "1", "yes")

        self.rank = (
            cli_rank
            if cli_rank is not None
            else _cfg_get(cfg, "adasvd_rank", _cfg_get(cfg, "rank", rank))
        )

            )
        )

                cfg,
                "adasvd_target_attention_only",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only),
            )
        )
        self.min_rank = int(_cfg_get(cfg, "adasvd_min_rank", _cfg_get(cfg, "min_rank", min_rank)))

        self.use_weighted_als = (
            cli_use_weighted_als
            if cli_use_weighted_als is not None
            else bool(
                _cfg_get(
                    cfg,
                    "adasvd_use_weighted_als",
                    _cfg_get(cfg, "use_weighted_als", use_weighted_als),
                )
            )
        )
        self.use_mean_shift = bool(
            _cfg_get(

                _cfg_get(cfg, "use_mean_shift", use_mean_shift),
            )
        )

            else bool(

                    "adasvd_use_gating_d",
                    _cfg_get(cfg, "use_gating_d", use_gating_d),
                )
            )
        )

        self.target_modules = target_modules
        self.max_calib_tokens = int(max_calib_tokens)
        self.fused_qkv_error_threshold = float(
            _cfg_get(cfg, "adasvd_fused_qkv_error_threshold", fused_qkv_error_threshold)
        )

        if gelu_layer_patterns is None:
            gelu_layer_patterns = (
                "lin1",
                "intermediate.dense",
                "gate_proj",
                "up_proj",
                "dense_h_to_4h",
                "c_fc",
                "wi_0",
                "wi_1",
            )
        self.gelu_layer_patterns = tuple(gelu_layer_patterns)

        # Tokenizer auto-resolve
        if tokenizer is None:
            tokenizer = getattr(model, "tokenizer", None) or getattr(
                getattr(model, "config", None), "tokenizer", None
            )
        if tokenizer is None:
            mp = getattr(getattr(model, "config", None), "_name_or_path", None)
            if mp:
                try:
                    from transformers import AutoTokenizer

                    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(mp)
                    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
                        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("Could not auto-load tokenizer from %s: %s", mp, e)
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer

        self.calibration_texts = (
            calibration_texts
            or _cfg_get(cfg, "calibration_texts", None)
            or DEFAULT_CALIBRATION_CORPUS
        )
        self.device = (
            torch.device(device) if device is not None else next(model.parameters()).device
        )
        self.comp_config = cfg

        # State

        self.layer_means: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        self.layer_out_means: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        self.layer_out_vars: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        self.mean_shifts: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}

        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.layer_ranks: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.layer_iters: dict[str, int] = {}

        self.calibration_time_s: float = 0.0

        self._orig_storage_bytes: int = 0
        self._comp_storage_bytes: int = 0
        self._compressed_params: int = 0
        self._compressed_names_seen: set = set()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Target selection
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        if isinstance(module, (SVDLinear, FusedQKVSVDLinear)):
            return False
        if any(
            name.endswith(sfx) for sfx in (".base_layer", ".lora_A", ".lora_B", ".lora_dropout")
        ):
            return False

        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
        target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()

        weight_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "weight", getattr(module, "weight", None))
        if weight_tensor is None or not hasattr(weight_tensor, "dim") or weight_tensor.dim() != 2:
            return False

        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(module, nn.Linear)
            or isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
            or (
                Conv1D is not None
                and (isinstance(module, Conv1D) or isinstance(target_mod, Conv1D))
            )
            or hasattr(module, "nf")
            or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            or "loralinear" in cls_name
            or hasattr(module, "base_layer")
        )
        if not is_linear_like:
            return False

        name_lower = name.lower()
        if any(
            skip in name_lower
            for skip in (
                "lm_head",
                "classifier",
                "head",
                "output_layer",
                "score",
                "pooler",
                "embedding",
                "layernorm",
                "rmsnorm",
            )
        ):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            ffn_patterns = (
                "ffn",
                "mlp",
                "lin1",
                "lin2",
                "intermediate",
                "gate_proj",
                "up_proj",
                "down_proj",
                "c_fc",
            )
            if any(p in name_lower for p in ffn_patterns):
                return False
            if "output.dense" in name_lower and "attention" not in name_lower:
                return False
            attn_patterns = (
                "attn",
                "attention",
                "q_proj",
                "k_proj",
                "v_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "out_lin",
                "q_lin",
                "k_lin",
                "v_lin",
                "c_attn",
                "c_proj",
                "query",
                "key",
                "value",
                "in_proj_qkv",
                "in_proj_z",
                "in_proj_b",
                "out_proj",
            )
            if not any(p in name_lower for p in attn_patterns):
                return False
        else:
            try:
                if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
                    return False
            except Exception:
                pass

        if self.target_modules is not None:
            return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)
        return True

    @staticmethod
    def _is_fused_qkv_layer(name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        if "c_attn" not in name.lower():
            return False
        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        nf = getattr(module, "nf", None) or getattr(target_mod, "nf", None)
        if nf is None or nf % 3 != 0:
            return False
        w = getattr(target_mod, "weight", None)
        return w is not None and w.dim() == 2

    @staticmethod
    def _gelu_derivative_squared(y: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        phi = 0.5 * (1.0 + torch.erf(y / math.sqrt(2.0)))
        pdf = torch.exp(-y.pow(2) / 2.0) / math.sqrt(2.0 * math.pi)
        gelu_prime = phi + y * pdf
        return gelu_prime.pow(2)

    def _is_followed_by_gelu(self, layer_name: str) -> bool:
        name_lower = layer_name.lower()
        return any(p in name_lower for p in self.gelu_layer_patterns)

    @staticmethod
    def _compute_effective_rank(W: torch.Tensor) -> float:
        with torch.no_grad():
            s = torch.linalg.svdvals(W.float())
            s_sq = s.pow(2)
            denom = s_sq.sum().clamp(min=1e-12)
            p = s_sq / denom
            p = p[p > 1e-12]
            return float(torch.exp(-(p * torch.log(p + 1e-12)).sum()).item())

    _compute_nuclear_norm = _compute_effective_rank

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Calibration
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _collect_activations(self, calibration_data: Any | None = None) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        activations: dict[str, list[torch.Tensor]] = {}
        out_activations: dict[str, list[torch.Tensor]] = {}
        hooks = []
        max_tokens = self.max_calib_tokens

        def make_hook(layer_name: str):
            def hook(module, inp, outp):
                if not inp or inp[0] is None:
                    return
                x = inp[0].detach()
                target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                is_conv1d = (
                    (
                        Conv1D is not None
                        and (isinstance(module, Conv1D) or isinstance(target_mod, Conv1D))
                    )
                    or hasattr(module, "nf")
                    or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
                )
                weight_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "weight", getattr(module, "weight", None))
                if weight_tensor is None or weight_tensor.dim() != 2:
                    return

                in_f = weight_tensor.shape[0] if is_conv1d else weight_tensor.shape[1]
                x_flat = x.float().reshape(-1, in_f)
                mask = x_flat.abs().sum(dim=-1) > 1e-5
                x_active = x_flat[mask] if mask.sum() > 0 else x_flat

                bucket = activations.setdefault(layer_name, [])
                curr_n = sum(t.size(0) for t in bucket)
                if curr_n < max_tokens:
                    avail = max_tokens - curr_n
                    bucket.append(x_active[:avail].cpu())

                if isinstance(outp, torch.Tensor):
                    out_f = weight_tensor.shape[1] if is_conv1d else weight_tensor.shape[0]
                    y_flat = outp.detach().float().reshape(-1, out_f)
                    y_bucket = out_activations.setdefault(layer_name, [])
                    curr_ny = sum(t.size(0) for t in y_bucket)
                    if curr_ny < max_tokens:
                        avail_y = max_tokens - curr_ny
                        y_bucket.append(y_flat[:avail_y].cpu())

            return hook

        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if self._is_target_module(name, module):
                hooks.append(module.register_forward_hook(make_hook(name)))

        if not hooks:
            return {}

        self.model.eval()
        dev = next(self.model.parameters()).device

        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                if calibration_data is not None:
                    self._run_calibration(calibration_data, dev)
                elif self.tokenizer is not None and self.calibration_texts:
                    inputs = self.tokenizer(
                        self.calibration_texts,
                        return_tensors="pt",
                        padding=True,
                        truncation=True,
                        max_length=512,
                    )
                    inputs = {k: v.to(dev) for k, v in inputs.items()}
                    self.model(**inputs)
                else:
                    gen = torch.Generator(device="cpu").manual_seed(42)
                    dummy_ids = torch.randint(100, 2000, (8, 64), generator=gen).to(dev)
                    try:
                        self.model(input_ids=dummy_ids)
                    except Exception:
                        try:
                            self.model(dummy_ids)
                        except Exception:
                            try:
                                dummy_x = torch.randn(128, 64, device=dev)
                                self.model(dummy_x)
                            except Exception as e:
                                logger.debug("AdaSVD synthetic calibration failed: %s", e)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("AdaSVD calibration pass exception: %s", e)
        finally:
            for h in hooks:
                h.remove()

        result: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        for name, tensor_list in activations.items():
            if tensor_list:
                cat_x = torch.cat(tensor_list, dim=0)
                result[name] = cat_x
                self.layer_means[name] = cat_x.mean(dim=0).cpu()

        for name, y_list in out_activations.items():
            if y_list:
                cat_y = torch.cat(y_list, dim=0)
                self.layer_out_means[name] = cat_y.mean(dim=0).cpu()

                if self.use_gating_d and self._is_followed_by_gelu(name):
                    gelu_sq = self._gelu_derivative_squared(cat_y)
                    d_values = gelu_sq.mean(dim=0)
                else:
                    d_values = cat_y.var(dim=0, unbiased=False)

                self.layer_out_vars[name] = d_values.cpu()

        return result

    def _run_calibration(self, calibration_data: Any, dev: torch.device) -> None:
        if isinstance(calibration_data, torch.Tensor):
            try:
                self.model(calibration_data.to(dev))
            except Exception:
                self.model(input_ids=calibration_data.to(dev))
        elif isinstance(calibration_data, (list, tuple)) and self.tokenizer is not None:
            inputs = self.tokenizer(
                list(calibration_data),
                return_tensors="pt",
                padding=True,
                truncation=True,
                max_length=512,
            )
            inputs = {k: v.to(dev) for k, v in inputs.items()}
            self.model(**inputs)
        elif hasattr(calibration_data, "__iter__"):
            for batch in calibration_data:
                try:
                    if isinstance(batch, torch.Tensor):
                        self.model(batch.to(dev))
                    elif isinstance(batch, dict):
                        b_dev = {
                            k: v.to(dev) for k, v in batch.items() if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor)
                        }
                        self.model(**b_dev)
                    else:
                        continue
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("AdaSVD calibration batch skipped: %s", e)
                    continue

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # SVD helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _init_whitened_svd(
        self,
        W: torch.Tensor,
        r: int,
        C_X: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        d_out, d_in = W.shape
        device = W.device
        dtype = W.dtype

        if C_X is not None and C_X.shape == (d_in, d_in):
            C_dev = C_X.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
            eps = max(1e-8, float(1e-6 * torch.trace(C_dev).item() / max(1, d_in)))
            eye = torch.eye(d_in, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
            S_white = None
            try:
                S_white = torch.linalg.cholesky(C_dev + eps * eye)
            except Exception:
                for exp in (-5, -4, -3, -2, -1):
                    try:
                        reg = 10**exp * (torch.trace(C_dev).item() / max(1, d_in))
                        S_white = torch.linalg.cholesky(C_dev + reg * eye)
                        break
                    except Exception:
                        continue

            if S_white is not None:
                try:
                    S_inv = torch.linalg.inv(S_white)
                    W_white = W.float() @ S_white
                    U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W_white, full_matrices=False)
                    Vt = Vt @ S_inv
                except Exception:
                    U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W.float(), full_matrices=False)
            else:
                U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W.float(), full_matrices=False)
        else:
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W.float(), full_matrices=False)

        sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
        A = U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
        B = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]
        return A.to(dtype=dtype, device=device), B.to(dtype=dtype, device=device)

    def _two_sided_als(
        self,
        W: torch.Tensor,
        r: int,
        C_X: torch.Tensor,
        D_var: torch.Tensor,
        X_calib: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        mu_x: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        num_iters: int = 10,
        patience: int = 3,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, int]:
        device = W.device
        dtype = W.dtype

        A, B = self._init_whitened_svd(W, r, C_X)
        A = A.float()
        B = B.float()

        E = C_X.float().to(device)
        D = torch.diag(torch.clamp(D_var.float().to(device), min=1e-6))
        WE = W.float() @ E
        DW = D @ W.float()

        def _err(recon):
            if X_calib is not None:
                X_eval = (X_calib - mu_x.to(device)) if mu_x is not None else X_calib
                WX_c = W.float() @ X_eval.float().t()
                reconX_c = recon @ X_eval.float().t()
                return float(
                    torch.norm(WX_c - reconX_c, p="fro").item()
                    / (torch.norm(WX_c, p="fro").item() + 1e-9)
                )
            diff = W.float() - recon
            num = math.sqrt(max(0.0, torch.trace(diff @ E @ diff.t()).item()))
            den = math.sqrt(max(1e-9, torch.trace(W.float() @ E @ W.float().t()).item()))
            return float(num / (den + 1e-9))

        best_A, best_B = A.clone(), B.clone()
        best_err = _err(A @ B)
        patience_counter = 0
        actual_iters = 0

        for _ in range(num_iters):
            BEBt = B @ E @ B.t()
            eps_B = max(1e-9, float(1e-6 * BEBt.trace().item() / max(1, r)))
            try:
                A_new = (
                    WE
                    @ B.t()
                    @ torch.linalg.inv(BEBt + eps_B * torch.eye(r, device=device, dtype=BEBt.dtype))
                )
            except Exception:
                break

            AtDA = A_new.t() @ D @ A_new
            eps_A = max(1e-9, float(1e-6 * AtDA.trace().item() / max(1, r)))
            try:
                B_new = (
                    torch.linalg.inv(AtDA + eps_A * torch.eye(r, device=device, dtype=AtDA.dtype))
                    @ A_new.t()
                    @ DW
                )
            except Exception:
                break

            if not torch.isfinite(A_new).all() or not torch.isfinite(B_new).all():
                break

            actual_iters += 1
            err = _err(A_new @ B_new)

            if err < best_err - 1e-6:
                best_A, best_B = A_new.clone(), B_new.clone()
                best_err = err
                patience_counter = 0
            else:
                patience_counter += 1
                if patience_counter >= patience:
                    break

            A, B = A_new, B_new

        return best_A.to(dtype), best_B.to(dtype), best_err, actual_iters

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Fused QKV compression
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _compress_fused_qkv(
        self,
        layer: nn.Module,
        rank: int,
        name: str,
    ) -> tuple[FusedQKVSVDLinear | None, float, int]:
        target_mod = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
        w = getattr(target_mod, "weight", None)
        if w is None or w.dim() != 2:
            return None, float("inf"), 0

        raw_w = w.data
        in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape
        if out_f % 3 != 0:
            logger.warning("FusedQKV %s: out_features %d not divisible by 3", name, out_f)
            return None, float("inf"), 0

        split_size = out_f // 3
        max_rank = min(in_f, split_size)
        r_floor = max(16, min(int(0.15 * split_size), self.min_rank * 4))
        r = max(r_floor, min(int(rank), max_rank))

        device = raw_w.device
        dtype = raw_w.dtype
        has_bias = getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None

        fused_layer = FusedQKVSVDLinear(
            in_features=in_f,
            out_features=out_f,
            rank=r,
            bias=has_bias,
            dtype=dtype,
            device=device,
            lora_r=0,
            lora_alpha=1.0,
        )

        try:
            fused_layer.set_fused_factors(raw_w)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("FusedQKV %s factor split failed: %s", name, e)
            return None, float("inf"), 0

        # ---- Mean-shift ----
        mean_shift = None
        mu_x = None
        if self.use_mean_shift:
            mu_x = self.layer_means.get(name, None)
            if mu_x is not None:
                mu_x = mu_x.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
                mean_shift = raw_w.float().t() @ mu_x
                self.mean_shifts[name] = mean_shift.cpu()
                fused_layer.set_input_mean(mu_x)

        if has_bias and fused_layer.bias is not None:
            with torch.no_grad():
                base_b = target_mod.bias.data.float().to(device)
                if mean_shift is not None:
                    base_b = base_b + mean_shift
                fused_layer.bias.data.copy_(base_b.to(dtype=dtype, device=device))

        # ---- Reconstruction error ----
        with torch.no_grad():
            W_recon = fused_layer.weight.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
            W_orig = raw_w.float()
            norm_orig = torch.norm(W_orig, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
            rel_err = (torch.norm(W_orig - W_recon, p="fro") / norm_orig).item()

        threshold = self.fused_qkv_error_threshold
        if r >= 0.5 * max_rank:
            threshold = max(threshold, 0.45)

        if rel_err > threshold:
            logger.warning(
                "AdaSVD fused-QKV %s: err=%.4f > threshold %.4f; falling back to original.",
                name,
                rel_err,
                threshold,
            )
            return None, rel_err, 0

        logger.info(
            "AdaSVD fused-QKV: %s in=%d out=%d split=%d rank=%d err=%.4f",
            name,
            in_f,
            out_f,
            split_size,
            r,
            rel_err,
        )
        return fused_layer, rel_err, 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Single-layer compression
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress_linear(
        self,
        layer: nn.Module,
        rank: int,
        name: str = "",
        covariance: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        X_calib: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        out_variance: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[nn.Module | None, float, int]:
        if self._is_fused_qkv_layer(name, layer):
            return self._compress_fused_qkv(layer, rank, name)

        target_mod = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
        is_conv1d = (
            (Conv1D is not None and (isinstance(layer, Conv1D) or isinstance(target_mod, Conv1D)))
            or hasattr(layer, "nf")
            or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        )

        weight_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "weight", getattr(layer, "weight", None))
        if weight_tensor is None or weight_tensor.dim() != 2:
            return None, float("inf"), 0

        raw_w = weight_tensor.data
        if is_conv1d:
            in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
        else:
            out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.float()

        max_param_r = max(1, min(in_f, out_f, int(0.75 * (in_f * out_f) / (in_f + out_f))))
        if self.rank is not None:
            r_floor = max(1, min(self.min_rank, rank))
        else:
            r_floor = max(self.min_rank, int(0.15 * min(in_f, out_f)))
        r = max(r_floor, min(rank, max_param_r))

        device = weight_tensor.device
        dtype = weight_tensor.dtype

        # ---- Mean-shift ----
        mu_x = self.layer_means.get(name, None) if self.use_mean_shift else None
        mean_shift = None
        if mu_x is not None:
            mu_x = mu_x.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
            mean_shift = (W.float() @ mu_x).to(dtype=torch.float32)
            self.mean_shifts[name] = mean_shift.cpu()

        X = X_calib if X_calib is not None else self.calibration_activations.get(name, None)
        X_centered = None
        C_X = None
        if X is not None:
            X = X.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
            X_centered = (X - mu_x) if mu_x is not None else X
            C_X = (X_centered.t() @ X_centered) / max(1, X_centered.shape[0])
        elif covariance is not None and covariance.shape == (in_f, in_f):
            C_X = covariance.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)

        D_var = out_variance if out_variance is not None else self.layer_out_vars.get(name, None)

        if C_X is not None and D_var is not None and self.use_weighted_als and self.num_iters > 0:
            A, B, final_err, actual_iters = self._two_sided_als(
                W,
                r,
                C_X,
                D_var,
                X_calib=X,
                mu_x=mu_x,
                num_iters=self.num_iters,
            )
        else:
            A, B = self._init_whitened_svd(W, r, C_X)
            actual_iters = 0
            recon = A @ B
            if X is not None:
                X_eval = X_centered if X_centered is not None else X
                WX = W.float() @ X_eval.t()
                reconX = recon.float() @ X_eval.t()
                final_err = float(
                    torch.norm(WX - reconX, p="fro").item()
                    / (torch.norm(WX, p="fro").item() + 1e-9)
                )
            else:
                W_norm = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()
                final_err = (
                    0.0
                    if W_norm < 1e-12
                    else float(torch.norm(W - recon, p="fro").item() / (W_norm + 1e-9))
                )

        bias_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "bias", getattr(layer, "bias", None))
        has_bias = (bias_tensor is not None) or (mean_shift is not None)

        svd_layer = SVDLinear(
            in_features=in_f,
            out_features=out_f,
            rank=r,
            bias=has_bias,
            dtype=dtype,
            device=device,
        )
        svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(
            U_factor=A.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
            V_factor=B.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
        )

        if has_bias and svd_layer.bias is not None:
            if bias_tensor is not None:
                b_base = bias_tensor.data.float().to(device)
            else:
                b_base = torch.zeros(out_f, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
            new_bias = b_base + mean_shift if mean_shift is not None else b_base
            svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(new_bias.to(dtype=dtype, device=device))

        # ---- Mean-shift registration on SVDLinear ----
        # SVDLinear exposes `_input_mean` (may be None). If it is a buffer,
        # we register and copy; otherwise we set attribute.
        if mu_x is not None:
            mu_f = mu_x.to(device=device, dtype=dtype)
            if hasattr(svd_layer, "register_buffer"):
                try:
                    # Only register if not already registered
                    if not any(n == "_input_mean" for n, _ in svd_layer.named_buffers()):
                        svd_layer.register_buffer("_input_mean", mu_f, persistent=True)
                    else:
                        svd_layer._input_mean.copy_(mu_f)
                except Exception:
                    svd_layer._input_mean = mu_f
        # else: keep SVDLinear's default (None)

        return svd_layer, final_err, actual_iters

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main compress
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress(self, calibration_data: Any | None = None) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.layer_errors = {}
        self.layer_ranks = {}
        self.layer_iters = {}
        self._orig_storage_bytes = 0
        self._comp_storage_bytes = 0
        self._compressed_names_seen = set()

        target_layers = [
            (name, mod)
            for name, mod in self.model.named_modules()
            if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
        ]

        if not target_layers:
            logger.warning("AdaSVD: no compressible layers found.")
            return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

        # Track original bytes
        for name, mod in target_layers:
            target_mod = getattr(mod, "base_layer", mod)
            try:
                for p in target_mod.parameters():
                    self._orig_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
            except Exception:
                pass

        # ---- Step 1: calibration ----
        t_calib_start = time.monotonic()
        self.calibration_activations = self._collect_activations(calibration_data=calibration_data)
        self.calibration_time_s = time.monotonic() - t_calib_start
        logger.info("AdaSVD calibration time: %.2f s", self.calibration_time_s)

        # ---- Step 2: spectral importance ----
        layer_importances: dict[str, float] = {}
        total_importance = 0.0
        for name, module in target_layers:
            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            weight_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "weight", getattr(module, "weight", None))
            if weight_tensor is None or weight_tensor.dim() != 2:
                continue
            is_conv1d = (
                (
                    Conv1D is not None
                    and (isinstance(module, Conv1D) or isinstance(target_mod, Conv1D))
                )
                or hasattr(module, "nf")
                or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            )
            raw_w = weight_tensor.data
            W_2d = raw_w.t().contiguous().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.float()
            imp = self._compute_effective_rank(W_2d)
            layer_importances[name] = max(imp, 1e-4)
            total_importance += layer_importances[name]

        # ---- Step 3: compress each layer ----
        for name, module in target_layers:
            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            weight_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "weight", getattr(module, "weight", None))
            if weight_tensor is None or weight_tensor.dim() != 2:
                continue
            is_conv1d = (
                (
                    Conv1D is not None
                    and (isinstance(module, Conv1D) or isinstance(target_mod, Conv1D))
                )
                or hasattr(module, "nf")
                or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            )
            raw_w = weight_tensor.data
            if is_conv1d:
                in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            else:
                out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]

            max_param_r = max(1, min(in_f, out_f, int(0.75 * (in_f * out_f) / (in_f + out_f))))
            if self.rank is not None:
                r_floor = max(1, min(self.min_rank, self.rank))
                denom = max(1, len(target_layers))
                frac = layer_importances.get(name, 1.0) / max(total_importance / denom, 1e-8)
                allocated_rank = int(round(self.rank * frac))
            else:
                r_floor = max(self.min_rank, int(0.15 * min(in_f, out_f)))
                frac = layer_importances.get(name, 1.0) / max(total_importance, 1e-8)
                allocated_rank = int(
                    round(min(in_f, out_f) * self.target_param_ratio * frac * len(target_layers))
                )

            if self._is_fused_qkv_layer(name, module):
                if self.rank is not None:
                    r_floor = max(r_floor, min(128, self.rank))
                    max_param_r = min(max_param_r, 432)
                else:
                    r_floor = max(r_floor, 128)
                    max_param_r = min(max_param_r, 432)
            else:
                # FIXED: Adaptive Rank Strategy: Force rank=64 (or 128 for fused above)
                # for attention projections to prevent 98% spectral loss (Initial PPL: 1233)
                if self.rank is not None:
                    r_floor = max(r_floor, min(64, self.rank))
                else:
                    r_floor = max(r_floor, 64)

            allocated_rank = max(r_floor, min(allocated_rank, max_param_r))

            svd_layer, err, iters = self.compress_linear(
                module,
                rank=allocated_rank,
                name=name,
            )
            self.layer_errors[name] = err
            self.layer_ranks[name] = allocated_rank
            self.layer_iters[name] = iters

            if svd_layer is None or err > self.max_error:
                logger.warning(
                    "AdaSVD: %s reconstruction error %.4f > max_error %.4f; "
                    "falling back to original.",
                    name,
                    err,
                    self.max_error,
                )
                if self.fallback_to_original:
                    self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                    # Fallback keeps original bytes, so add them to comp
                    try:
                        for p in target_mod.parameters():
                            self._comp_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    continue

            # ---- Splice ----
            if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                module.base_layer = svd_layer
                for attr in ("lora_A", "lora_B"):
                    sub = getattr(module, attr, None)
                    if sub is None:
                        continue
                    params = sub.parameters() if hasattr(sub, "parameters") else iter([sub])
                    for p in params:
                        if hasattr(p, "requires_grad"):
                            p.requires_grad = True
            else:
                replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)
            self.compressed_layers.append(name)
            self._compressed_names_seen.add(name)

            # ---- Storage accounting ----
            try:
                for p in svd_layer.parameters():
                    self._comp_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
                for b in svd_layer.buffers():
                    if b is not None:
                        self._comp_storage_bytes += b.numel() * b.element_size()
            except Exception:
                pass

        # ---- Freeze fallback layers ----
        for name, mod in self.model.named_modules():
            if name in self.fallback_layers:
                target = getattr(mod, "base_layer", mod)
                for p in target.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False

        # ---- Recompute honest storage ----
        comp_bytes_real = 0
        for name, mod in self.model.named_modules():
            if name in self.compressed_layers or name in self.fallback_layers:
                target = getattr(mod, "base_layer", mod)
                try:
                    for p in target.parameters():
                        comp_bytes_real += p.numel() * p.element_size()
                    for b in target.buffers():
                        if b is not None:
                            comp_bytes_real += b.numel() * b.element_size()
                except Exception:
                    pass
        self._comp_storage_bytes = comp_bytes_real

        try:
            self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        except Exception:
            self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        avg_err = (
            sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
            / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
            if self.compressed_layers
            else 0.0
        )
        storage_ratio = (
            self._orig_storage_bytes / self._comp_storage_bytes
            if self._comp_storage_bytes > 0
            else 1.0
        )
        effective_ratio = (
            self._comp_storage_bytes / self._orig_storage_bytes
            if self._orig_storage_bytes > 0
            else 1.0
        )

        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        stats.update(
            {
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
                "layer_iters": self.layer_iters,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "calibration_time_s": self.calibration_time_s,
                "use_weighted_als": self.use_weighted_als,
                "use_mean_shift": self.use_mean_shift,
                "use_gating_d": self.use_gating_d,
                "orig_storage_bytes": self._orig_storage_bytes,
                "comp_storage_bytes": self._comp_storage_bytes,
                "storage_compression_ratio": round(storage_ratio, 3),
                "storage_reduction_pct": round(100.0 * (1.0 - effective_ratio), 2),
            }
        )
        try:
            self.model.compression_stats = stats
        except Exception:
            pass

        logger.info(
            "AdaSVD done: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, storage_ratio=%.2fx",
            len(self.compressed_layers),
            len(self.fallback_layers),
            avg_err,
            storage_ratio,
        )
        return self.model, stats

Base class for all compressors. Not a nn.Module to avoid parameter storage.

Expand source code 
def compress_linear(
    self,
    layer: nn.Module,
    rank: int,
    name: str = "",
    covariance: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    X_calib: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    out_variance: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    **kwargs: Any,
) -> tuple[nn.Module | None, float, int]:
    if self._is_fused_qkv_layer(name, layer):
        return self._compress_fused_qkv(layer, rank, name)

    target_mod = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
    is_conv1d = (
        (Conv1D is not None and (isinstance(layer, Conv1D) or isinstance(target_mod, Conv1D)))
        or hasattr(layer, "nf")
        or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
    )

    weight_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "weight", getattr(layer, "weight", None))
    if weight_tensor is None or weight_tensor.dim() != 2:
        return None, float("inf"), 0

    raw_w = weight_tensor.data
    if is_conv1d:
        in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
        W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
    else:
        out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
        W = raw_w.float()

    max_param_r = max(1, min(in_f, out_f, int(0.75 * (in_f * out_f) / (in_f + out_f))))
    if self.rank is not None:
        r_floor = max(1, min(self.min_rank, rank))
    else:
        r_floor = max(self.min_rank, int(0.15 * min(in_f, out_f)))
    r = max(r_floor, min(rank, max_param_r))

    device = weight_tensor.device
    dtype = weight_tensor.dtype

    # ---- Mean-shift ----
    mu_x = self.layer_means.get(name, None) if self.use_mean_shift else None
    mean_shift = None
    if mu_x is not None:
        mu_x = mu_x.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        mean_shift = (W.float() @ mu_x).to(dtype=torch.float32)
        self.mean_shifts[name] = mean_shift.cpu()

    X = X_calib if X_calib is not None else self.calibration_activations.get(name, None)
    X_centered = None
    C_X = None
    if X is not None:
        X = X.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        X_centered = (X - mu_x) if mu_x is not None else X
        C_X = (X_centered.t() @ X_centered) / max(1, X_centered.shape[0])
    elif covariance is not None and covariance.shape == (in_f, in_f):
        C_X = covariance.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)

    D_var = out_variance if out_variance is not None else self.layer_out_vars.get(name, None)

    if C_X is not None and D_var is not None and self.use_weighted_als and self.num_iters > 0:
        A, B, final_err, actual_iters = self._two_sided_als(
            W,
            r,
            C_X,
            D_var,
            X_calib=X,
            mu_x=mu_x,
            num_iters=self.num_iters,
        )
    else:
        A, B = self._init_whitened_svd(W, r, C_X)
        actual_iters = 0
        recon = A @ B
        if X is not None:
            X_eval = X_centered if X_centered is not None else X
            WX = W.float() @ X_eval.t()
            reconX = recon.float() @ X_eval.t()
            final_err = float(
                torch.norm(WX - reconX, p="fro").item()
                / (torch.norm(WX, p="fro").item() + 1e-9)
            )
        else:
            W_norm = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()
            final_err = (
                0.0
                if W_norm < 1e-12
                else float(torch.norm(W - recon, p="fro").item() / (W_norm + 1e-9))
            )

    bias_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "bias", getattr(layer, "bias", None))
    has_bias = (bias_tensor is not None) or (mean_shift is not None)

    svd_layer = SVDLinear(
        in_features=in_f,
        out_features=out_f,
        rank=r,
        bias=has_bias,
        dtype=dtype,
        device=device,
    )
    svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(
        U_factor=A.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
        V_factor=B.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
    )

    if has_bias and svd_layer.bias is not None:
        if bias_tensor is not None:
            b_base = bias_tensor.data.float().to(device)
        else:
            b_base = torch.zeros(out_f, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        new_bias = b_base + mean_shift if mean_shift is not None else b_base
        svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(new_bias.to(dtype=dtype, device=device))

    # ---- Mean-shift registration on SVDLinear ----
    # SVDLinear exposes `_input_mean` (may be None). If it is a buffer,
    # we register and copy; otherwise we set attribute.
    if mu_x is not None:
        mu_f = mu_x.to(device=device, dtype=dtype)
        if hasattr(svd_layer, "register_buffer"):
            try:
                # Only register if not already registered
                if not any(n == "_input_mean" for n, _ in svd_layer.named_buffers()):
                    svd_layer.register_buffer("_input_mean", mu_f, persistent=True)
                else:
                    svd_layer._input_mean.copy_(mu_f)
            except Exception:
                svd_layer._input_mean = mu_f
    # else: keep SVDLinear's default (None)

    return svd_layer, final_err, actual_iters
