@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "compressed_attention_layer",
    supports_causal=True,
    supports_bidirectional=True,
    supports_gqa=True,
)
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "compressed_attention",
    supports_causal=True,
    supports_bidirectional=True,
    supports_gqa=True,
)
class CompressedAttentionLayer(BaseAttention):
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_bidirectional: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    supports_rope: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        rank_qkv: int = 256,
        rank_out: int = 256,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        original_layer: Any = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        cfg = config if config is not None else original_layer
        super().__init__(cfg, rank)
        self.adapter = ModelAdapter(cfg)
        self.hidden_size: int = self.adapter.hidden_size or kwargs.get(
            "hidden_size", kwargs.get("d_model", 768)
        )
        self.num_heads: int = self.adapter.num_heads or kwargs.get(
            "num_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)
        )
        self.num_kv_heads: int = self.adapter.num_key_value_heads or kwargs.get(
            "num_kv_heads", kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads)
        )
        self.head_dim: int = self.adapter.head_dim or (self.hidden_size // max(1, self.num_heads))
        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat: int = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        _mt = getattr(cfg, "model_type", "").lower() if cfg else ""
        _decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        self.is_decoder: bool = bool(
            getattr(cfg, "is_decoder", False) or any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes)
        )
        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = False

        rank_val = rank or kwargs.get("rank_qkv", rank_qkv)
        self.rank_qkv = min(rank_val, self.all_head_size + 2 * self.kv_head_size, self.hidden_size)
        self.rank_out = min(
            rank or kwargs.get("rank_out", rank_out),
            self.all_head_size,
            self.hidden_size,
        )
        self.lora_rank = getattr(cfg, "lora_rank", lora_rank)
        self.lora_scaling = (32.0 / self.lora_rank) if self.lora_rank > 0 else 1.0

        qkv_out_dim = self.all_head_size + 2 * self.kv_head_size

        # Frozen base SVD parameters
        self.Vh_qkv = nn.Parameter(
            torch.randn(self.rank_qkv, self.hidden_size) * 0.02, requires_grad=False
        )
        self.S_qkv_sqrt = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.rank_qkv), requires_grad=False)
        self.U_qkv = nn.Parameter(
            torch.randn(qkv_out_dim, self.rank_qkv) * 0.02, requires_grad=False
        )
        self.bias_qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(qkv_out_dim), requires_grad=False)

        self.Vh_out = nn.Parameter(
            torch.randn(self.rank_out, self.all_head_size) * 0.02, requires_grad=False
        )
        self.S_out_sqrt = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.rank_out), requires_grad=False)
        self.U_out = nn.Parameter(
            torch.randn(self.hidden_size, self.rank_out) * 0.02, requires_grad=False
        )
        self.bias_out = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.hidden_size), requires_grad=False)

        # Trainable LoRA residual adapters
        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A_qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.hidden_size))
            self.lora_B_qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(qkv_out_dim, self.lora_rank))
            self.lora_A_out = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.all_head_size))
            self.lora_B_out = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.hidden_size, self.lora_rank))
            self.reset_lora_parameters()
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A_qkv", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B_qkv", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_A_out", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B_out", None)

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(cfg, "dropout", getattr(cfg, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
            if cfg
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)
        self.rotary_emb = getattr(pretrained_attn, "rotary_emb", None)

        src = pretrained_attn or (
            original_layer
            if hasattr(original_layer, "q_lin")
            or hasattr(original_layer, "query")
            or hasattr(original_layer, "c_attn")
            else None
        )
        if src is not None:
            self.copy_weights(src)

    @property
    def qkv_weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Reconstruct the effective base QKV weight from SVD factors."""
        return (self.U_qkv * self.S_qkv_sqrt.view(1, -1)) @ (
            self.Vh_qkv * self.S_qkv_sqrt.view(-1, 1)
        )

    @property
    def out_weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Reconstruct the effective base Out weight from SVD factors."""
        return (self.U_out * self.S_out_sqrt.view(1, -1)) @ (
            self.Vh_out * self.S_out_sqrt.view(-1, 1)
        )

    def reset_lora_parameters(self) -> None:
        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A_qkv, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B_qkv)
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A_out, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B_out)
            self.lora_A_qkv.requires_grad = True
            self.lora_B_qkv.requires_grad = True
            self.lora_A_out.requires_grad = True
            self.lora_B_out.requires_grad = True

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. HuggingFace / standard UniversalAdapter extraction
        for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
            try:
                w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = (w, b)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug(
                    f"UniversalAdapter failed for {proj}: {e}"
                )  # continue to fallback paths

        # 2. GPT-2 Fused c_attn Conv1D handling
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            weight = src.c_attn.weight.data
            bias = getattr(src.c_attn, "bias", None)
            bias_data = bias.data if bias is not None else None
            if weight.dim() == 2 and weight.shape[1] == 3 * self.hidden_size:
                weight = weight.t().contiguous()
            split_size = weight.shape[0] // 3
            q_w = weight[:split_size, :]
            k_w = weight[split_size : 2 * split_size, :]
            v_w = weight[2 * split_size :, :]
            q_b = bias_data[:split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
            k_b = bias_data[split_size : 2 * split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
            v_b = bias_data[2 * split_size :] if bias_data is not None else None
            extracted["q"] = (q_w, q_b)
            extracted["k"] = (k_w, k_b)
            extracted["v"] = (v_w, v_b)

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            w_o = src.c_proj.weight.data
            if (
                w_o.dim() == 2
                and w_o.shape[0] != self.hidden_size
                and w_o.shape[1] == self.hidden_size
            ):
                w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
            b_o = getattr(src.c_proj, "bias", None)
            extracted["o"] = (w_o, b_o.data if b_o is not None else None)

        # 3. RoBERTa / BERT Nested Encoder handling
        if hasattr(src, "self") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            s = src.self
            if hasattr(s, "query"):
                extracted["q"] = (s.query.weight.data, getattr(s.query, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "key"):
                extracted["k"] = (s.key.weight.data, getattr(s.key, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "value"):
                extracted["v"] = (s.value.weight.data, getattr(s.value, "bias", None))

        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            dense = src.output.dense
            extracted["o"] = (dense.weight.data, getattr(dense, "bias", None))

        # 4. Modern Decoders (Qwen3.5, LLaMA, Mistral) & DistilBERT
        named_map = {
            "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query"],
            "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key"],
            "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value"],
            "o": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"],
        }
        for proj, names in named_map.items():
            if extracted[proj][0] is None:
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        layer = getattr(src, name)
                        if hasattr(layer, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj] = (
                                layer.weight.data,
                                getattr(layer, "bias", None),
                            )
                            break

        # Detect nested encoder architecture
        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_proj = any(
            hasattr(src, attr)
            for attr in (
                "dense",
                "out_proj",
                "c_proj",
                "out_lin",
                "o_proj",
                "wo",
                "o",
                "proj",
            )
        )
        # Check output projection attrs FIRST — DistilBERT has out_lin and is NOT nested
        if hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
            self.is_nested_encoder = False
        elif (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention")
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and not has_out_proj)
            )
            and not has_out_proj
        ):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True
        else:
            self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # Check required weights
        missing = [k for k in ["q", "k", "v"] if extracted[k][0] is None]
        if not self.is_nested_encoder and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            missing.append("o")
        if missing:
            logger.warning(
                f"CompressedAttentionLayer: missing weights for projections {missing}; proceeding with random init"
            )

        # Frobenius norm validation
        for proj_key in ["q", "k", "v"]:
            w = extracted[proj_key][0]
            if w is not None:
                norm = torch.linalg.norm(w.float())
                if norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        f"CompressedAttentionLayer: extracted weight for {proj_key} has near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm.item():.6f}); continuing"
                    )

        if extracted["o"][0] is not None:
            norm_o = torch.linalg.norm(extracted["o"][0].float())
            if norm_o < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    f"CompressedAttentionLayer: extracted weight for output projection has near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm_o.item():.6f}); continuing"
                )

        # Joint SVD on Q, K, V
        def _orient(w: torch.Tensor, in_dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t().contiguous()
            return w

        with torch.no_grad():
            w_q = _orient(extracted["q"][0].float(), self.hidden_size)
            w_k = _orient(extracted["k"][0].float(), self.hidden_size)
            w_v = _orient(extracted["v"][0].float(), self.hidden_size)
            w_qkv = torch.cat([w_q, w_k, w_v], dim=0)

            U, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(w_qkv, full_matrices=False)
            r_qkv = min(self.rank_qkv, U.size(1), Vh.size(0))
            sqrt_s = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r_qkv], min=1e-12))

            qkv_out_dim = self.all_head_size + 2 * self.kv_head_size
            self.U_qkv.data[: min(qkv_out_dim, U.size(0)), :r_qkv].copy_(
                U[: min(qkv_out_dim, U.size(0)), :r_qkv]
            )
            self.S_qkv_sqrt.data[:r_qkv].copy_(sqrt_s)
            self.Vh_qkv.data[:r_qkv, : min(self.hidden_size, Vh.size(1))].copy_(
                Vh[:r_qkv, : min(self.hidden_size, Vh.size(1))]
            )

            b_q = (
                extracted["q"][1]
                if extracted["q"][1] is not None
                else torch.zeros(w_q.size(0), device=w_q.device)
            )
            b_k = (
                extracted["k"][1]
                if extracted["k"][1] is not None
                else torch.zeros(w_k.size(0), device=w_k.device)
            )
            b_v = (
                extracted["v"][1]
                if extracted["v"][1] is not None
                else torch.zeros(w_v.size(0), device=w_v.device)
            )
            b_q_val = b_q.data if isinstance(b_q, nn.Parameter) else b_q
            b_k_val = b_k.data if isinstance(b_k, nn.Parameter) else b_k
            b_v_val = b_v.data if isinstance(b_v, nn.Parameter) else b_v
            b_qkv = torch.cat([b_q_val, b_k_val, b_v_val], dim=0)
            self.bias_qkv.data[: min(qkv_out_dim, b_qkv.size(0))].copy_(
                b_qkv[: min(qkv_out_dim, b_qkv.size(0))]
            )

            # Output projection SVD
            if extracted["o"][0] is not None:
                w_o = _orient(extracted["o"][0].float(), self.all_head_size)
                U_o, S_o, Vh_o = torch.linalg.svd(w_o, full_matrices=False)
                r_out = min(self.rank_out, U_o.size(1), Vh_o.size(0))
                sqrt_so = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S_o[:r_out], min=1e-12))

                self.U_out.data[: min(self.hidden_size, U_o.size(0)), :r_out].copy_(
                    U_o[: min(self.hidden_size, U_o.size(0)), :r_out]
                )
                self.S_out_sqrt.data[:r_out].copy_(sqrt_so)
                self.Vh_out.data[:r_out, : min(self.all_head_size, Vh_o.size(1))].copy_(
                    Vh_o[:r_out, : min(self.all_head_size, Vh_o.size(1))]
                )

                if extracted["o"][1] is not None:
                    b_o = extracted["o"][1]
                    b_o_data = b_o.data if isinstance(b_o, nn.Parameter) else b_o
                    self.bias_out.data[: min(self.hidden_size, b_o_data.size(0))].copy_(
                        b_o_data[: min(self.hidden_size, b_o_data.size(0))]
                    )
            elif self.is_nested_encoder:
                self.U_out.data.zero_()
                min_d = min(self.hidden_size, self.rank_out)
                self.U_out.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                self.S_out_sqrt.data.fill_(1.0)
                self.Vh_out.data.zero_()
                self.Vh_out.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                self.bias_out.data.zero_()

            # Ensure base weights are frozen
            self.Vh_qkv.requires_grad = False
            self.S_qkv_sqrt.requires_grad = False
            self.U_qkv.requires_grad = False
            self.bias_qkv.requires_grad = False
            self.Vh_out.requires_grad = False
            self.S_out_sqrt.requires_grad = False
            self.U_out.requires_grad = False
            self.bias_out.requires_grad = False

            if self.lora_rank > 0:
                self.reset_lora_parameters()

        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        if hidden_states.dim() == 2:
            hidden_states = hidden_states.unsqueeze(0)
        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        # FIX 1: Pre-transpose weight matrices to avoid runtime transposes
        # Vh_qkv is (Rank, D), U_qkv is (Out_Dim, Rank)
        # S_qkv_sqrt is (Rank)

        # Optimize Matmul path
        v_proj = torch.matmul(hidden_states, self.Vh_qkv.t()) * self.S_qkv_sqrt
        qkv = torch.matmul(v_proj, (self.U_qkv * self.S_qkv_sqrt).t()) + self.bias_qkv

        # LoRA residual adapter
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A_qkv is not None and self.lora_B_qkv is not None:
            lora_qkv = (
                F.linear(F.linear(hidden_states, self.lora_A_qkv), self.lora_B_qkv)
                * self.lora_scaling
            )
            qkv = qkv + lora_qkv

        # Split into Q, K, V
        q, k, v = torch.split(
            qkv, [self.all_head_size, self.kv_head_size, self.kv_head_size], dim=-1
        )

        # FIX 2: Remove .contiguous() memory bombs
        q = q.view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        k = k.view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        v = v.view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)

        # Apply RoPE
        position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if (
            position_embeddings is not None
            and isinstance(position_embeddings, (tuple, list))
            and len(position_embeddings) >= 2
        ):
            cos, sin = position_embeddings[0], position_embeddings[1]
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin, kwargs.get("position_ids"))

        # GQA repeat
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            k = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)

        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)

        # Use torch.baddbmm or scaled_dot_product_attention for huge memory savings
        # Fallback to standard matmul if not available or unsupported shapes
        try:
            # PyTorch 2.0+ Flash Attention / Memory Efficient Attention
            # This handles Causal masking automatically and saves massive VRAM
            is_causal = self.is_decoder and (attention_mask is None or attention_mask.dim() != 4)
            attn_out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                q,
                k,
                v,
                attn_mask=attention_mask,
                dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                is_causal=is_causal,
            )
            probs = None  # SDPA doesn't return probabilities to save memory
        except Exception:
            # Legacy Path (if SDPA fails due to old PyTorch or weird masks)
            scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale

            mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("mask")
            if mask is not None:
                if mask.dim() == 4:
                    if mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                        scores = scores.masked_fill(~mask, -10000.0)
                    else:
                        scores = scores + mask.to(scores.dtype)
                else:
                    valid_m = get_token_valid_mask(mask, B, N, device)
                    if valid_m is not None:
                        scores = scores.masked_fill((~valid_m).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -10000.0)

            # Explicit Causal Masking
            if self.is_decoder and (mask is None or mask.dim() != 4):
                causal_tril = (
                    torch.tril(torch.ones((N, N), device=device, dtype=torch.bool))
                    .unsqueeze(0)
                    .unsqueeze(0)
                )
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal_tril, -10000.0)

            # FIX 3: Safe Softmax without extra type casts that create new tensors
            probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(dtype)
            probs = self.dropout(probs)
            attn_out = torch.matmul(probs, v)

        # Transpose back and view (reshape handles non-contiguous memory safely without deep copies)
        attn_out = attn_out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, N, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = attn_out
        else:
            # SVD Out forward
            out_v = torch.matmul(attn_out, self.Vh_out.t()) * self.S_out_sqrt
            out = torch.matmul(out_v, (self.U_out * self.S_out_sqrt).t()) + self.bias_out
            if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A_out is not None and self.lora_B_out is not None:
                lora_out = (
                    F.linear(F.linear(attn_out, self.lora_A_out), self.lora_B_out)
                    * self.lora_scaling
                )
                out = out + lora_out

        return out, (probs if output_attentions and probs is not None else None)

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

class Conv1D (nf, nx)
Expand source code 
class Conv1D(nn.Module):
    """
    1D-convolutional layer as defined by Radford et al. for OpenAI GPT (and also used in GPT-2).

    Basically works like a linear layer but the weights are transposed.

    Args:
        nf (`int`): The number of output features.
        nx (`int`): The number of input features.
    """

    def __init__(self, nf, nx):
        super().__init__()
        self.nf = nf
        self.nx = nx
        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(nx, nf))
        self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(nf))
        nn.init.normal_(self.weight, std=0.02)

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return "Conv1D(nf={nf}, nx={nx})".format(**self.__dict__)

    def forward(self, x):
        size_out = x.size()[:-1] + (self.nf,)
        x = torch.addmm(self.bias, x.view(-1, x.size(-1)), self.weight)
        x = x.view(size_out)
        return x

1D-convolutional layer as defined by Radford et al. for OpenAI GPT (and also used in GPT-2).

Basically works like a linear layer but the weights are transposed.

Args -----= nf (int): The number of output features. nx (int): The number of input features. Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

def forward(self, x) ‑> Callable[..., typing.Any]
Expand source code 
def forward(self, x):
    size_out = x.size()[:-1] + (self.nf,)
    x = torch.addmm(self.bias, x.view(-1, x.size(-1)), self.weight)
    x = x.view(size_out)
    return x

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("direct_svd")
class DirectSVDCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    """
    Direct SVD Compressor.

    Performs direct truncated SVD on target linear and Conv1D layers:
        W ≈ U_r * Sigma_r * V_r^T
    Allocates singular energy symmetrically:
        U_factor = U_r * sqrt(Sigma_r)
        V_factor = sqrt(Sigma_r) * V_r^T
    and replaces dense layers with factorized SVDLinear modules.

    Production hardening features:
    - Relative Frobenius reconstruction error validation with configurable max_error.
    - Automatic rank escalation retries when error threshold is exceeded.
    - Safe fallback to original layer if error remains high or decomposition fails.
    - Fallback and non-target layers frozen (requires_grad=False) during recovery.
    - Robust LoRALinear and Conv1D support (with transpose and adapter preservation).
    - target_attention_only parameter: restricts SVD factorization to attention projections.
    - Strict rank safeguard: enforces r < min(in_f, out_f) and parameter reduction r * (in + out) < in * out.
    - Unified critical-layer protection via should_compress_linear.
    - Full FP32 computation for numerical stability.
    - Real metrics recording: original_layer_count, compressed_layer_count, fallback_layer_count, effective_parameter_ratio.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        rank: int | None = 32,
        min_rank: int = 16,
        rank_ratio: float | None = None,
        max_error: float = 0.35,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        protect_critical: bool = True,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        skip_attention_qkv: bool = True,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        max_retries: int = 2,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        cfg: Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="direct_svd", **kwargs)
        cfg_obj = cfg if cfg is not None else kwargs.get("comp_config")
        self.cfg = cfg_obj

        rank = _cfg_get(
            cfg_obj,
            "direct_svd_rank",
            _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "svd_rank", _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "rank", rank)),
        )
        min_rank = _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "min_rank", min_rank)
        rank_ratio = _cfg_get(
            cfg_obj,
            "direct_svd_rank_ratio",
            _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "rank_ratio", rank_ratio),
        )
        max_error = _cfg_get(
            cfg_obj, "direct_svd_max_error", _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "max_error", max_error)
        )
        fallback_to_original = _cfg_get(
            cfg_obj,
            "direct_svd_fallback_to_original",
            _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original),
        )
        protect_critical = _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "protect_critical", protect_critical)
        lora_r = _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "lora_r", _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "lora_rank", lora_r))
        lora_alpha = _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha)
        skip_attention_qkv = _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "skip_attention_qkv", False)
        target_attention_only = _cfg_get(
            cfg_obj,
            "direct_svd_target_attention_only",
            _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only),
        )

        if "rank" in kwargs and kwargs["rank"] is not None:
            rank = kwargs["rank"]

        self.rank = rank
        self.min_rank = max(1, int(min_rank))
        self.rank_ratio = float(rank_ratio) if rank_ratio is not None else None
        if max_error is None:
            max_error = 0.05

        self.max_error = float(max_error)
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(fallback_to_original)
        self.protect_critical = bool(protect_critical)
        self.lora_r = int(lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.skip_attention_qkv = bool(skip_attention_qkv)
        self.target_attention_only = bool(target_attention_only)
        self.max_retries = int(max_retries)
        self.target_modules = target_modules

        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.skipped_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_ranks: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}

    def _is_gqa_asymmetric(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        if not self.skip_attention_qkv:
            return False
        cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
        num_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_attention_heads", None)
        num_kv_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_key_value_heads", None)
        if num_heads is not None and num_kv_heads is not None and num_kv_heads < num_heads:
            name_lower = name.lower()
            if any(
                p in name_lower
                for p in [
                    "q_proj",
                    "k_proj",
                    "v_proj",
                    "query",
                    "key",
                    "value",
                    "c_attn",
                ]
            ):
                return True
        return False

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        name_lower = name.lower()
        if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
            return False
        if self.protect_critical and any(
            k in name_lower
            for k in [
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embed",
                "logits_processor",
            ]
        ):
            return False

        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        if not (is_linear or is_conv1d):
            return False
        if isinstance(target_mod, SVDLinear) or isinstance(module, SVDLinear):
            return False
        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False
        if self._is_gqa_asymmetric(name, target_mod):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            last_part = name.split(".")[-1]
            is_attn = (last_part in _ATTN_NAMES) or any(
                k in name_lower for k in ("attn", "attention")
            )
            if not is_attn:
                return False

        if self.target_modules is not None:
            return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)
        return True

    def _determine_rank(self, in_f: int, out_f: int, target_r: int | None) -> int:
        """Compute target rank guaranteeing parameter reduction and strict bounds."""
        max_reduction_r = max(1, (in_f * out_f) // (in_f + out_f) - 1)
        max_r = max(1, min(in_f - 1, out_f - 1, max_reduction_r))

        ref_dim = 768.0
        if (
            hasattr(self, "model")
            and hasattr(self.model, "config")
            and self.model.config is not None
        ):
            cfg_obj = self.model.config
            ref_dim = float(
                getattr(
                    cfg_obj,
                    "hidden_size",
                    getattr(cfg_obj, "d_model", getattr(cfg_obj, "n_embd", 768.0)),
                )
            )
        ref_dim = max(1.0, ref_dim)

        if self.rank_ratio is not None:
            r = int(round(min(in_f, out_f) * max(0.01, min(0.99, self.rank_ratio))))
        elif target_r is not None:
            r = int(round(target_r * (min(in_f, out_f) / ref_dim)))
        else:
            r = int(round(32 * (min(in_f, out_f) / ref_dim)))

        # Dynamic architecture-aware rank selection for large models
        r = get_dynamic_compression_rank(
            in_f,
            out_f,
            model_or_family=self.model,
            default_rank=r,
            comp_config=getattr(self, "cfg", None),
        )
        r = max(self.min_rank, r)
        return max(1, min(r, max_r))

    def compress_linear(
        self,
        layer: nn.Module,
        rank: int | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[SVDLinear | None, float, int]:
        """
        Decompose a single linear or Conv1D layer via SVD with symmetric factor distribution.
        Returns: (svd_layer, relative_reconstruction_error, chosen_rank)
        """
        target_mod = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        raw_w = target_mod.weight.data

        if is_conv1d:
            in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()  # [out_features, in_features]
        else:
            out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.float()  # [out_features, in_features]

        max_reduction_r = max(1, (in_f * out_f) // (in_f + out_f) - 1)
        max_r = max(1, min(in_f - 1, out_f - 1, max_reduction_r))

        current_r = self._determine_rank(in_f, out_f, rank)
        device = raw_w.device
        dtype = raw_w.dtype
        norm_w = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()

        try:
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("DirectSVD SVD computation failed: %s", e)
            return None, 1.0, 0

        best_svd_layer: SVDLinear | None = None
        best_err = 1.0
        chosen_r = current_r

        # Retry with higher rank if error exceeds threshold
        for attempt in range(self.max_retries + 1):
            r = max(1, min(current_r, max_r))
            sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
            U_r = (U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)).to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
            V_r = (sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]).to(dtype=dtype, device=device)

            W_recon = torch.matmul(U_r.float(), V_r.float())
            if norm_w < 1e-12:
                err = 0.0
            else:
                err = float(torch.norm(W - W_recon, p="fro").item() / norm_w)

            best_err = err
            chosen_r = r

            # Determine LoRA parameters to preserve or initialize
            eff_lora_r = self.lora_r
            eff_lora_alpha = self.lora_alpha
            if hasattr(layer, "lora_A") and hasattr(layer, "r"):
                eff_lora_r = getattr(layer, "r", self.lora_r)
                eff_lora_alpha = getattr(layer, "lora_alpha", self.lora_alpha)

            svd_layer = SVDLinear(
                in_features=in_f,
                out_features=out_f,
                rank=r,
                bias=target_mod.bias is not None,
                dtype=dtype,
                device=device,
                lora_r=eff_lora_r,
                lora_alpha=eff_lora_alpha,
            )
            svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(U_r, V_r)
            if target_mod.bias is not None and svd_layer.bias is not None:
                svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(target_mod.bias.data)

            # If the original layer was a LoRALinear wrapper, copy over existing adapter weights
            if (
                hasattr(layer, "lora_A")
                and hasattr(layer, "lora_B")
                and svd_layer.lora_A is not None
            ):
                try:
                    if (
                        hasattr(layer.lora_A, "weight")
                        and svd_layer.lora_A.weight.shape == layer.lora_A.weight.shape
                    ):
                        svd_layer.lora_A.weight.data.copy_(layer.lora_A.weight.data)
                    if (
                        hasattr(layer.lora_B, "weight")
                        and svd_layer.lora_B.weight.shape == layer.lora_B.weight.shape
                    ):
                        svd_layer.lora_B.weight.data.copy_(layer.lora_B.weight.data)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("DirectSVD adapter preservation notice: %s", e)

            best_svd_layer = svd_layer

            if err <= self.max_error or r >= max_r:
                break

            if attempt < self.max_retries:
                next_r = min(int(r * 1.5), max_r)
                if next_r <= r:
                    break
                current_r = next_r

        return best_svd_layer, best_err, chosen_r

    def compress(self, **kwargs: Any) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Compress target linear and Conv1D layers across the model."""
        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.skipped_layers = []
        self.layer_ranks = {}
        self.layer_errors = {}
        seen_weights: set[int] = set()

        total_orig_decomp_params = 0
        total_comp_decomp_params = 0

        target_layers = [
            (name, mod)
            for name, mod in list(self.model.named_modules())
            if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
        ]

        if not target_layers:
            logger.warning("DirectSVD: No compressible linear layers found.")
            return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

        # Handle freezing of non-target layers when target_attention_only=True
        if self.target_attention_only:
            for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
                target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
                is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
                is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
                if is_linear or is_conv1d:
                    if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                        for p in module.parameters():
                            p.requires_grad = False
                        self.skipped_layers.append(name)

        for name, module in target_layers:
            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            if not hasattr(target_mod, "weight") or target_mod.weight is None:
                continue
            w_id = id(target_mod.weight)
            if w_id in seen_weights:
                self.skipped_layers.append(name)
                continue
            seen_weights.add(w_id)

            raw_w = target_mod.weight.data
            cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            if is_conv1d:
                in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            else:
                out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]

            orig_params = out_f * in_f
            total_orig_decomp_params += orig_params

            svd_layer, err, r = self.compress_linear(module, rank=self.rank)
            self.layer_errors[name] = err
            self.layer_ranks[name] = r

            if svd_layer is None or err > self.max_error:
                logger.warning(
                    "DirectSVD reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f.",
                    err,
                    name,
                    self.max_error,
                )
                if self.fallback_to_original:
                    logger.info(
                        "Skipping DirectSVD compression for %s (fallback to original and frozen).",
                        name,
                    )
                    self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    total_comp_decomp_params += orig_params
                    continue

            replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)
            self.compressed_layers.append(name)
            total_comp_decomp_params += r * (in_f + out_f)

        # Post-compression forward sanity check
        forward_check_success = False
        try:
            self.model.eval()
            with torch.no_grad():
                d = next(self.model.parameters()).device
                dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 16), device=d)
                self.model(dummy)
            forward_check_success = True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("DirectSVD post-compression forward sanity check exception: %s", e)

        effective_ratio = (
            total_comp_decomp_params / total_orig_decomp_params
            if total_orig_decomp_params > 0
            else 1.0
        )
        self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        avg_err = (
            sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
            / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
            if self.compressed_layers
            else 0.0
        )
        stats.update(
            {
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "compressed_layer_count": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "fallback_layer_count": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
                "skipped_layer_count": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "original_layer_count": len(target_layers),
                "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "lora_r": self.lora_r,
                "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "effective_parameter_ratio": round(effective_ratio, 4),
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "method": "direct_truncated_svd",
            }
        )
        logger.info(
            "Compressor direct_svd finished: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, max_err_threshold=%.4f",
            len(self.compressed_layers),
            len(self.fallback_layers),
            avg_err,
            self.max_error,
        )
        self.stats = stats
        return self.model, stats

Base class for all compressors. Not a nn.Module to avoid parameter storage.

var is_custom_attention : bool

Direct SVD Compressor.

Performs direct truncated SVD on target linear and Conv1D layers: W ≈ U_r * Sigma_r * V_r^T Allocates singular energy symmetrically: U_factor = U_r * sqrt(Sigma_r) V_factor = sqrt(Sigma_r) * V_r^T and replaces dense layers with factorized SVDLinear modules.

Production hardening features: - Relative Frobenius reconstruction error validation with configurable max_error. - Automatic rank escalation retries when error threshold is exceeded. - Safe fallback to original layer if error remains high or decomposition fails. - Fallback and non-target layers frozen (requires_grad=False) during recovery. - Robust LoRALinear and Conv1D support (with transpose and adapter preservation). - target_attention_only parameter: restricts SVD factorization to attention projections. - Strict rank safeguard: enforces r < min(in_f, out_f) and parameter reduction r * (in + out) < in * out. - Unified critical-layer protection via should_compress_linear. - Full FP32 computation for numerical stability. - Real metrics recording: original_layer_count, compressed_layer_count, fallback_layer_count, effective_parameter_ratio.

def compress(self, **kwargs: Any) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Compress target linear and Conv1D layers across the model."""
    self.compressed_layers = []
    self.fallback_layers = []
    self.skipped_layers = []
    self.layer_ranks = {}
    self.layer_errors = {}
    seen_weights: set[int] = set()

    total_orig_decomp_params = 0
    total_comp_decomp_params = 0

    target_layers = [
        (name, mod)
        for name, mod in list(self.model.named_modules())
        if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
    ]

    if not target_layers:
        logger.warning("DirectSVD: No compressible linear layers found.")
        return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

    # Handle freezing of non-target layers when target_attention_only=True
    if self.target_attention_only:
        for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            if is_linear or is_conv1d:
                if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    self.skipped_layers.append(name)

    for name, module in target_layers:
        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        if not hasattr(target_mod, "weight") or target_mod.weight is None:
            continue
        w_id = id(target_mod.weight)
        if w_id in seen_weights:
            self.skipped_layers.append(name)
            continue
        seen_weights.add(w_id)

        raw_w = target_mod.weight.data
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        if is_conv1d:
            in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
        else:
            out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]

        orig_params = out_f * in_f
        total_orig_decomp_params += orig_params

        svd_layer, err, r = self.compress_linear(module, rank=self.rank)
        self.layer_errors[name] = err
        self.layer_ranks[name] = r

        if svd_layer is None or err > self.max_error:
            logger.warning(
                "DirectSVD reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f.",
                err,
                name,
                self.max_error,
            )
            if self.fallback_to_original:
                logger.info(
                    "Skipping DirectSVD compression for %s (fallback to original and frozen).",
                    name,
                )
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                total_comp_decomp_params += orig_params
                continue

        replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)
        self.compressed_layers.append(name)
        total_comp_decomp_params += r * (in_f + out_f)

    # Post-compression forward sanity check
    forward_check_success = False
    try:
        self.model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            d = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 16), device=d)
            self.model(dummy)
        forward_check_success = True
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug("DirectSVD post-compression forward sanity check exception: %s", e)

    effective_ratio = (
        total_comp_decomp_params / total_orig_decomp_params
        if total_orig_decomp_params > 0
        else 1.0
    )
    self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
    stats = self.get_compression_stats()
    avg_err = (
        sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
        / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
        if self.compressed_layers
        else 0.0
    )
    stats.update(
        {
            "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
            "compressed_layer_count": len(self.compressed_layers),
            "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
            "fallback_layer_count": len(self.fallback_layers),
            "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
            "skipped_layer_count": len(self.skipped_layers),
            "original_layer_count": len(target_layers),
            "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
            "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
            "max_error": self.max_error,
            "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
            "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
            "lora_r": self.lora_r,
            "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
            "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
            "effective_parameter_ratio": round(effective_ratio, 4),
            "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
            "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
            "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
            "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
            "method": "direct_truncated_svd",
        }
    )
    logger.info(
        "Compressor direct_svd finished: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, max_err_threshold=%.4f",
        len(self.compressed_layers),
        len(self.fallback_layers),
        avg_err,
        self.max_error,
    )
    self.stats = stats
    return self.model, stats

Compress target linear and Conv1D layers across the model.

def compress_linear(self, layer: nn.Module, rank: int | None = None) ‑> tuple[SVDLinear | None, float, int]
Expand source code 
def compress_linear(
    self,
    layer: nn.Module,
    rank: int | None = None,
) -> tuple[SVDLinear | None, float, int]:
    """
    Decompose a single linear or Conv1D layer via SVD with symmetric factor distribution.
    Returns: (svd_layer, relative_reconstruction_error, chosen_rank)
    """
    target_mod = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
    cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
    is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
    raw_w = target_mod.weight.data

    if is_conv1d:
        in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
        W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()  # [out_features, in_features]
    else:
        out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
        W = raw_w.float()  # [out_features, in_features]

    max_reduction_r = max(1, (in_f * out_f) // (in_f + out_f) - 1)
    max_r = max(1, min(in_f - 1, out_f - 1, max_reduction_r))

    current_r = self._determine_rank(in_f, out_f, rank)
    device = raw_w.device
    dtype = raw_w.dtype
    norm_w = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()

    try:
        U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("DirectSVD SVD computation failed: %s", e)
        return None, 1.0, 0

    best_svd_layer: SVDLinear | None = None
    best_err = 1.0
    chosen_r = current_r

    # Retry with higher rank if error exceeds threshold
    for attempt in range(self.max_retries + 1):
        r = max(1, min(current_r, max_r))
        sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
        U_r = (U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)).to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
        V_r = (sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]).to(dtype=dtype, device=device)

        W_recon = torch.matmul(U_r.float(), V_r.float())
        if norm_w < 1e-12:
            err = 0.0
        else:
            err = float(torch.norm(W - W_recon, p="fro").item() / norm_w)

        best_err = err
        chosen_r = r

        # Determine LoRA parameters to preserve or initialize
        eff_lora_r = self.lora_r
        eff_lora_alpha = self.lora_alpha
        if hasattr(layer, "lora_A") and hasattr(layer, "r"):
            eff_lora_r = getattr(layer, "r", self.lora_r)
            eff_lora_alpha = getattr(layer, "lora_alpha", self.lora_alpha)

        svd_layer = SVDLinear(
            in_features=in_f,
            out_features=out_f,
            rank=r,
            bias=target_mod.bias is not None,
            dtype=dtype,
            device=device,
            lora_r=eff_lora_r,
            lora_alpha=eff_lora_alpha,
        )
        svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(U_r, V_r)
        if target_mod.bias is not None and svd_layer.bias is not None:
            svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(target_mod.bias.data)

        # If the original layer was a LoRALinear wrapper, copy over existing adapter weights
        if (
            hasattr(layer, "lora_A")
            and hasattr(layer, "lora_B")
            and svd_layer.lora_A is not None
        ):
            try:
                if (
                    hasattr(layer.lora_A, "weight")
                    and svd_layer.lora_A.weight.shape == layer.lora_A.weight.shape
                ):
                    svd_layer.lora_A.weight.data.copy_(layer.lora_A.weight.data)
                if (
                    hasattr(layer.lora_B, "weight")
                    and svd_layer.lora_B.weight.shape == layer.lora_B.weight.shape
                ):
                    svd_layer.lora_B.weight.data.copy_(layer.lora_B.weight.data)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("DirectSVD adapter preservation notice: %s", e)

        best_svd_layer = svd_layer

        if err <= self.max_error or r >= max_r:
            break

        if attempt < self.max_retries:
            next_r = min(int(r * 1.5), max_r)
            if next_r <= r:
                break
            current_r = next_r

    return best_svd_layer, best_err, chosen_r

Expand source code 
class DirectSVDEncoderLayer(nn.Module):
    """Encoder layer that replaces MHA with SVDSingleHead + standard FFN."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        hidden_size: int | None = None,
        intermediate_size: int | None = None,
        num_attention_heads: int | None = None,
        svd_rank: int = 20,
        layer_norm_eps: float = 1e-12,
        hidden_dropout_prob: float = 0.1,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.config = config
        self.hidden_size = int(hidden_size or getattr(config, "hidden_size", 768))
        self.intermediate_size = int(
            intermediate_size
            or getattr(config, "intermediate_size", None)
            or (4 * self.hidden_size)
        )
        self.num_attention_heads = int(
            num_attention_heads or getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", 12)
        )
        self.svd_rank = int(getattr(config, "svd_rank", None) or svd_rank)
        self.lora_r = int(lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)

        self.svd_attention = SVDSingleHead(self.hidden_size, svd_rank=self.svd_rank)
        self.dense_attn = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.hidden_size)
        self.LayerNorm_attn = nn.LayerNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=layer_norm_eps)
        self.dropout_attn = nn.Dropout(hidden_dropout_prob)

        self.dense_intermediate = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.intermediate_size)
        self.intermediate_act = nn.GELU()
        self.dense_output = nn.Linear(self.intermediate_size, self.hidden_size)
        self.LayerNorm_output = nn.LayerNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=layer_norm_eps)
        self.dropout_output = nn.Dropout(hidden_dropout_prob)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool = False,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> torch.Tensor | tuple[torch.Tensor, ...]:
        attn_out = self.svd_attention(hidden_states)
        attn_proj = self.dropout_attn(self.dense_attn(attn_out))
        h = self.LayerNorm_attn(hidden_states + attn_proj)

        inter = self.intermediate_act(self.dense_intermediate(h))
        inter = self.dropout_output(self.dense_output(inter))
        output = self.LayerNorm_output(h + inter)

        if output_attentions:
            return (output, None)
        return output

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

Expand source code 
class DobiSVDCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    """
    Dobi-SVD Compressor: Differentiable SVD with learnable spectral gating.

    Extracts truncated SVD factors from pretrained weights with symmetric
    square-root singular value distribution (U_r * sqrt(Sigma_r), sqrt(Sigma_r) * V_r^T),
    compensates for initial gate value to ensure near-lossless initialization, and
    replaces layers with DobiSVDLinearLayer containing learnable gating parameters.

    Production hardening features:
    - Relative Frobenius reconstruction error validation with configurable max_error.
    - Automatic rank escalation retries when error exceeds threshold.
    - Safe fallback to original module if error remains high.
    - Fallback and non-target layers frozen (requires_grad=False) during recovery.
    - Robust LoRALinear and Conv1D support (with transpose and adapter preservation).
    - target_attention_only parameter: restricts SVD factorization to attention projections.
    - Strict rank bounds guaranteeing parameter reduction.
    - Critical-layer protection via should_compress_linear.
    - Detailed per-layer stats, avg error, and effective parameter ratio.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        init_rank_ratio: float = 0.5,
        rank: int | None = None,
        init_gate_val: float = 2.5,
        max_error: float = 0.35,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        target_attention_only: bool = True,
        max_retries: int = 2,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        cfg: Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="dobi", **kwargs)
        cfg_obj = cfg if cfg is not None else kwargs.get("comp_config")
        self.cfg = cfg_obj

        init_rank_ratio = _cfg_get(
            cfg_obj,
            "dobi_init_rank_ratio",
            _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "init_rank_ratio", init_rank_ratio),
        )
        rank = _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "dobi_rank", _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "rank", rank))
        init_gate_val = _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "init_gate_val", init_gate_val)
        max_error = _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "dobi_max_error", _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "max_error", max_error))
        fallback_to_original = _cfg_get(
            cfg_obj,
            "dobi_fallback_to_original",
            _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original),
        )
        target_attention_only = _cfg_get(
            cfg_obj,
            "dobi_target_attention_only",
            _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only),
        )

        if "rank" in kwargs and kwargs["rank"] is not None:
            rank = kwargs["rank"]

        self.init_rank_ratio = float(init_rank_ratio)
        self.rank = rank
        self.init_gate_val = float(init_gate_val)
        if max_error is None or max_error == 0.35:
            max_error = adaptive_max_error(self.rank, 768, 768, "distilbert")

        self.max_error = float(max_error)
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(fallback_to_original)
        self.target_attention_only = bool(target_attention_only)
        self.max_retries = int(max_retries)
        self.target_modules = target_modules

        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.skipped_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_ranks: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        name_lower = name.lower()
        if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
            return False
        if any(
            k in name_lower
            for k in [
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embedding",
                "router",
                "gate",
            ]
        ):
            return False

        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        if not (is_linear or is_conv1d):
            return False
        if isinstance(target_mod, DobiSVDLinearLayer) or isinstance(module, DobiSVDLinearLayer):
            return False
        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            last_part = name.split(".")[-1]
            is_attn = (last_part in _ATTN_NAMES) or any(
                k in name_lower for k in ("attn", "attention")
            )
            if not is_attn:
                return False

        if self.target_modules is not None:
            return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)
        return True

    def _determine_rank(self, in_f: int, out_f: int, target_r: int | None) -> int:
        """Compute target rank guaranteeing parameter reduction and physical bounds."""
        max_reduction_r = max(1, (in_f * out_f) // (in_f + out_f) - 1)
        max_r = max(1, min(in_f - 1, out_f - 1, max_reduction_r, 128))

        if target_r is not None:
            r = target_r
        else:
            r = int(round(min(in_f, out_f) * self.init_rank_ratio))

        return max(1, min(r, max_r))

    def compress_linear(
        self,
        layer: nn.Module,
        rank: int | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[DobiSVDLinearLayer | None, float, int]:
        """Decompose a single linear or Conv1D layer into DobiSVDLinearLayer."""
        target_mod = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        raw_w = target_mod.weight.data

        # Determine in_features and out_features accurately for Linear, Conv1D, and fused QKV
        in_f = None
        out_f = None
        if (
            target_mod.bias is not None
            and getattr(target_mod.bias, "ndim", 0) == 1
            and raw_w.ndim == 2
        ):
            bias_len = target_mod.bias.shape[0]
            if raw_w.shape[1] == bias_len and raw_w.shape[0] != bias_len:
                # Conv1D format: [in_features, out_features]
                in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            elif raw_w.shape[0] == bias_len and raw_w.shape[1] != bias_len:
                # Linear format: [out_features, in_features]
                out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]

        if in_f is None or out_f is None:
            if hasattr(target_mod, "in_features") and hasattr(target_mod, "out_features"):
                in_f, out_f = target_mod.in_features, target_mod.out_features
            elif hasattr(target_mod, "nf") and hasattr(target_mod, "nx"):
                in_f, out_f = target_mod.nx, target_mod.nf
            elif "conv1d" in cls_name or (raw_w.ndim == 2 and raw_w.shape[0] < raw_w.shape[1]):
                in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            else:
                out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]

        # W must strictly be [out_features, in_features]
        if raw_w.shape == (out_f, in_f):
            W = raw_w.float()
        elif raw_w.shape == (in_f, out_f):
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
        else:
            out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.float()

        assert W.shape == (
            out_f,
            in_f,
        ), f"W shape {W.shape} does not match ({out_f}, {in_f})"

        max_reduction_r = max(1, (in_f * out_f) // (in_f + out_f) - 1)
        max_r = max(1, min(in_f - 1, out_f - 1, max_reduction_r, 128))

        current_r = self._determine_rank(in_f, out_f, rank)
        device = raw_w.device
        dtype = raw_w.dtype
        norm_w = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()
        if norm_w < 1e-4:
            logger.warning(
                "DobiSVD: weight tensor has near-zero Frobenius norm (%.6e < 1e-4)",
                norm_w,
            )
            return None, 1.0, 0

        try:
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("DobiSVD SVD computation failed: %s", e)
            return None, 1.0, 0

        best_layer: DobiSVDLinearLayer | None = None
        best_err = 1.0
        chosen_r = current_r

        # Initial gate scaling factor to achieve exact initial SVD energy
        init_gate_scale = 1.0 / (1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-self.init_gate_val)))

        retries = 0 if rank is not None else self.max_retries
        for attempt in range(retries + 1):
            r = max(1, min(current_r, max_r))
            sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
            U_r = (U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)).to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
            # Pre-scale V by 1/sigma(alpha) so initial gated output matches SVD reconstruction
            V_r = (sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :] * init_gate_scale).to(dtype=dtype, device=device)

            dobi_layer = DobiSVDLinearLayer(
                in_features=in_f,
                out_features=out_f,
                rank=r,
                bias=target_mod.bias is not None,
                init_gate_val=self.init_gate_val,
                dtype=dtype,
                device=device,
            )
            dobi_layer.set_low_rank_factors(U_r, V_r)
            assert dobi_layer.U.shape == (
                out_f,
                r,
            ), f"U shape mismatch: {dobi_layer.U.shape} vs ({out_f}, {r})"
            assert dobi_layer.V.shape == (
                r,
                in_f,
            ), f"V shape mismatch: {dobi_layer.V.shape} vs ({r}, {in_f})"

            if target_mod.bias is not None and dobi_layer.bias is not None:
                tb = target_mod.bias.data
                db = dobi_layer.bias.data
                min_b = min(tb.numel(), db.numel())
                db[:min_b].copy_(tb[:min_b])

            W_recon = dobi_layer.weight.float()
            recon_norm = torch.norm(W_recon, p="fro").item()
            if recon_norm < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    "DobiSVD: reconstructed weight Frobenius norm too small (%.6e < 1e-4)",
                    recon_norm,
                )
                continue

            if norm_w < 1e-12:
                err = 0.0
            else:
                err = float(torch.norm(W - W_recon, p="fro").item() / norm_w)

            best_err = err
            chosen_r = r
            best_layer = dobi_layer

            if err <= self.max_error or r >= max_r:
                break

            if attempt < retries:
                next_r = min(int(r * 1.5), max_r)
                if next_r <= r:
                    break
                current_r = next_r

        if best_layer is not None:
            best_layer.reconstruction_error = best_err
            return best_layer, best_err, chosen_r
        return None, best_err, chosen_r

    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Compress all target linear and Conv1D layers across the model."""
        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.skipped_layers = []
        self.layer_ranks = {}
        self.layer_errors = {}

        total_orig_decomp_params = 0
        total_comp_decomp_params = 0

        target_layers = [
            (name, mod)
            for name, mod in self.model.named_modules()
            if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
        ]

        if not target_layers:
            logger.warning("Dobi-SVD: No compressible linear layers found.")
            return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

        # If target_attention_only=True, freeze non-target linear/conv layers during recovery
        if self.target_attention_only:
            for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
                target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
                is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
                is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
                if is_linear or is_conv1d:
                    if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                        for p in module.parameters():
                            p.requires_grad = False
                        self.skipped_layers.append(name)

        for name, module in target_layers:
            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            raw_w = target_mod.weight.data
            cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            in_f = None
            out_f = None
            if (
                target_mod.bias is not None
                and getattr(target_mod.bias, "ndim", 0) == 1
                and raw_w.ndim == 2
            ):
                bias_len = target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                if raw_w.shape[1] == bias_len and raw_w.shape[0] != bias_len:
                    in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
                elif raw_w.shape[0] == bias_len and raw_w.shape[1] != bias_len:
                    out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]

            if in_f is None or out_f is None:
                if hasattr(target_mod, "in_features") and hasattr(target_mod, "out_features"):
                    in_f, out_f = target_mod.in_features, target_mod.out_features
                elif hasattr(target_mod, "nf") and hasattr(target_mod, "nx"):
                    in_f, out_f = target_mod.nx, target_mod.nf
                elif "conv1d" in cls_name or (raw_w.ndim == 2 and raw_w.shape[0] < raw_w.shape[1]):
                    in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
                else:
                    out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]

            orig_params = out_f * in_f
            total_orig_decomp_params += orig_params

            new_layer, err, r = self.compress_linear(module, rank=self.rank)
            self.layer_errors[name] = err
            self.layer_ranks[name] = r

            if new_layer is None or err > self.max_error:
                logger.warning(
                    "Dobi-SVD reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f.",
                    err,
                    name,
                    self.max_error,
                )
                if self.fallback_to_original:
                    logger.info(
                        "Skipping Dobi-SVD compression for %s (fallback to original and frozen).",
                        name,
                    )
                    self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    total_comp_decomp_params += orig_params
                    continue

            # If wrapped in LoRALinear, replace the inner base_layer or linear
            has_base_layer = hasattr(module, "base_layer") or (
                hasattr(module, "linear") and (hasattr(module, "lora_A") or hasattr(module, "r"))
            )
            if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                module.base_layer = new_layer
            elif hasattr(module, "linear") and (hasattr(module, "lora_A") or hasattr(module, "r")):
                module.linear = new_layer
            else:
                replace_module(self.model, name, new_layer)

            if has_base_layer:
                for p_name, p in module.named_parameters():
                    if any(t in p_name.lower() for t in ("lora_a", "lora_b", "adapter")):
                        p.requires_grad = True

            self.compressed_layers.append(name)
            # Parameters: U (out*r) + V (r*in) + gate (r)
            comp_params = r * (in_f + out_f) + r
            total_comp_decomp_params += comp_params

        effective_ratio = (
            total_comp_decomp_params / total_orig_decomp_params
            if total_orig_decomp_params > 0
            else 1.0
        )
        self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        avg_err = (
            sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
            / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
            if self.compressed_layers
            else 0.0
        )
        stats.update(
            {
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "compressed_layer_count": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "fallback_layer_count": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
                "skipped_layer_count": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "original_layer_count": len(target_layers),
                "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "init_rank_ratio": self.init_rank_ratio,
                "init_gate_val": self.init_gate_val,
                "effective_parameter_ratio": round(effective_ratio, 4),
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
            }
        )
        logger.info(
            "Compressor dobi finished: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, max_err_threshold=%.4f",
            len(self.compressed_layers),
            len(self.fallback_layers),
            avg_err,
            self.max_error,
        )
        return self.model, stats

Dobi-SVD Compressor: Differentiable SVD with learnable spectral gating.

Extracts truncated SVD factors from pretrained weights with symmetric square-root singular value distribution (U_r * sqrt(Sigma_r), sqrt(Sigma_r) * V_r^T), compensates for initial gate value to ensure near-lossless initialization, and replaces layers with DobiSVDLinearLayer containing learnable gating parameters.

Production hardening features: - Relative Frobenius reconstruction error validation with configurable max_error. - Automatic rank escalation retries when error exceeds threshold. - Safe fallback to original module if error remains high. - Fallback and non-target layers frozen (requires_grad=False) during recovery. - Robust LoRALinear and Conv1D support (with transpose and adapter preservation). - target_attention_only parameter: restricts SVD factorization to attention projections. - Strict rank bounds guaranteeing parameter reduction. - Critical-layer protection via should_compress_linear. - Detailed per-layer stats, avg error, and effective parameter ratio.

Ancestors
