@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("erc_svd")
class ERCCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    """
    Error-Controlled SVD (ERC-SVD) Compressor.

    Finds the minimal rank r per layer such that
        ||W - W_r||_F / ||W||_F <= allowed_error.
    Factored symmetrically (U_r sqrt(S), sqrt(S) V_r^T) and replaces with SVDLinear.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        allowed_error: float = 0.20,
        max_error: float | None = None,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        rank: int | None = None,
        max_retries: int = 2,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        cfg: Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="erc", **kwargs)
        cfg = cfg or kwargs.get("comp_config")
        self.cfg = cfg

        c_allowed = _cfg_get(
            cfg, "erc_allowed_error", _cfg_get(cfg, "allowed_error", allowed_error)
        )
        effective_max = _cfg_get(cfg, "erc_max_error", _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error))
        if effective_max is None and (c_allowed is None or c_allowed <= 0.25):
            effective_max = adaptive_max_error(rank, 768, 768, "distilbert")
        if c_allowed is None or c_allowed <= 0.25:
            c_allowed = adaptive_max_error(rank, 768, 768, "distilbert")

        self.allowed_error = float(c_allowed)
        self.max_error = float(effective_max if effective_max is not None else self.allowed_error)
        self.target_attention_only = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "erc_target_attention_only",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only),
            )
        )
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "erc_fallback_to_original",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original),
            )
        )
        self.rank = _cfg_get(cfg, "erc_rank", _cfg_get(cfg, "rank", rank))
        if self.rank is not None:
            self.rank = int(self.rank)
        self.max_retries = max(0, int(max_retries))
        self.target_modules = target_modules

        # Pre-LayerNorm encoder detection (e.g., RoBERTa): pre-LN amplifies residual truncation noise.
        # Enforce stricter maximum error threshold (0.15) to prevent below-chance catastrophic collapse.
        model_name_or_cls = (
            getattr(getattr(model, "config", None), "_name_or_path", "") or model.__class__.__name__
        ).lower()
        self.is_pre_layernorm_encoder = "roberta" in model_name_or_cls
        if self.is_pre_layernorm_encoder and self.max_error > 0.30:
            logger.info(
                "ERC-SVD: Detected pre-LayerNorm encoder (%s). Capping max_error from %.2f to 0.30.",
                model_name_or_cls,
                self.max_error,
            )
            self.max_error = min(self.max_error, 0.30)

        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.skipped_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_ranks: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self._compressed_params: int = 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        name_lower = name.lower()
        if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
            return False
        if any(
            skip in name_lower
            for skip in [
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embedding",
                "router",
                "gate",
                "head",
                "output_layer",
            ]
        ):
            return False

        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        if not (is_linear or is_conv1d):
            return False
        if isinstance(target_mod, SVDLinear):
            return False
        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False
        if self.target_modules is not None:
            return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)

        if self.target_attention_only:
            last_part = name.split(".")[-1]
            is_attn = (last_part in _ATTN_NAMES) or any(
                k in name_lower for k in ("attn", "attention")
            )
            if not is_attn:
                return False

        return True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # ER-FIX-02: PEFT-compatible LoRA rank detection
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _detect_lora(module: nn.Module) -> tuple[int, float]:
        """Return (lora_rank, lora_alpha) from an existing LoRA wrapper if present."""
        if not hasattr(module, "lora_A"):
            return 0, 1.0
        rank = 0
        la = getattr(module, "lora_A", None)
        if la is not None:
            if hasattr(la, "weight") and la.weight is not None and la.weight.dim() >= 1:
                rank = int(la.weight.shape[0])
            elif isinstance(la, nn.Parameter) and la.dim() >= 1:
                rank = int(la.shape[0])
        alpha = 1.0
        for attr in ("lora_alpha", "alpha"):
            a = getattr(module, attr, None)
            if a is not None:
                try:
                    alpha = float(a)
                except (TypeError, ValueError):
                    pass
                break
        return rank, alpha

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress_linear(
        self,
        module: nn.Module,
    ) -> tuple[SVDLinear | None, float, int]:
        """Decompose a single linear, Conv1D, or LoRALinear layer via error-controlled SVD."""
        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        raw_w = target_mod.weight.data

        if is_conv1d:
            in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
        else:
            out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape
            W = raw_w.float()

        bound_r = (in_f * out_f) // (in_f + out_f)
        max_r = min(in_f, out_f, bound_r)
        if max_r < 16:
            return None, 1.0, 0

        device = raw_w.device
        dtype = raw_w.dtype
        norm_w = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()

        try:
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("ERC-SVD SVD computation failed: %s", e)
            return None, 1.0, 0

        s_squared = S**2
        total_energy = s_squared.sum()
        if total_energy <= 1e-12:
            r_init = 1
        else:
            cum_energy = torch.cumsum(s_squared, dim=0) / total_energy
            target_retention = max(0.0, 1.0 - (self.allowed_error**2))
            idx = torch.where(cum_energy >= target_retention)[0]
            r_init = int(idx[0].item()) + 1 if len(idx) > 0 else max_r

        # ER-FIX-05: clamp r_init to max_r before use.
        r_init = min(r_init, max_r)
        if self.rank is not None:
            r_init = min(r_init, int(self.rank))
        current_r = max(1, min(r_init, max_r))

        best_svd_layer: SVDLinear | None = None
        best_err = 1.0
        chosen_r = current_r

        lora_r, lora_alpha = self._detect_lora(module)

        for attempt in range(self.max_retries + 1):
            r = max(1, min(current_r, max_r))
            sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
            U_r = (U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)).to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
            V_r = (sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]).to(dtype=dtype, device=device)

            W_recon = torch.matmul(U_r.float(), V_r.float())
            if norm_w < 1e-12:
                err = 0.0
            else:
                err = float(torch.norm(W - W_recon, p="fro").item() / norm_w)

            best_err = err
            chosen_r = r

            svd_layer = SVDLinear(
                in_features=in_f,
                out_features=out_f,
                rank=r,
                bias=(
                    getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None and target_mod.bias is not None
                ),
                dtype=dtype,
                device=device,
                lora_r=lora_r,
                lora_alpha=lora_alpha,
            )
            svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(U_r, V_r)
            if (
                getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None
                and target_mod.bias is not None
                and svd_layer.bias is not None
            ):
                svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(target_mod.bias.data)

            if (
                lora_r > 0
                and getattr(svd_layer, "lora_A", None) is not None
                and getattr(svd_layer, "lora_B", None) is not None
            ):
                try:
                    src_la = getattr(module, "lora_A", None)
                    src_lb = getattr(module, "lora_B", None)
                    la_w = getattr(src_la, "weight", src_la)
                    lb_w = getattr(src_lb, "weight", src_lb)
                    if hasattr(la_w, "data") and hasattr(svd_layer.lora_A, "data"):
                        svd_layer.lora_A.data.copy_(la_w.data)
                    if hasattr(lb_w, "data") and hasattr(svd_layer.lora_B, "data"):
                        svd_layer.lora_B.data.copy_(lb_w.data)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug(
                        "ERC-SVD LoRA copy failed on %s: %s",
                        getattr(module, "_name", "?"),
                        e,
                    )

            best_svd_layer = svd_layer

            if err <= self.max_error or r >= max_r:
                break

            if attempt < self.max_retries:
                next_r = min(int(r * 1.5), max_r)
                if next_r <= r:
                    break
                current_r = next_r

        if best_svd_layer is not None:
            best_svd_layer.reconstruction_error = best_err
        return best_svd_layer, best_err, chosen_r

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Compress all target linear and Conv1D layers across the model."""
        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.skipped_layers = []
        self.layer_ranks = {}
        self.layer_errors = {}

        total_orig_params = 0
        total_comp_params = 0

        if self.target_attention_only:
            for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
                if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
                    continue
                target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
                is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
                is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
                if (is_linear or is_conv1d) and not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    self.skipped_layers.append(name)

        target_layers = [
            (name, mod)
            for name, mod in self.model.named_modules()
            if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
        ]

        if not target_layers:
            logger.warning("ERC-SVD: No compressible linear layers found.")
            return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

        for name, module in target_layers:
            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            raw_w = target_mod.weight.data
            orig_params = raw_w.numel()
            total_orig_params += orig_params

            svd_layer, err, r = self.compress_linear(module)
            self.layer_errors[name] = err
            self.layer_ranks[name] = r

            # ER-FIX-01: safe fallback when svd_layer is None.
            if svd_layer is None:
                logger.warning(
                    "ERC-SVD: could not compress %s (rank floor not met or SVD failed).",
                    name,
                )
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                total_comp_params += orig_params
                continue

            if err > self.max_error:
                logger.warning(
                    "ERC-SVD: reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f.",
                    err,
                    name,
                    self.max_error,
                )
                if self.fallback_to_original:
                    logger.info("ERC-SVD: fallback to original on %s.", name)
                    self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    total_comp_params += orig_params
                    continue
                # fallback_to_original=False: proceed with high error (warned).

            replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)
            self.compressed_layers.append(name)
            comp_params = r * (svd_layer.in_features + svd_layer.out_features)
            total_comp_params += comp_params

        # ER-FIX-03/04: forward sanity check with attention_mask.
        forward_check_success = True
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                first_p = next(self.model.parameters(), None)
                dev = first_p.device if first_p is not None else torch.device("cpu")
                dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 8), device=dev, dtype=torch.long)
                attn = torch.ones_like(dummy)
                try:
                    self.model(input_ids=dummy, attention_mask=attn)
                except Exception:
                    try:
                        self.model(dummy)
                    except Exception as e_inner:
                        logger.debug("ERC-SVD forward check (input_ids) failed: %s", e_inner)
                        first_linear = next(
                            (m for m in self.model.modules() if isinstance(m, nn.Linear)),
                            None,
                        )
                        in_dim = getattr(first_linear, "in_features", 8)
                        self.model(
                            torch.randn(
                                1,
                                8,
                                in_dim,
                                device=dev,
                                dtype=(first_p.dtype if first_p is not None else torch.float32),
                            )
                        )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("ERC-SVD forward sanity check failed: %s", e)
            forward_check_success = False

        try:
            self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        except Exception:
            self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        effective_ratio = (total_comp_params / total_orig_params) if total_orig_params > 0 else 1.0
        avg_err = (
            sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
            / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
            if self.compressed_layers
            else 0.0
        )
        try:
            stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        except Exception:
            stats = {}
        stats.update(
            {
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "compressed_layer_count": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "fallback_layer_count": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
                "skipped_layer_count": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "allowed_error": self.allowed_error,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "effective_parameter_ratio": round(effective_ratio, 4),
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "method": "erc_svd",
            }
        )
        logger.info(
            "Compressor erc finished: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, max_err_threshold=%.4f",
            len(self.compressed_layers),
            len(self.fallback_layers),
            avg_err,
            self.max_error,
        )
        return self.model, stats

Base class for all compressors. Not a nn.Module to avoid parameter storage.

Ancestors

  • BaseCompressor

Class variables

var is_custom_attention : bool

Error-Controlled SVD (ERC-SVD) Compressor.

Finds the minimal rank r per layer such that ||W - W_r||_F / ||W||_F <= allowed_error. Factored symmetrically (U_r sqrt(S), sqrt(S) V_r^T) and replaces with SVDLinear.

Methods

        self.max_error,

Compress all target linear and Conv1D layers across the model.

def compress_linear(self, module: nn.Module) ‑> tuple[SVDLinear | None, float, int]
Expand source code 
def compress_linear(
    self,
    module: nn.Module,
) -> tuple[SVDLinear | None, float, int]:
    """Decompose a single linear, Conv1D, or LoRALinear layer via error-controlled SVD."""
    target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
    cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
    is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
    raw_w = target_mod.weight.data

    if is_conv1d:
        in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
        W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
    else:
        out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape
        W = raw_w.float()

    bound_r = (in_f * out_f) // (in_f + out_f)
    max_r = min(in_f, out_f, bound_r)
    if max_r < 16:
        return None, 1.0, 0

    device = raw_w.device
    dtype = raw_w.dtype
    norm_w = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()

    try:
        U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("ERC-SVD SVD computation failed: %s", e)
        return None, 1.0, 0

    s_squared = S**2
    total_energy = s_squared.sum()
    if total_energy <= 1e-12:
        r_init = 1
    else:
        cum_energy = torch.cumsum(s_squared, dim=0) / total_energy
        target_retention = max(0.0, 1.0 - (self.allowed_error**2))
        idx = torch.where(cum_energy >= target_retention)[0]
        r_init = int(idx[0].item()) + 1 if len(idx) > 0 else max_r

    # ER-FIX-05: clamp r_init to max_r before use.
    r_init = min(r_init, max_r)
    if self.rank is not None:
        r_init = min(r_init, int(self.rank))
    current_r = max(1, min(r_init, max_r))

    best_svd_layer: SVDLinear | None = None
    best_err = 1.0
    chosen_r = current_r

    lora_r, lora_alpha = self._detect_lora(module)

    for attempt in range(self.max_retries + 1):
        r = max(1, min(current_r, max_r))
        sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
        U_r = (U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)).to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
        V_r = (sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]).to(dtype=dtype, device=device)

        W_recon = torch.matmul(U_r.float(), V_r.float())
        if norm_w < 1e-12:
            err = 0.0
        else:
            err = float(torch.norm(W - W_recon, p="fro").item() / norm_w)

        best_err = err
        chosen_r = r

        svd_layer = SVDLinear(
            in_features=in_f,
            out_features=out_f,
            rank=r,
            bias=(
                getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None and target_mod.bias is not None
            ),
            dtype=dtype,
            device=device,
            lora_r=lora_r,
            lora_alpha=lora_alpha,
        )
        svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(U_r, V_r)
        if (
            getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None
            and target_mod.bias is not None
            and svd_layer.bias is not None
        ):
            svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(target_mod.bias.data)

        if (
            lora_r > 0
            and getattr(svd_layer, "lora_A", None) is not None
            and getattr(svd_layer, "lora_B", None) is not None
        ):
            try:
                src_la = getattr(module, "lora_A", None)
                src_lb = getattr(module, "lora_B", None)
                la_w = getattr(src_la, "weight", src_la)
                lb_w = getattr(src_lb, "weight", src_lb)
                if hasattr(la_w, "data") and hasattr(svd_layer.lora_A, "data"):
                    svd_layer.lora_A.data.copy_(la_w.data)
                if hasattr(lb_w, "data") and hasattr(svd_layer.lora_B, "data"):
                    svd_layer.lora_B.data.copy_(lb_w.data)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug(
                    "ERC-SVD LoRA copy failed on %s: %s",
                    getattr(module, "_name", "?"),
                    e,
                )

        best_svd_layer = svd_layer

        if err <= self.max_error or r >= max_r:
            break

        if attempt < self.max_retries:
            next_r = min(int(r * 1.5), max_r)
            if next_r <= r:
                break
            current_r = next_r

    if best_svd_layer is not None:
        best_svd_layer.reconstruction_error = best_err
    return best_svd_layer, best_err, chosen_r

Decompose a single linear, Conv1D, or LoRALinear layer via error-controlled SVD.

@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("entropy_budget_allocation")
class EntropyBudgetCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    """
    Entropy Budget Low-Rank SVD Compressor.

    Computes activation covariance and whiten weights, calculates per-component
    singular entropy contributions, allocates low-rank budgets globally across layers,
    and replaces dense nn.Linear layers with factorized SVDLinear modules with symmetric
    singular value splitting (U * sqrt(Sigma), sqrt(Sigma) * V^T).

    Hardened for:
    - LoRALinear wrappers & Conv1D support.
    - target_attention_only filtering.
    - Relative Frobenius reconstruction error validation & fallback.
    - Freezing of fallback and skipped layers.
    - Real compression metrics recording.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        calibration_loader: Any | None = None,
        total_budget_ratio: float = 0.7,
        rank: int | None = None,
        eps: float = 1e-6,
        max_error: float = 0.35,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        cfg: Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="entropy_budget", **kwargs)
        cfg = cfg or kwargs.get("comp_config")
        self.cfg = cfg

        self.calibration_loader = calibration_loader or _cfg_get(cfg, "calibration_loader", None)
        self.total_budget_ratio = float(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "entropy_budget_total_budget_ratio",
                _cfg_get(
                    cfg,
                    "total_budget_ratio",
                    _cfg_get(cfg, "budget_ratio", total_budget_ratio),
                ),
            )
        )
        self.rank = _cfg_get(
            cfg,
            "entropy_budget_rank",
            _cfg_get(cfg, "rank", _cfg_get(cfg, "target_rank", rank)),
        )
        if self.rank is not None:
            self.rank = int(self.rank)
        self.eps = float(eps)
        c_max_error = _cfg_get(
            cfg, "entropy_budget_max_error", _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error)
        )
        if c_max_error is None or c_max_error == 0.35:
            c_max_error = adaptive_max_error(self.rank or 64, 768, 768, "distilbert")

        self.max_error = float(c_max_error)
        self.target_attention_only = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "entropy_budget_target_attention_only",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only),
            )
        )
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "entropy_budget_fallback_to_original",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original),
            )
        )
        self.target_modules = target_modules

        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.skipped_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_ranks: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        name_lower = name.lower()
        if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
            return False
        if any(
            skip in name_lower
            for skip in [
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embedding",
                "router",
                "gate",
                "head",
                "output_layer",
            ]
        ):
            return False

        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        if not (is_linear or is_conv1d):
            return False
        if isinstance(target_mod, SVDLinear):
            return False

        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False

        if hasattr(target_mod, "in_features") and hasattr(target_mod, "out_features"):
            if min(target_mod.in_features, target_mod.out_features) < 16:
                return False
        elif hasattr(target_mod, "weight") and target_mod.weight is not None:
            if min(target_mod.weight.shape) < 16:
                return False

        if self.target_modules is not None:
            return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)

        if self.target_attention_only:
            last_part = name.split(".")[-1]
            is_attn = (last_part in _ATTN_NAMES) or any(
                k in name_lower for k in ("attn", "attention")
            )
            if not is_attn:
                return False

        return True

    def collect_covariances(self) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        """Collect empirical input activation covariances with double-precision accumulation."""
        if self.calibration_loader is None:
            return {}

        activations: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        sample_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        hooks = []

        def get_hook(name: str):
            def hook(module: nn.Module, inp: tuple[Any, ...], outp: Any) -> None:
                if not inp or inp[0] is None:
                    return
                x = inp[0].detach()
                if x.ndim >= 2:
                    x_flat = x.reshape(-1, x.shape[-1]).float()
                    cov = x_flat.T @ x_flat
                    n = x_flat.shape[0]
                    if name not in activations:
                        activations[name] = cov
                        sample_counts[name] = n
                    else:
                        activations[name] += cov
                        sample_counts[name] += n

            return hook

        target_layers = [
            (name, getattr(mod, "base_layer", mod))
            for name, mod in self.model.named_modules()
            if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
        ]

        if not target_layers:
            return {}

        for name, mod in target_layers:
            hooks.append(mod.register_forward_hook(get_hook(name)))

        device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
        self.model.eval()

        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                for batch in self.calibration_loader:
                    if isinstance(batch, dict):
                        batch = {
                            k: v.to(device) if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v
                            for k, v in batch.items()
                        }
                        self.model(**batch)
                    elif isinstance(batch, (list, tuple)):
                        batch = [v.to(device) if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v for v in batch]
                        self.model(*batch)
                    elif isinstance(batch, torch.Tensor):
                        self.model(batch.to(device))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(
                "EntropyBudget: Calibration forward pass failed (%s). Falling back to weight spectra.",
                e,
            )
        finally:
            for h in hooks:
                h.remove()

        normalized_covs: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        for name, cov in activations.items():
            cnt = max(1, sample_counts.get(name, 1))
            normalized_covs[name] = cov / cnt

        return normalized_covs

    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Compress model using whitened entropy budget allocation and covariance-aligned singular vectors."""
        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.skipped_layers = []
        self.layer_ranks = {}
        self.layer_errors = {}

        total_orig_params = 0
        total_comp_params = 0

        # Freeze skipped layers if target_attention_only is enabled
        if self.target_attention_only:
            for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
                if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
                    continue
                target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
                is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
                is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
                if (is_linear or is_conv1d) and not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    self.skipped_layers.append(name)

        target_layers = [
            (name, mod)
            for name, mod in self.model.named_modules()
            if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
        ]

        if not target_layers:
            logger.warning("EntropyBudget: No compressible linear layers found.")
            return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

        covs = self.collect_covariances()
        residual_candidates = []
        layer_decomp_info = {}

        # Step 1: Compute covariance-whitened singular decomposition and singular entropy per layer
        for name, module in target_layers:
            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            raw_w = target_mod.weight.data

            if is_conv1d:
                in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
                W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
            else:
                out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
                W = raw_w.float()

            orig_layer_params = out_f * in_f
            total_orig_params += orig_layer_params
            max_r = min(out_f, in_f)
            r_floor = max(1, min(16, max_r))

            cov_inv_sqrt = None
            if name in covs and covs[name].shape == (in_f, in_f):
                cov = covs[name].to(device=W.device, dtype=torch.float32)
                try:
                    eigvals, eigvecs = torch.linalg.eigh(
                        cov + 1e-4 * torch.eye(in_f, device=W.device)
                    )
                    eigvals_clamped = torch.clamp(eigvals, min=1e-4)
                    cov_sqrt = eigvecs @ torch.diag(torch.sqrt(eigvals_clamped)) @ eigvecs.T
                    cov_inv_sqrt = (
                        eigvecs @ torch.diag(1.0 / torch.sqrt(eigvals_clamped)) @ eigvecs.T
                    )
                    M = W @ cov_sqrt
                except Exception:
                    M = W
                    cov_inv_sqrt = None
            else:
                M = W

            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(M, full_matrices=False)
            layer_decomp_info[name] = (
                U,
                S,
                Vt,
                cov_inv_sqrt,
                max_r,
                r_floor,
                W,
                is_conv1d,
                target_mod,
            )

            total_s = S.sum() + self.eps
            p = torch.clamp(S / total_s, min=self.eps, max=1.0)
            # B18: Shannon entropy in bits using log2
            entropy = -torch.sum(p * torch.log2(p)).item()
            entropy = max(entropy, 1e-4)

            for i in range(r_floor, max_r):
                s_val = S[i].item()
                score = (s_val**2) / entropy
                residual_candidates.append((name, i, s_val, score))

        # Step 2: Allocate ranks
        if self.rank is not None:
            allocated_ranks = {
                name: max(
                    layer_decomp_info[name][5],
                    min(self.rank, layer_decomp_info[name][4]),
                )
                for name, _ in target_layers
            }
        else:
            allocated_ranks = {name: layer_decomp_info[name][5] for name, _ in target_layers}
            base_allocated = sum(allocated_ranks.values())
            total_possible = sum(layer_decomp_info[name][4] for name, _ in target_layers)
            target_total = max(
                base_allocated,
                min(total_possible, int(total_possible * self.total_budget_ratio)),
            )
            residual_budget = target_total - base_allocated

            residual_candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[3], reverse=True)
            for c in residual_candidates[:residual_budget]:
                allocated_ranks[c[0]] += 1

        # Step 3: Factorized replacement with validation & fallback
        forward_check_success = True

        for name, module in target_layers:
            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            r = allocated_ranks[name]
            U, S, Vt, cov_inv_sqrt, max_r, _, W, is_conv1d, _ = layer_decomp_info[name]
            out_f, in_f = W.shape
            orig_layer_params = out_f * in_f
            device = target_mod.weight.device
            dtype = target_mod.weight.dtype
            r = max(1, min(r, max_r))

            sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
            U_r = U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
            if cov_inv_sqrt is not None:
                V_r = (sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]) @ cov_inv_sqrt
                norm_u = torch.norm(U_r, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
                norm_v = torch.norm(V_r, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
                scale = torch.sqrt(norm_u / norm_v)
                U_r = U_r / scale
                V_r = V_r * scale
            else:
                V_r = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]

            # Compute reconstructed weight & Frobenius error
            W_recon = torch.matmul(U_r.float(), V_r.float())
            norm_w = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()
            err = (
                float(torch.norm(W - W_recon, p="fro").item() / max(norm_w, 1e-12))
                if norm_w > 1e-12
                else 0.0
            )
            self.layer_errors[name] = err
            self.layer_ranks[name] = r

            if err > self.max_error and self.fallback_to_original:
                logger.warning(
                    "EntropyBudget error %.4f on %s > max_error %.4f; falling back.",
                    err,
                    name,
                    self.max_error,
                )
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                total_comp_params += orig_layer_params
                continue

            U_r = U_r.to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
            V_r = V_r.to(dtype=dtype, device=device)

            lora_r = getattr(module, "r", 0) if hasattr(module, "lora_A") else 0
            lora_alpha = getattr(module, "lora_alpha", 1.0) if hasattr(module, "lora_A") else 1.0

            svd_layer = SVDLinear(
                in_features=in_f,
                out_features=out_f,
                rank=r,
                bias=getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None and target_mod.bias is not None,
                dtype=dtype,
                device=device,
                lora_r=lora_r,
                lora_alpha=lora_alpha,
            )
            svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(U_r, V_r)
            if (
                getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None
                and target_mod.bias is not None
                and svd_layer.bias is not None
            ):
                svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(target_mod.bias.data)

            # If module was LoRALinear wrapper, copy existing trained adapter weights
            if (
                hasattr(module, "lora_A")
                and hasattr(module, "lora_B")
                and svd_layer.lora_A is not None
                and svd_layer.lora_B is not None
            ):
                if hasattr(module.lora_A, "weight") and module.lora_A.weight is not None:
                    svd_layer.lora_A.data.copy_(module.lora_A.weight.data)
                elif isinstance(module.lora_A, nn.Parameter):
                    svd_layer.lora_A.data.copy_(module.lora_A.data)
                if hasattr(module.lora_B, "weight") and module.lora_B.weight is not None:
                    svd_layer.lora_B.data.copy_(module.lora_B.weight.data)
                elif isinstance(module.lora_B, nn.Parameter):
                    svd_layer.lora_B.data.copy_(module.lora_B.data)

            replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)
            self.compressed_layers.append(name)
            comp_layer_params = r * (in_f + out_f)
            total_comp_params += comp_layer_params

        # Sanity check forward pass
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                first_p = next(self.model.parameters(), None)
                dev = first_p.device if first_p is not None else torch.device("cpu")
                dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 8), device=dev)
                try:
                    self.model(dummy)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
        except Exception:
            forward_check_success = False

        effective_ratio = total_comp_params / total_orig_params if total_orig_params > 0 else 1.0
        avg_err = (
            sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
            / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
            if self.compressed_layers
            else 0.0
        )

        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        stats.update(
            {
                "allocated_ranks": allocated_ranks,
                "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "compressed_layer_count": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "fallback_layer_count": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
                "skipped_layer_count": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "effective_parameter_ratio": round(effective_ratio, 4),
                "total_budget_ratio": self.total_budget_ratio,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
            }
        )
        logger.info(
            "Compressor entropy_budget finished: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, max_err_threshold=%.4f",
            len(self.compressed_layers),
            len(self.fallback_layers),
            avg_err,
            self.max_error,
        )
        return self.model, stats

Base class for all compressors. Not a nn.Module to avoid parameter storage.

def collect_covariances(self) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
    """Collect empirical input activation covariances with double-precision accumulation."""
    if self.calibration_loader is None:
        return {}

    activations: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
    sample_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    hooks = []

    def get_hook(name: str):
        def hook(module: nn.Module, inp: tuple[Any, ...], outp: Any) -> None:
            if not inp or inp[0] is None:
                return
            x = inp[0].detach()
            if x.ndim >= 2:
                x_flat = x.reshape(-1, x.shape[-1]).float()
                cov = x_flat.T @ x_flat
                n = x_flat.shape[0]
                if name not in activations:
                    activations[name] = cov
                    sample_counts[name] = n
                else:
                    activations[name] += cov
                    sample_counts[name] += n

        return hook

    target_layers = [
        (name, getattr(mod, "base_layer", mod))
        for name, mod in self.model.named_modules()
        if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
    ]

    if not target_layers:
        return {}

    for name, mod in target_layers:
        hooks.append(mod.register_forward_hook(get_hook(name)))

    device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
    self.model.eval()

    try:
        with torch.no_grad():
            for batch in self.calibration_loader:
                if isinstance(batch, dict):
                    batch = {
                        k: v.to(device) if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v
                        for k, v in batch.items()
                    }
                    self.model(**batch)
                elif isinstance(batch, (list, tuple)):
                    batch = [v.to(device) if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v for v in batch]
                    self.model(*batch)
                elif isinstance(batch, torch.Tensor):
                    self.model(batch.to(device))
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(
            "EntropyBudget: Calibration forward pass failed (%s). Falling back to weight spectra.",
            e,
        )
    finally:
        for h in hooks:
            h.remove()

    normalized_covs: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
    for name, cov in activations.items():
        cnt = max(1, sample_counts.get(name, 1))
        normalized_covs[name] = cov / cnt

    return normalized_covs

def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Compress model using whitened entropy budget allocation and covariance-aligned singular vectors."""
    self.compressed_layers = []
    self.fallback_layers = []
    self.skipped_layers = []
    self.layer_ranks = {}
    self.layer_errors = {}

    total_orig_params = 0
    total_comp_params = 0

    # Freeze skipped layers if target_attention_only is enabled
    if self.target_attention_only:
        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
                continue
            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            if (is_linear or is_conv1d) and not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                self.skipped_layers.append(name)

    target_layers = [
        (name, mod)
        for name, mod in self.model.named_modules()
        if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
    ]

    if not target_layers:
        logger.warning("EntropyBudget: No compressible linear layers found.")
        return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

    covs = self.collect_covariances()
    residual_candidates = []
    layer_decomp_info = {}

    # Step 1: Compute covariance-whitened singular decomposition and singular entropy per layer
    for name, module in target_layers:
        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        raw_w = target_mod.weight.data

        if is_conv1d:
            in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
        else:
            out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.float()

        orig_layer_params = out_f * in_f
        total_orig_params += orig_layer_params
        max_r = min(out_f, in_f)
        r_floor = max(1, min(16, max_r))

        cov_inv_sqrt = None
        if name in covs and covs[name].shape == (in_f, in_f):
            cov = covs[name].to(device=W.device, dtype=torch.float32)
            try:
                eigvals, eigvecs = torch.linalg.eigh(
                    cov + 1e-4 * torch.eye(in_f, device=W.device)
                )
                eigvals_clamped = torch.clamp(eigvals, min=1e-4)
                cov_sqrt = eigvecs @ torch.diag(torch.sqrt(eigvals_clamped)) @ eigvecs.T
                cov_inv_sqrt = (
                    eigvecs @ torch.diag(1.0 / torch.sqrt(eigvals_clamped)) @ eigvecs.T
                )
                M = W @ cov_sqrt
            except Exception:
                M = W
                cov_inv_sqrt = None
        else:
            M = W

        U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(M, full_matrices=False)
        layer_decomp_info[name] = (
            U,
            S,
            Vt,
            cov_inv_sqrt,
            max_r,
            r_floor,
            W,
            is_conv1d,
            target_mod,
        )

        total_s = S.sum() + self.eps
        p = torch.clamp(S / total_s, min=self.eps, max=1.0)
        # B18: Shannon entropy in bits using log2
        entropy = -torch.sum(p * torch.log2(p)).item()
        entropy = max(entropy, 1e-4)

        for i in range(r_floor, max_r):
            s_val = S[i].item()
            score = (s_val**2) / entropy
            residual_candidates.append((name, i, s_val, score))

    # Step 2: Allocate ranks
    if self.rank is not None:
        allocated_ranks = {
            name: max(
                layer_decomp_info[name][5],
                min(self.rank, layer_decomp_info[name][4]),
            )
            for name, _ in target_layers
        }
    else:
        allocated_ranks = {name: layer_decomp_info[name][5] for name, _ in target_layers}
        base_allocated = sum(allocated_ranks.values())
        total_possible = sum(layer_decomp_info[name][4] for name, _ in target_layers)
        target_total = max(
            base_allocated,
            min(total_possible, int(total_possible * self.total_budget_ratio)),
        )
        residual_budget = target_total - base_allocated

        residual_candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[3], reverse=True)
        for c in residual_candidates[:residual_budget]:
            allocated_ranks[c[0]] += 1

    # Step 3: Factorized replacement with validation & fallback
    forward_check_success = True

    for name, module in target_layers:
        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        r = allocated_ranks[name]
        U, S, Vt, cov_inv_sqrt, max_r, _, W, is_conv1d, _ = layer_decomp_info[name]
        out_f, in_f = W.shape
        orig_layer_params = out_f * in_f
        device = target_mod.weight.device
        dtype = target_mod.weight.dtype
        r = max(1, min(r, max_r))

        sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
        U_r = U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
        if cov_inv_sqrt is not None:
            V_r = (sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]) @ cov_inv_sqrt
            norm_u = torch.norm(U_r, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
            norm_v = torch.norm(V_r, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
            scale = torch.sqrt(norm_u / norm_v)
            U_r = U_r / scale
            V_r = V_r * scale
        else:
            V_r = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]

        # Compute reconstructed weight & Frobenius error
        W_recon = torch.matmul(U_r.float(), V_r.float())
        norm_w = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()
        err = (
            float(torch.norm(W - W_recon, p="fro").item() / max(norm_w, 1e-12))
            if norm_w > 1e-12
            else 0.0
        )
        self.layer_errors[name] = err
        self.layer_ranks[name] = r

        if err > self.max_error and self.fallback_to_original:
            logger.warning(
                "EntropyBudget error %.4f on %s > max_error %.4f; falling back.",
                err,
                name,
                self.max_error,
            )
            self.fallback_layers.append(name)
            for p in module.parameters():
                p.requires_grad = False
            total_comp_params += orig_layer_params
            continue

        U_r = U_r.to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
        V_r = V_r.to(dtype=dtype, device=device)

        lora_r = getattr(module, "r", 0) if hasattr(module, "lora_A") else 0
        lora_alpha = getattr(module, "lora_alpha", 1.0) if hasattr(module, "lora_A") else 1.0

        svd_layer = SVDLinear(
            in_features=in_f,
            out_features=out_f,
            rank=r,
            bias=getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None and target_mod.bias is not None,
            dtype=dtype,
            device=device,
            lora_r=lora_r,
            lora_alpha=lora_alpha,
        )
        svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(U_r, V_r)
        if (
            getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None
            and target_mod.bias is not None
            and svd_layer.bias is not None
        ):
            svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(target_mod.bias.data)

        # If module was LoRALinear wrapper, copy existing trained adapter weights
        if (
            hasattr(module, "lora_A")
            and hasattr(module, "lora_B")
            and svd_layer.lora_A is not None
            and svd_layer.lora_B is not None
        ):
            if hasattr(module.lora_A, "weight") and module.lora_A.weight is not None:
                svd_layer.lora_A.data.copy_(module.lora_A.weight.data)
            elif isinstance(module.lora_A, nn.Parameter):
                svd_layer.lora_A.data.copy_(module.lora_A.data)
            if hasattr(module.lora_B, "weight") and module.lora_B.weight is not None:
                svd_layer.lora_B.data.copy_(module.lora_B.weight.data)
            elif isinstance(module.lora_B, nn.Parameter):
                svd_layer.lora_B.data.copy_(module.lora_B.data)

        replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)
        self.compressed_layers.append(name)
        comp_layer_params = r * (in_f + out_f)
        total_comp_params += comp_layer_params

    # Sanity check forward pass
    try:
        with torch.no_grad():
            first_p = next(self.model.parameters(), None)
            dev = first_p.device if first_p is not None else torch.device("cpu")
            dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 8), device=dev)
            try:
                self.model(dummy)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
    except Exception:
        forward_check_success = False

    effective_ratio = total_comp_params / total_orig_params if total_orig_params > 0 else 1.0
    avg_err = (
        sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
        / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
        if self.compressed_layers
        else 0.0
    )

    stats = self.get_compression_stats()
    stats.update(
        {
            "allocated_ranks": allocated_ranks,
            "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
            "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
            "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
            "compressed_layer_count": len(self.compressed_layers),
            "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
            "fallback_layer_count": len(self.fallback_layers),
            "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
            "skipped_layer_count": len(self.skipped_layers),
            "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
            "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
            "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
            "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
            "effective_parameter_ratio": round(effective_ratio, 4),
            "total_budget_ratio": self.total_budget_ratio,
            "max_error": self.max_error,
            "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
            "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
        }
    )
    logger.info(
        "Compressor entropy_budget finished: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, max_err_threshold=%.4f",
        len(self.compressed_layers),
        len(self.fallback_layers),
        avg_err,
        self.max_error,
    )
    return self.model, stats

Compress model using whitened entropy budget allocation and covariance-aligned singular vectors.

class EntropyRankAllocatorRegistered(DynamicEntropySVDCompressor):
    pass

Dynamic Entropy SVD Compressor.

  1. Computes singular entropy H(Sigma_l) on layer activation covariances or weights.
  2. Allocates layer ranks within [min_rank, max_rank] subject to parameter/rank budget.
  3. Replaces dense Linear/Conv1D layers with DynamicSVDLinear factorized modules.
  4. Provides relative Frobenius error validation, max_error threshold, and safe fallback.
  5. Freezes fallback and non-target layers during recovery.

@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("fwsvd_compressor")
class FWSVDCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    """
    Fisher-Weighted SVD Compressor.
    Projects weights using diagonal Fisher activation weights F_i = E[x_i^2].
    Replaces dense nn.Linear with SVDLinear.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        tokenizer: Any | None = None,
        rank: int = 64,
        max_error: float = 0.50,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        calibration_texts: list[str] | None = None,
        device: str | torch.device | None = None,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        target_attention_only: bool = True,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="fwsvd", **kwargs)
        cfg = kwargs.get("comp_config", kwargs.get("cfg"))
        if cfg is not None:
            rank = getattr(cfg, "fwsvd_rank", getattr(cfg, "rank", rank))
            tokenizer = getattr(cfg, "tokenizer", tokenizer)
            calibration_texts = getattr(cfg, "calibration_texts", calibration_texts)
            max_error = getattr(cfg, "fwsvd_max_error", getattr(cfg, "max_error", max_error))
            fallback_to_original = getattr(
                cfg,
                "fwsvd_fallback_to_original",
                getattr(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original),
            )
            target_attention_only = getattr(
                cfg,
                "fwsvd_target_attention_only",
                getattr(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only),
            )

        target_attention_only = kwargs.get(
            "target_attention_only",
            kwargs.get("fwsvd_target_attention_only", target_attention_only),
        )
        self.rank = rank
        self.max_error = max_error
        self.fallback_to_original = fallback_to_original
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.calibration_texts = calibration_texts or DEFAULT_CALIBRATION_CORPUS
        self.target_modules = target_modules
        self.target_attention_only = target_attention_only
        self.device = (
            torch.device(device) if device is not None else next(model.parameters()).device
        )
        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.layer_ranks: dict[str, int] = {}

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        if isinstance(module, SVDLinear):
            return False
        if any(
            name.endswith(sfx) for sfx in [".base_layer", ".lora_A", ".lora_B", ".lora_dropout"]
        ):
            return False

        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
        target_cls = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()

        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(module, nn.Linear)
            or isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
            or "conv1d" in cls_name
            or "conv1d" in target_cls
            or hasattr(module, "nf")
            or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            or "loralinear" in cls_name
            or hasattr(module, "base_layer")
        )
        if not is_linear_like:
            return False
        name_lower = name.lower()
        if any(
            skip in name_lower
            for skip in [
                "lm_head",
                "classifier",
                "head",
                "output_layer",
                "score",
                "pooler",
            ]
        ):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            ffn_patterns = [
                "ffn",
                "mlp",
                "lin1",
                "lin2",
                "intermediate",
                "gate_proj",
                "up_proj",
                "down_proj",
                "c_fc",
            ]
            if any(p in name_lower for p in ffn_patterns):
                return False
            if "output.dense" in name_lower and "attention" not in name_lower:
                return False
            attn_patterns = [
                "attn",
                "attention",
                "q_proj",
                "k_proj",
                "v_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "out_lin",
                "q_lin",
                "k_lin",
                "v_lin",
                "c_attn",
                "c_proj",
                "query",
                "key",
                "value",
            ]
            if not any(p in name_lower for p in attn_patterns):
                return False
        else:
            if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
                return False

        if self.target_modules is not None:
            return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)
        return True

    def compress_linear(
        self,
        layer: nn.Module,
        rank: int | None = None,
        fisher_diag: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[SVDLinear | None, float, int]:
        try:
            target_mod = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
            cls_name = layer.__class__.__name__.lower()
            target_cls = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = (
                "conv1d" in cls_name
                or "conv1d" in target_cls
                or hasattr(layer, "nf")
                or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            )
            weight_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "weight", getattr(layer, "weight", None))
            if weight_tensor is None:
                return None, float("inf"), 0

            raw_w = weight_tensor.data

            if is_conv1d:
                in_feat, out_feat = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
                W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
            else:
                out_feat, in_feat = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
                W = raw_w.float()

            r = get_dynamic_compression_rank(
                in_feat,
                out_feat,
                model_or_family=self.model,
                default_rank=rank or self.rank,
                comp_config=getattr(self, "comp_config", None),
            )
            max_allowed_rank = max(
                1,
                min(
                    in_feat,
                    out_feat,
                    int(0.75 * (in_feat * out_feat) / (in_feat + out_feat)),
                ),
            )
            r = min(r, max_allowed_rank)
            W_norm = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()

            sqrt_f: torch.Tensor | None = None
            inv_sqrt_f: torch.Tensor | None = None

            if fisher_diag is not None and fisher_diag.shape == (in_feat,):
                f_clamped = fisher_diag.to(device=layer.weight.device, dtype=torch.float32).clamp(
                    min=1e-8
                )
                sqrt_f = torch.sqrt(f_clamped)
                inv_sqrt_f = 1.0 / sqrt_f

            if sqrt_f is not None and inv_sqrt_f is not None:
                W_weighted = W * sqrt_f.unsqueeze(0)
            else:
                W_weighted = W

            W_weighted_dev = W_weighted.to(device=layer.weight.device, dtype=torch.float32)
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W_weighted_dev, full_matrices=False)

            sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
            U_r = U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
            V_r = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]

            if inv_sqrt_f is not None:
                V_r = V_r * inv_sqrt_f.unsqueeze(0)

            W_recon = torch.matmul(U_r, V_r)
            err = float(torch.norm(W - W_recon, p="fro").item() / max(W_norm, 1e-12))

            dtype = weight_tensor.dtype
            device = weight_tensor.device
            bias_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "bias", getattr(layer, "bias", None))

            svd_layer = SVDLinear(
                in_features=in_feat,
                out_features=out_feat,
                rank=r,
                bias=bias_tensor is not None,
                dtype=dtype,
                device=device,
                lora_r=getattr(self, "lora_r", 0),
                lora_alpha=getattr(self, "lora_alpha", 1.0),
            )
            svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(
                U_r.to(device=device, dtype=dtype),
                V_r.to(device=device, dtype=dtype),
            )
            if bias_tensor is not None and svd_layer.bias is not None:
                svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(bias_tensor.data)

            svd_layer.reconstruction_error = err
            svd_layer.rank = r
            return svd_layer, err, r
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("FWSVD factorization failed on linear: %s", e)
            return None, float("inf"), 0

    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        self.model.eval()
        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.layer_errors = {}
        self.layer_ranks = {}

        fishers: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}

        if self.tokenizer is None:
            logger.info("No tokenizer provided for FWSVD; proceeding with standard unweighted SVD.")
        else:
            hooks = []

            def make_hook(name: str):
                def hook(module: nn.Module, inp: tuple[torch.Tensor, ...], outp: Any):
                    if not inp or inp[0] is None:
                        return
                    x = inp[0].detach()
                    dev_type = x.device.type
                    autocast_ctx = (
                        torch.autocast(device_type=dev_type, enabled=False)
                        if hasattr(torch, "autocast") and dev_type in ("cuda", "cpu")
                        else contextlib.nullcontext()
                    )
                    with autocast_ctx:
                        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                        cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
                        target_cls = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
                        is_conv1d = (
                            "conv1d" in cls_name
                            or "conv1d" in target_cls
                            or hasattr(module, "nf")
                            or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
                        )
                        weight_tensor = getattr(
                            target_mod, "weight", getattr(module, "weight", None)
                        )
                        if weight_tensor is None:
                            return
                        in_f = weight_tensor.shape[0] if is_conv1d else weight_tensor.shape[1]
                        x_fp32 = x.float()
                        x_flat = x_fp32.reshape(-1, in_f)
                        mask = x_flat.abs().sum(dim=-1) > 1e-5
                        if mask.sum() == 0:
                            return
                        x_active = x_flat[mask]
                        batch_fisher = torch.mean(x_active**2, dim=0).cpu()

                    if name not in fishers:
                        fishers[name] = batch_fisher
                    else:
                        fishers[name] = 0.5 * (fishers[name] + batch_fisher)

                return hook

            for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
                if self._is_target_module(name, module):
                    hooks.append(module.register_forward_hook(make_hook(name)))

            try:
                inputs = self.tokenizer(
                    self.calibration_texts,
                    return_tensors="pt",
                    padding=True,
                    truncation=True,
                    max_length=512,
                )
                inputs = {k: v.to(self.device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
                with torch.no_grad():
                    self.model(**inputs)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("FWSVD calibration forward pass exception: %s", e)

            for h in hooks:
                h.remove()

        for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
            if self._is_target_module(name, module):
                f_diag = fishers.get(name)
                svd_layer, err, r = self.compress_linear(module, rank=self.rank, fisher_diag=f_diag)
                self.layer_errors[name] = err
                self.layer_ranks[name] = r

                if svd_layer is None or err > self.max_error:
                    if self.fallback_to_original:
                        logger.warning(
                            "FWSVD reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f. Skipping compression (fallback).",
                            err,
                            name,
                            self.max_error,
                        )
                        self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                        continue
                    else:
                        if svd_layer is None:
                            self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                            continue

                if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                    module.base_layer = svd_layer
                else:
                    replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)
                self.compressed_layers.append(name)

        stats = {
            "compressed_layers": len(self.compressed_layers),
            "fallback_layers": len(self.fallback_layers),
            "avg_reconstruction_error": (
                float(sum(self.layer_errors.values()) / max(1, len(self.layer_errors)))
                if self.layer_errors
                else 0.0
            ),
            "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
        }
        self.model.compression_stats = stats
        return self.model, stats

Fisher-Weighted SVD Compressor. Projects weights using diagonal Fisher activation weights F_i = E[x_i^2]. Replaces dense nn.Linear with SVDLinear.

Methods

def compress_linear(self,
layer: nn.Module,
rank: int | None = None,
fisher_diag: torch.Tensor | None = None) ‑> tuple[SVDLinear | None, float, int]
Expand source code 
def compress_linear(
    self,
    layer: nn.Module,
    rank: int | None = None,
    fisher_diag: torch.Tensor | None = None,
) -> tuple[SVDLinear | None, float, int]:
    try:
        target_mod = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
        cls_name = layer.__class__.__name__.lower()
        target_cls = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = (
            "conv1d" in cls_name
            or "conv1d" in target_cls
            or hasattr(layer, "nf")
            or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        )
        weight_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "weight", getattr(layer, "weight", None))
        if weight_tensor is None:
            return None, float("inf"), 0

        raw_w = weight_tensor.data

        if is_conv1d:
            in_feat, out_feat = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
        else:
            out_feat, in_feat = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.float()

        r = get_dynamic_compression_rank(
            in_feat,
            out_feat,
            model_or_family=self.model,
            default_rank=rank or self.rank,
            comp_config=getattr(self, "comp_config", None),
        )
        max_allowed_rank = max(
            1,
            min(
                in_feat,
                out_feat,
                int(0.75 * (in_feat * out_feat) / (in_feat + out_feat)),
            ),
        )
        r = min(r, max_allowed_rank)
        W_norm = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()

        sqrt_f: torch.Tensor | None = None
        inv_sqrt_f: torch.Tensor | None = None

        if fisher_diag is not None and fisher_diag.shape == (in_feat,):
            f_clamped = fisher_diag.to(device=layer.weight.device, dtype=torch.float32).clamp(
                min=1e-8
            )
            sqrt_f = torch.sqrt(f_clamped)
            inv_sqrt_f = 1.0 / sqrt_f

        if sqrt_f is not None and inv_sqrt_f is not None:
            W_weighted = W * sqrt_f.unsqueeze(0)
        else:
            W_weighted = W

        W_weighted_dev = W_weighted.to(device=layer.weight.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W_weighted_dev, full_matrices=False)

        sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
        U_r = U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
        V_r = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]

        if inv_sqrt_f is not None:
            V_r = V_r * inv_sqrt_f.unsqueeze(0)

        W_recon = torch.matmul(U_r, V_r)
        err = float(torch.norm(W - W_recon, p="fro").item() / max(W_norm, 1e-12))

        dtype = weight_tensor.dtype
        device = weight_tensor.device
        bias_tensor = getattr(target_mod, "bias", getattr(layer, "bias", None))

        svd_layer = SVDLinear(
            in_features=in_feat,
            out_features=out_feat,
            rank=r,
            bias=bias_tensor is not None,
            dtype=dtype,
            device=device,
            lora_r=getattr(self, "lora_r", 0),
            lora_alpha=getattr(self, "lora_alpha", 1.0),
        )
        svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(
            U_r.to(device=device, dtype=dtype),
            V_r.to(device=device, dtype=dtype),
        )
        if bias_tensor is not None and svd_layer.bias is not None:
            svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(bias_tensor.data)

        svd_layer.reconstruction_error = err
        svd_layer.rank = r
        return svd_layer, err, r
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("FWSVD factorization failed on linear: %s", e)
        return None, float("inf"), 0
