Expand source code 
class FusedQKVSVDLinear(nn.Module):
    """
    Compressed replacement for GPT-2's fused c_attn.

    Stores three independent low-rank factors (Q, K, V). Forward concatenates
    them so the output shape matches the original fused projection.

    Supports mean-shift bias absorption: if `_input_mean` is set, the input
    is centered before the low-rank projection and the shift is added back
    through the bias.
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        rank: int,
        bias: bool = True,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        if out_features % 3 != 0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"FusedQKVSVDLinear requires out_features divisible by 3, got {out_features}"
            )
        self.in_features = int(in_features)
        self.out_features = int(out_features)
        self.split_size = out_features // 3
        self.rank = int(rank)
        # Conv1D compatible
        self.nf = out_features
        self.nx = in_features
        self.lora_r = int(lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.scaling = (self.lora_alpha / self.lora_r) if self.lora_r > 0 else 1.0

        if device is None:
            device = torch.device("cpu")
        if dtype is None:
            dtype = torch.float32

        # Q, K, V factor pairs
        self.q_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.split_size, self.rank, dtype=dtype, device=device))
        self.q_B = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank, in_features, dtype=dtype, device=device))
        self.k_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.split_size, self.rank, dtype=dtype, device=device))
        self.k_B = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank, in_features, dtype=dtype, device=device))
        self.v_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.split_size, self.rank, dtype=dtype, device=device))
        self.v_B = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank, in_features, dtype=dtype, device=device))

        for A, B in ((self.q_A, self.q_B), (self.k_A, self.k_B), (self.v_A, self.v_B)):
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.zeros_(B)

        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(out_features, dtype=dtype, device=device))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if self.lora_r > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(self.lora_r, in_features, dtype=dtype, device=device)
            )
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(
                torch.zeros(out_features, self.lora_r, dtype=dtype, device=device)
            )
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        # Mean-shift buffer (persistent so it survives checkpoint save/load)
        self.register_buffer(
            "_input_mean",
            torch.zeros(in_features, dtype=torch.float32, device=device),
            persistent=True,
        )
        self._has_input_mean = False

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def set_fused_factors(self, W_fused: torch.Tensor) -> None:
        """Split a fused (in, out) or (out, in) weight into Q/K/V factors."""
        if W_fused.shape == (self.in_features, self.out_features):
            W = W_fused.t().contiguous().float()
        elif W_fused.shape == (self.out_features, self.in_features):
            W = W_fused.contiguous().float()
        else:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Unexpected fused weight shape {tuple(W_fused.shape)}; "
                f"expected ({self.in_features}, {self.out_features}) or "
                f"({self.out_features}, {self.in_features})"
            )

        S = self.split_size
        splits = [
            (W[:S, :], self.q_A, self.q_B),
            (W[S : 2 * S, :], self.k_A, self.k_B),
            (W[2 * S :, :], self.v_A, self.v_B),
        ]
        for W_part, A_param, B_param in splits:
            U, Svals, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W_part, full_matrices=False)
            r = min(self.rank, Svals.shape[0])
            sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(Svals[:r], min=1e-12))
            A_new = U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
            B_new = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]
            with torch.no_grad():
                A_param.data.zero_()
                B_param.data.zero_()
                A_param.data[:, :r].copy_(A_new.to(device=A_param.device, dtype=A_param.dtype))
                B_param.data[:r, :].copy_(B_new.to(device=B_param.device, dtype=B_param.dtype))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _forward_factor(x: torch.Tensor, A: torch.Tensor, B: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        h = F.linear(x.to(B.dtype), B)
        return F.linear(h, A)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        orig_dtype = x.dtype

        # Mean-shift centering
        if self._has_input_mean:
            mu = self._input_mean.to(x.device, x.dtype)
            x_c = x - mu
        else:
            x_c = x

        q = self._forward_factor(x_c, self.q_A, self.q_B)
        k = self._forward_factor(x_c, self.k_A, self.k_B)
        v = self._forward_factor(x_c, self.v_A, self.v_B)
        out = torch.cat([q, k, v], dim=-1)

        if self.bias is not None:
            out = out + self.bias.to(out.dtype)

        if self.lora_r > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            a = self.lora_A.to(x.dtype)
            b = self.lora_B.to(x.dtype)
            lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x, a), b) * self.scaling
            out = out + lora_out

        return out.to(orig_dtype)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def set_input_mean(self, mu: torch.Tensor) -> None:
        """Register the calibration activation mean (input centering)."""
        mu_f = mu.detach().float().to(self._input_mean.device)
        if mu_f.shape[0] != self.in_features:
            raise ValueError(f"input_mean shape {tuple(mu_f.shape)} != ({self.in_features},)")
        self._input_mean.copy_(mu_f)
        self._has_input_mean = True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Reconstruct the fused (in, out) Conv1D weight for compatibility."""
        W_q = self.q_A.float() @ self.q_B.float()
        W_k = self.k_A.float() @ self.k_B.float()
        W_v = self.v_A.float() @ self.v_B.float()
        W_linear = torch.cat([W_q, W_k, W_v], dim=0)
        return W_linear.t().contiguous()

    @weight.setter
    def weight(self, value: torch.Tensor) -> None:
        self.set_fused_factors(value)

Compressed replacement for GPT-2's fused c_attn.

Stores three independent low-rank factors (Q, K, V). Forward concatenates them so the output shape matches the original fused projection.

Supports mean-shift bias absorption: if _input_mean is set, the input is centered before the low-rank projection and the shift is added back through the bias.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

class FusedSVDLinear (in_features: int,

Expand source code 
class FusedSVDLinear(nn.Module):
    """
    RESEARCH SOTA: Fused SVD-Linear Module.
    Combines low-rank projection $Z = x V^T$, in-SRAM RMSNorm, and up-projection $Y = Z U^T + b$
    into a single kernel invocation, eliminating HBM roundtrips.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        rank: int = 32,
        bias: bool = True,
        use_rms_norm: bool = False,
        eps: float = 1e-6,
        dtype: torch.dtype = torch.float32,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features
        self.rank = rank
        self.eps = eps
        self.use_rms_norm = use_rms_norm

        # Low-rank factorized parameters
        self.V = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(rank, in_features, dtype=dtype, device=device))
        self.U = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features, rank, dtype=dtype, device=device))

        if use_rms_norm:
            self.norm_weight = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(rank, dtype=dtype, device=device))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("norm_weight", None)

        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features, dtype=dtype, device=device))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        self.reset_parameters()

    def reset_parameters(self) -> None:
        # Symmetrical square-root initialization
        nn.init.orthogonal_(self.U)
        nn.init.orthogonal_(self.V)
        if self.bias is not None:
            nn.init.zeros_(self.bias)
        if self.norm_weight is not None:
            nn.init.ones_(self.norm_weight)

    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Reconstructed effective weight matrix for compatibility."""
        return torch.matmul(self.U, self.V)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        # RESEARCH FIX: Dispatches fused kernel executing down-projection, RMSNorm, and up-projection
        return fused_svd_linear(
            x=x,
            V=self.V,
            U=self.U,
            norm_weight=self.norm_weight,
            bias=self.bias,
            eps=self.eps,
            use_rms_norm=self.use_rms_norm,
        )

    def set_low_rank_factors(self, U_factor: torch.Tensor, V_factor: torch.Tensor) -> None:
        """Set precomputed U and V low-rank factors."""
        with torch.no_grad():
            self.U.copy_(U_factor)
            self.V.copy_(V_factor)

    def decompose_from_weight(self, weight: torch.Tensor, bias: torch.Tensor | None = None) -> None:
        """Decompose dense weight matrix into symmetric SVD factors."""
        with torch.no_grad():
            W = weight.float()
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            r = min(self.rank, len(S))
            sqrt_s = torch.sqrt(S[:r])
            self.U.data.zero_()
            self.V.data.zero_()
            self.U.data[:, :r] = (U[:, :r] * sqrt_s).to(self.U.dtype)
            self.V.data[:r, :] = (sqrt_s.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]).to(self.V.dtype)
            if bias is not None and self.bias is not None:
                self.bias.data.copy_(bias)

    def get_orthogonality_loss(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        # RESEARCH FIX: Computes Stiefel manifold penalty to prevent singular subspace collapse.
        """
        u_gram = torch.matmul(self.U.T, self.U)
        v_gram = torch.matmul(self.V, self.V.T)
        eye_r = torch.eye(self.rank, device=self.U.device, dtype=self.U.dtype)
        loss_u = torch.norm(u_gram - eye_r, p="fro") ** 2
        loss_v = torch.norm(v_gram - eye_r, p="fro") ** 2
        return (loss_u + loss_v) / (self.rank * self.rank)

    def extra_repr(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"in_features={self.in_features}, out_features={self.out_features}, "
            f"rank={self.rank}, rms_norm={self.use_rms_norm}, bias={self.bias is not None}"
        )

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def get_orthogonality_loss(self) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def get_orthogonality_loss(self) -> torch.Tensor:
    """
    # RESEARCH FIX: Computes Stiefel manifold penalty to prevent singular subspace collapse.
    """
    u_gram = torch.matmul(self.U.T, self.U)
    v_gram = torch.matmul(self.V, self.V.T)
    eye_r = torch.eye(self.rank, device=self.U.device, dtype=self.U.dtype)
    loss_u = torch.norm(u_gram - eye_r, p="fro") ** 2
    loss_v = torch.norm(v_gram - eye_r, p="fro") ** 2
    return (loss_u + loss_v) / (self.rank * self.rank)

RESEARCH FIX: Computes Stiefel manifold penalty to prevent singular subspace collapse.

def reset_parameters(self) ‑> None
Expand source code 
def reset_parameters(self) -> None:
    # Symmetrical square-root initialization
    nn.init.orthogonal_(self.U)
    nn.init.orthogonal_(self.V)
    if self.bias is not None:
        nn.init.zeros_(self.bias)
    if self.norm_weight is not None:
        nn.init.ones_(self.norm_weight)

def set_low_rank_factors(self, U_factor: torch.Tensor, V_factor: torch.Tensor) -> None:
    """Set precomputed U and V low-rank factors."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        self.U.copy_(U_factor)
        self.V.copy_(V_factor)

Expand source code 
@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("gfws")
class GFWSCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    """
    Generalized Fisher / Covariance-Weighted SVD Compressor.
    Whitens weights using empirical input activation covariance square-root Cov(X)^{1/2},
    performs low-rank truncated SVD, and de-whitens to restore optimal compressed weights.

    Production hardening features:
    - Relative Frobenius reconstruction error validation with configurable max_error.
    - Automatic fallback to standard weight SVD if covariance whitening fails or error > max_error.
    - Safe fallback to original weights if even standard SVD exceeds max_error.
    - Robust Conv1D support (GPT-2 c_attn, c_proj) via weight transposition.
    - Unified critical-layer protection via should_compress_linear.
    - Full FP32 computation for numerical stability, casting back to original dtype only when assigning.
    - Trainable LoRA adapters (lora_A, lora_B) for parameter-efficient fine-tuning.
    - Per-layer logging and detailed compression statistics.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        rank: int = 64,
        damping: float = 0.01,
        max_error: float = 0.20,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        skip_attention_qkv: bool = True,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        tokenizer: Any | None = None,
        calibration_loader: Any | None = None,
        calibration_texts: list[str] | None = None,
        device: str | torch.device | None = None,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="gfws", **kwargs)
        cfg = kwargs.get("comp_config", kwargs.get("cfg"))
        if cfg is not None:
            rank = getattr(cfg, "gfws_rank", getattr(cfg, "rank", rank))
            damping = getattr(cfg, "gfws_damping", getattr(cfg, "damping", damping))
            max_error = getattr(cfg, "gfws_max_error", getattr(cfg, "max_error", max_error))
            fallback_to_original = getattr(
                cfg,
                "gfws_fallback_to_original",
                getattr(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original),
            )
            lora_r = getattr(cfg, "lora_r", getattr(cfg, "lora_rank", lora_r))
            lora_alpha = getattr(cfg, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha)
            skip_attention_qkv = getattr(cfg, "skip_attention_qkv", False)

        self.rank = int(rank)
        self.damping = float(damping)
        if max_error is None or max_error == 0.20:
            max_error = adaptive_max_error(self.rank, 768, 768, "distilbert")

        self.max_error = float(max_error)
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(fallback_to_original)
        self.lora_r = int(lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.skip_attention_qkv = bool(skip_attention_qkv)
        self.target_attention_only = target_attention_only
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer or getattr(model, "tokenizer", None)
        self.calibration_loader = calibration_loader
        self.calibration_texts = calibration_texts
        self.target_modules = target_modules
        self.target_device = (
            torch.device(device)
            if device is not None
            else next(model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
        )

        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.skipped_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.layer_ranks: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.used_covariance: dict[str, bool] = {}

    def _is_gqa_asymmetric(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        if not self.skip_attention_qkv:
            return False
        cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
        num_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_attention_heads", None)
        num_kv_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_key_value_heads", None)
        if num_heads is not None and num_kv_heads is not None and num_kv_heads < num_heads:
            name_lower = name.lower()
            if any(
                p in name_lower
                for p in [
                    "q_proj",
                    "k_proj",
                    "v_proj",
                    "query",
                    "key",
                    "value",
                    "c_attn",
                ]
            ):
                return True
        return False

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear = isinstance(module, nn.Linear)
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(module, "nf")
        if not (is_linear or is_conv1d):
            return False
        if isinstance(module, GFWSLoRALinear):
            return False
        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False
        if self._is_gqa_asymmetric(name, module):
            return False
        if self.target_attention_only and not any(
            k in name.lower()
            for k in [
                "attn",
                "attention",
                "q_proj",
                "k_proj",
                "v_proj",
                "out_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "q_lin",
                "k_lin",
                "v_lin",
                "out_lin",
                "query",
                "key",
                "value",
                "c_attn",
                "c_proj",
            ]
        ):
            return False
        if self.target_modules is not None:
            return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)
        return True

    def _collect_activation_covariances(self) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        """Collect empirical input activation covariances across target modules."""
        self.model.eval()
        running_cov: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        sample_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        hooks = []

        def make_hook(layer_name: str) -> Any:
            def hook(module: nn.Module, inp: tuple[torch.Tensor, ...], outp: Any) -> None:
                if len(inp) > 0 and isinstance(inp[0], torch.Tensor):
                    x = inp[0].detach().float()
                    cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
                    is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(module, "nf")
                    if is_conv1d:
                        in_f = module.weight.shape[0]
                    else:
                        in_f = module.weight.shape[1]
                    if x.ndim >= 2:
                        flat_x = x.reshape(-1, in_f)
                    else:
                        flat_x = x.unsqueeze(0)
                    n_samples = flat_x.shape[0]
                    if n_samples > 0:
                        batch_cov = torch.matmul(flat_x.T, flat_x).cpu()
                        if layer_name not in running_cov:
                            running_cov[layer_name] = batch_cov
                            sample_counts[layer_name] = n_samples
                        else:
                            running_cov[layer_name] += batch_cov
                            sample_counts[layer_name] += n_samples

            return hook

        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if self._is_target_module(name, module):
                hooks.append(module.register_forward_hook(make_hook(name)))

        calib_inputs = get_calibration_tensors(
            self.model,
            tokenizer=self.tokenizer,
            calibration_loader=self.calibration_loader,
            calibration_texts=self.calibration_texts,
            device=self.target_device,
        )

        with torch.no_grad():
            try:
                if isinstance(calib_inputs, dict):
                    self.model(**calib_inputs)
                elif isinstance(calib_inputs, torch.Tensor):
                    self.model(calib_inputs)
                elif isinstance(calib_inputs, (list, tuple)):
                    self.model(*calib_inputs)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("GFWS covariance collection forward pass exception: %s", e)

        for h in hooks:
            h.remove()

        covariances: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        for name, cov_sum in running_cov.items():
            n = float(max(sample_counts.get(name, 1), 1))
            covariances[name] = cov_sum / n

        return covariances

    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Compress layers via activation covariance whitening + low-rank SVD, with standard SVD fallback."""
        from neuralops.core.model_utils import replace_module

        covariances = self._collect_activation_covariances()
        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.skipped_layers = []
        self.layer_errors = {}
        self.layer_ranks = {}
        self.used_covariance = {}
        seen_weights: set[int] = set()

        target_layers = [
            (name, mod)
            for name, mod in self.model.named_modules()
            if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
        ]

        for name, module in target_layers:
            if not hasattr(module, "weight") or module.weight is None:
                continue

            w_id = id(module.weight)
            if w_id in seen_weights:
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                self.skipped_layers.append(name)
                continue
            seen_weights.add(w_id)

            cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(module, "nf")
            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            raw_w = target_mod.weight.data

            if is_conv1d:
                in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
                W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()  # [out_features, in_features]
            else:
                out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
                W = raw_w.float()  # [out_features, in_features]

            r = max(1, min(self.rank, out_f, in_f))
            device = target_mod.weight.device
            dtype = target_mod.weight.dtype
            W_norm = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()

            used_cov = False
            W_approx = None
            final_err = 1.0

            cov = covariances.get(name, None)
            if cov is not None and cov.shape == (in_f, in_f):
                try:
                    cov_dev = cov.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
                    diag_mean = torch.diagonal(cov_dev).mean().item()
                    if not math.isfinite(diag_mean) or diag_mean <= 0.0:
                        diag_mean = 1.0
                    lambda_reg = max(float(self.damping) * diag_mean, 1e-6)
                    cov_reg = cov_dev + lambda_reg * torch.eye(
                        in_f, device=device, dtype=torch.float32
                    )

                    eigvals, eigvecs = torch.linalg.eigh(cov_reg)
                    eigvals_clamped = torch.clamp(eigvals, min=1e-6)

                    # Cov(X)^{1/2} and Cov(X)^{-1/2}
                    cov_sqrt = eigvecs @ torch.diag(torch.sqrt(eigvals_clamped)) @ eigvecs.T
                    cov_inv_sqrt = (
                        eigvecs @ torch.diag(1.0 / torch.sqrt(eigvals_clamped)) @ eigvecs.T
                    )

                    # Whiten weights: M = W * Cov(X)^{1/2}
                    M = torch.matmul(W, cov_sqrt)
                    U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(M, full_matrices=False)

                    # Low-rank approximation of M: U_r * S_r * Vt_r
                    M_r = (U[:, :r] * S[:r].unsqueeze(0)) @ Vt[:r, :]

                    # De-whiten: candidate = M_r * Cov(X)^{-1/2}
                    candidate = torch.matmul(M_r, cov_inv_sqrt)

                    if not (torch.isnan(candidate).any() or torch.isinf(candidate).any()):
                        err = float(torch.norm(W - candidate, p="fro").item() / max(W_norm, 1e-12))
                        if err <= self.max_error:
                            W_approx = candidate
                            final_err = err
                            used_cov = True
                        else:
                            logger.debug(
                                "GFWS covariance de-whitened error (%.4f) > max_error (%.4f) on %s. Trying SVD fallback.",
                                err,
                                self.max_error,
                                name,
                            )
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug(
                        "GFWS covariance decomposition exception on layer %s: %s",
                        name,
                        e,
                    )

            # Fallback to standard SVD if covariance whitening was not used or failed
            if W_approx is None:
                try:
                    U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
                    candidate = (U[:, :r] * S[:r].unsqueeze(0)) @ Vt[:r, :]
                    if not (torch.isnan(candidate).any() or torch.isinf(candidate).any()):
                        err = float(torch.norm(W - candidate, p="fro").item() / max(W_norm, 1e-12))
                        W_approx = candidate
                        final_err = err
                        used_cov = False
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning("GFWS standard SVD failed on %s: %s", name, e)
                    W_approx = None
                    final_err = 1.0

            self.layer_errors[name] = final_err
            self.layer_ranks[name] = r
            self.used_covariance[name] = used_cov

            if W_approx is None or final_err > self.max_error:
                logger.warning(
                    "GFWS reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f.",
                    final_err,
                    name,
                    self.max_error,
                )
                if self.fallback_to_original:
                    logger.info("Skipping GFWS compression for %s (fallback to original).", name)
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                    continue

            # Replace with GFWSLoRALinear to support optional post-compression LoRA adapters
            new_layer = GFWSLoRALinear.from_linear(
                target_mod,
                weight=W_approx.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
                lora_r=self.lora_r,
                lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            )
            if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                if (
                    hasattr(module, "lora_A")
                    and hasattr(module, "lora_B")
                    and new_layer.lora_A is not None
                    and new_layer.lora_B is not None
                ):
                    with torch.no_grad():
                        src_A = (
                            module.lora_A.weight.data
                            if hasattr(module.lora_A, "weight")
                            else module.lora_A.data
                        )
                        src_B = (
                            module.lora_B.weight.data
                            if hasattr(module.lora_B, "weight")
                            else module.lora_B.data
                        )
                        if (
                            src_A.shape == new_layer.lora_A.shape
                            and src_B.shape == new_layer.lora_B.shape
                        ):
                            new_layer.lora_A.copy_(
                                src_A.to(
                                    new_layer.lora_A.device,
                                    dtype=new_layer.lora_A.dtype,
                                )
                            )
                            new_layer.lora_B.copy_(
                                src_B.to(
                                    new_layer.lora_B.device,
                                    dtype=new_layer.lora_B.dtype,
                                )
                            )
                module.base_layer = new_layer
                new_layer = module
            replace_module(self.model, name, new_layer)
            self.compressed_layers.append(name)

        # Post-compression forward sanity check
        forward_check_success = True
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                dev = next(self.model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
                dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 8), device=dev)
                try:
                    self.model(dummy)
                except Exception:
                    first_linear = next(
                        (
                            m
                            for m in self.model.modules()
                            if isinstance(m, (nn.Linear, GFWSLoRALinear))
                        ),
                        None,
                    )
                    in_dim = getattr(first_linear, "in_features", 8)
                    self.model(torch.randn(1, 8, in_dim, device=dev))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("GFWS post-compression forward check warning: %s", e)
            forward_check_success = False

        self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        avg_err = (
            sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
            / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
            if self.compressed_layers
            else 0.0
        )
        stats.update(
            {
                "rank": self.rank,
                "damping": self.damping,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
                "used_covariance": self.used_covariance,
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "lora_r": self.lora_r,
                "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "method": "covariance_weighted_svd",
            }
        )
        logger.info(
            "Compressor gfws finished: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, max_err_threshold=%.4f",
            len(self.compressed_layers),
            len(self.fallback_layers),
            avg_err,
            self.max_error,
        )
        return self.model, stats

Base class for all compressors. Not a nn.Module to avoid parameter storage.

var is_custom_attention : bool

Generalized Fisher / Covariance-Weighted SVD Compressor. Whitens weights using empirical input activation covariance square-root Cov(X)^{1/2}, performs low-rank truncated SVD, and de-whitens to restore optimal compressed weights.

Production hardening features: - Relative Frobenius reconstruction error validation with configurable max_error. - Automatic fallback to standard weight SVD if covariance whitening fails or error > max_error. - Safe fallback to original weights if even standard SVD exceeds max_error. - Robust Conv1D support (GPT-2 c_attn, c_proj) via weight transposition. - Unified critical-layer protection via should_compress_linear. - Full FP32 computation for numerical stability, casting back to original dtype only when assigning. - Trainable LoRA adapters (lora_A, lora_B) for parameter-efficient fine-tuning. - Per-layer logging and detailed compression statistics.

def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Compress layers via activation covariance whitening + low-rank SVD, with standard SVD fallback."""
    from neuralops.core.model_utils import replace_module

    covariances = self._collect_activation_covariances()
    self.compressed_layers = []
    self.fallback_layers = []
    self.skipped_layers = []
    self.layer_errors = {}
    self.layer_ranks = {}
    self.used_covariance = {}
    seen_weights: set[int] = set()

    target_layers = [
        (name, mod)
        for name, mod in self.model.named_modules()
        if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
    ]

    for name, module in target_layers:
        if not hasattr(module, "weight") or module.weight is None:
            continue

        w_id = id(module.weight)
        if w_id in seen_weights:
            for p in module.parameters():
                p.requires_grad = False
            self.skipped_layers.append(name)
            continue
        seen_weights.add(w_id)

        cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(module, "nf")
        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        raw_w = target_mod.weight.data

        if is_conv1d:
            in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()  # [out_features, in_features]
        else:
            out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.float()  # [out_features, in_features]

        r = max(1, min(self.rank, out_f, in_f))
        device = target_mod.weight.device
        dtype = target_mod.weight.dtype
        W_norm = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()

        used_cov = False
        W_approx = None
        final_err = 1.0

        cov = covariances.get(name, None)
        if cov is not None and cov.shape == (in_f, in_f):
            try:
                cov_dev = cov.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
                diag_mean = torch.diagonal(cov_dev).mean().item()
                if not math.isfinite(diag_mean) or diag_mean <= 0.0:
                    diag_mean = 1.0
                lambda_reg = max(float(self.damping) * diag_mean, 1e-6)
                cov_reg = cov_dev + lambda_reg * torch.eye(
                    in_f, device=device, dtype=torch.float32
                )

                eigvals, eigvecs = torch.linalg.eigh(cov_reg)
                eigvals_clamped = torch.clamp(eigvals, min=1e-6)

                # Cov(X)^{1/2} and Cov(X)^{-1/2}
                cov_sqrt = eigvecs @ torch.diag(torch.sqrt(eigvals_clamped)) @ eigvecs.T
                cov_inv_sqrt = (
                    eigvecs @ torch.diag(1.0 / torch.sqrt(eigvals_clamped)) @ eigvecs.T
                )

                # Whiten weights: M = W * Cov(X)^{1/2}
                M = torch.matmul(W, cov_sqrt)
                U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(M, full_matrices=False)

                # Low-rank approximation of M: U_r * S_r * Vt_r
                M_r = (U[:, :r] * S[:r].unsqueeze(0)) @ Vt[:r, :]

                # De-whiten: candidate = M_r * Cov(X)^{-1/2}
                candidate = torch.matmul(M_r, cov_inv_sqrt)

                if not (torch.isnan(candidate).any() or torch.isinf(candidate).any()):
                    err = float(torch.norm(W - candidate, p="fro").item() / max(W_norm, 1e-12))
                    if err <= self.max_error:
                        W_approx = candidate
                        final_err = err
                        used_cov = True
                    else:
                        logger.debug(
                            "GFWS covariance de-whitened error (%.4f) > max_error (%.4f) on %s. Trying SVD fallback.",
                            err,
                            self.max_error,
                            name,
                        )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug(
                    "GFWS covariance decomposition exception on layer %s: %s",
                    name,
                    e,
                )

        # Fallback to standard SVD if covariance whitening was not used or failed
        if W_approx is None:
            try:
                U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
                candidate = (U[:, :r] * S[:r].unsqueeze(0)) @ Vt[:r, :]
                if not (torch.isnan(candidate).any() or torch.isinf(candidate).any()):
                    err = float(torch.norm(W - candidate, p="fro").item() / max(W_norm, 1e-12))
                    W_approx = candidate
                    final_err = err
                    used_cov = False
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("GFWS standard SVD failed on %s: %s", name, e)
                W_approx = None
                final_err = 1.0

        self.layer_errors[name] = final_err
        self.layer_ranks[name] = r
        self.used_covariance[name] = used_cov

        if W_approx is None or final_err > self.max_error:
            logger.warning(
                "GFWS reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f.",
                final_err,
                name,
                self.max_error,
            )
            if self.fallback_to_original:
                logger.info("Skipping GFWS compression for %s (fallback to original).", name)
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                continue

        # Replace with GFWSLoRALinear to support optional post-compression LoRA adapters
        new_layer = GFWSLoRALinear.from_linear(
            target_mod,
            weight=W_approx.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
            lora_r=self.lora_r,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
        )
        if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
            if (
                hasattr(module, "lora_A")
                and hasattr(module, "lora_B")
                and new_layer.lora_A is not None
                and new_layer.lora_B is not None
            ):
                with torch.no_grad():
                    src_A = (
                        module.lora_A.weight.data
                        if hasattr(module.lora_A, "weight")
                        else module.lora_A.data
                    )
                    src_B = (
                        module.lora_B.weight.data
                        if hasattr(module.lora_B, "weight")
                        else module.lora_B.data
                    )
                    if (
                        src_A.shape == new_layer.lora_A.shape
                        and src_B.shape == new_layer.lora_B.shape
                    ):
                        new_layer.lora_A.copy_(
                            src_A.to(
                                new_layer.lora_A.device,
                                dtype=new_layer.lora_A.dtype,
                            )
                        )
                        new_layer.lora_B.copy_(
                            src_B.to(
                                new_layer.lora_B.device,
                                dtype=new_layer.lora_B.dtype,
                            )
                        )
            module.base_layer = new_layer
            new_layer = module
        replace_module(self.model, name, new_layer)
        self.compressed_layers.append(name)

    # Post-compression forward sanity check
    forward_check_success = True
    try:
        with torch.no_grad():
            dev = next(self.model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
            dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 8), device=dev)
            try:
                self.model(dummy)
            except Exception:
                first_linear = next(
                    (
                        m
                        for m in self.model.modules()
                        if isinstance(m, (nn.Linear, GFWSLoRALinear))
                    ),
                    None,
                )
                in_dim = getattr(first_linear, "in_features", 8)
                self.model(torch.randn(1, 8, in_dim, device=dev))
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("GFWS post-compression forward check warning: %s", e)
        forward_check_success = False

    self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
    stats = self.get_compression_stats()
    avg_err = (
        sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
        / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
        if self.compressed_layers
        else 0.0
    )
    stats.update(
        {
            "rank": self.rank,
            "damping": self.damping,
            "max_error": self.max_error,
            "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
            "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
            "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
            "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
            "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
            "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
            "used_covariance": self.used_covariance,
            "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
            "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
            "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
            "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
            "lora_r": self.lora_r,
            "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
            "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
            "method": "covariance_weighted_svd",
        }
    )
    logger.info(
        "Compressor gfws finished: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, max_err_threshold=%.4f",
        len(self.compressed_layers),
        len(self.fallback_layers),
        avg_err,
        self.max_error,
    )
    return self.model, stats

Compress layers via activation covariance whitening + low-rank SVD, with standard SVD fallback.

class GFWSLoRALinear(nn.Module):
    """
    Linear layer containing Fisher/Covariance-whitened SVD compressed base weights (frozen)
    with optional trainable LoRA adapters (lora_A, lora_B).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        weight: torch.Tensor,
        bias: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features
        self.nf = out_features  # Conv1D compatibility
        self.lora_r = int(lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.scaling = (self.lora_alpha / self.lora_r) if self.lora_r > 0 else 1.0

        target_device = device if device is not None else weight.device
        target_dtype = dtype if dtype is not None else weight.dtype

        self.weight = nn.Parameter(
            weight.to(device=target_device, dtype=target_dtype),
            requires_grad=False,
        )
        if bias is not None:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(
                bias.to(device=target_device, dtype=target_dtype),
                requires_grad=False,
            )
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if self.lora_r > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(
                    (self.lora_r, in_features),
                    dtype=torch.float32,
                    device=target_device,
                )
            )
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(
                torch.zeros(
                    (out_features, self.lora_r),
                    dtype=torch.float32,
                    device=target_device,
                )
            )
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

    @classmethod
    def from_linear(
        cls,
        linear: nn.Module,
        weight: torch.Tensor,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
    ) -> GFWSLoRALinear:
        bias = linear.bias.detach() if hasattr(linear, "bias") and linear.bias is not None else None
        in_features = getattr(linear, "in_features", weight.shape[1])
        out_features = getattr(linear, "out_features", weight.shape[0])
        return cls(
            in_features=in_features,
            out_features=out_features,
            weight=weight,
            bias=bias,
            lora_r=lora_r,
            lora_alpha=lora_alpha,
            device=weight.device,
            dtype=weight.dtype,
        )

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        orig_shape = x.shape
        x_flat = x.reshape(-1, self.in_features)
        out = F.linear(x_flat, self.weight, self.bias)

        if self.lora_r > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x_flat.float(), self.lora_A), self.lora_B)
            out = out + (self.scaling * lora_out).to(out.dtype)

        return out.reshape(*orig_shape[:-1], self.out_features)

Linear layer containing Fisher/Covariance-whitened SVD compressed base weights (frozen) with optional trainable LoRA adapters (lora_A, lora_B).

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    orig_shape = x.shape
    x_flat = x.reshape(-1, self.in_features)
    out = F.linear(x_flat, self.weight, self.bias)

    if self.lora_r > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
        lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x_flat.float(), self.lora_A), self.lora_B)
        out = out + (self.scaling * lora_out).to(out.dtype)

    return out.reshape(*orig_shape[:-1], self.out_features)

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("hybrid_svd_layer")
class HybridSVDCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    """
    Weight-based SVD compressor for Linear / Conv1D layers.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        svd_rank: int = 128,
        use_full_layer_replacement: bool = False,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        max_error: float = 0.85,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        cfg: Any | None = None,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="hybrid_svd", **kwargs)
        cfg = cfg or kwargs.get("comp_config")
        self.cfg = cfg

        self.svd_rank = int(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "hybrid_svd_rank",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "svd_rank", _cfg_get(cfg, "rank", svd_rank)),
            )
        )
        self.use_full_layer_replacement = bool(
            _cfg_get(cfg, "use_full_layer_replacement", use_full_layer_replacement)
        )
        self.lora_r = int(_cfg_get(cfg, "lora_r", _cfg_get(cfg, "lora_rank", lora_r)))
        self.lora_alpha = float(_cfg_get(cfg, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha))
        self.max_error = float(
            _cfg_get(cfg, "hybrid_svd_max_error", _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error))
        )
        self.target_attention_only = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "hybrid_svd_target_attention_only",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only),
            )
        )
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "hybrid_svd_fallback_to_original",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original),
            )
        )
        self.target_modules = target_modules or _cfg_get(cfg, "target_modules", None)

        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.skipped_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self._compressed_params: int = 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        name_lower = name.lower()
        if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
            return False
        if any(
            skip in name_lower
            for skip in [
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embedding",
                "router",
                "gate",
            ]
        ):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            if not any(attn_kw in name_lower for attn_kw in _ATTN_NAMES):
                return False

        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        if not (is_linear or is_conv1d) or isinstance(target_mod, SVDLinear):
            return False
        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False
        if self.target_modules is not None:
            return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)
        return True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Decompose linear layers via SVD and replace with SVDLinear."""
        replaced_count = 0
        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.skipped_layers = []
        self.layer_errors = {}
        seen_weights: set = set()

        total_orig_params = 0
        total_comp_params = 0

        # Freeze non-target layers if attention-only.
        if self.target_attention_only:
            for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
                if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
                    continue
                target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
                is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
                is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
                if (is_linear or is_conv1d) and not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    self.skipped_layers.append(name)

        # Fallback: linear-layer SVD.
        for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
            if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                continue

            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            if not hasattr(target_mod, "weight") or target_mod.weight is None:
                continue

            w_id = id(target_mod.weight)
            if w_id in seen_weights:
                self.skipped_layers.append(name)
                continue
            seen_weights.add(w_id)

            raw_w = target_mod.weight.data
            cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.float()
            out_f, in_f = W.shape
            orig_params = in_f * out_f
            total_orig_params += orig_params

            max_rank = min(in_f, out_f)
            bound_r = (in_f * out_f) // (in_f + out_f)
            if max_rank < 16 or bound_r < 4:
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                total_comp_params += orig_params
                continue

            r = min(self.svd_rank, bound_r, max_rank)

            try:
                U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
                for escalation_step in range(4):
                    sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
                    U_r = (U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)).to(
                        dtype=raw_w.dtype, device=raw_w.device
                    )
                    V_r = (sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]).to(
                        dtype=raw_w.dtype, device=raw_w.device
                    )

                    W_recon = torch.matmul(U[:, :r] * S[:r].unsqueeze(0), Vt[:r, :])
                    err = (
                        torch.norm(W - W_recon, p="fro") / torch.norm(W, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
                    ).item()
                    self.layer_errors[name] = float(err)

                    if err > self.max_error and r < min(bound_r, max_rank):
                        r_new = min(int(r * 1.5), bound_r, max_rank)
                        if r_new > r:
                            logger.info(
                                "HybridSVD: %s err=%.4f at rank=%d, escalating to rank=%d",
                                name,
                                err,
                                r,
                                r_new,
                            )
                            r = r_new
                            continue

                    if err > self.max_error:
                        logger.warning(
                            "HybridSVD: layer %s reconstruction error %.4f > max_error %.4f (final rank=%d)",
                            name,
                            err,
                            self.max_error,
                            r,
                        )
                        if self.fallback_to_original:
                            self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                            for p in module.parameters():
                                p.requires_grad = False
                            total_comp_params += orig_params
                            break
                    break

                if name in self.fallback_layers:
                    continue

                # Detect existing LoRA rank.
                existing_lora_A = getattr(module, "lora_A", None)
                existing_lora_B = getattr(module, "lora_B", None)
                effective_lora_r = self.lora_r
                if (
                    existing_lora_A is not None
                    and hasattr(existing_lora_A, "weight")
                    and hasattr(existing_lora_A.weight, "shape")
                ):
                    try:
                        effective_lora_r = int(existing_lora_A.weight.shape[0])
                    except (AttributeError, IndexError, TypeError):
                        pass

                svd_layer = SVDLinear(
                    in_f,
                    out_f,
                    r,
                    bias=(getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None),
                    dtype=raw_w.dtype,
                    device=raw_w.device,
                    lora_r=effective_lora_r,
                    lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                )
                svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(U_r, V_r)
                if getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(target_mod.bias.data)

                if (
                    existing_lora_A is not None
                    and existing_lora_B is not None
                    and getattr(svd_layer, "lora_A", None) is not None
                ):
                    w_A = getattr(existing_lora_A, "weight", existing_lora_A)
                    w_B = getattr(existing_lora_B, "weight", existing_lora_B)
                    if hasattr(w_A, "data") and hasattr(w_B, "data"):
                        try:
                            svd_layer.lora_A.data.copy_(w_A.data)
                            svd_layer.lora_B.data.copy_(w_B.data)
                            svd_layer.lora_A.requires_grad = True
                            svd_layer.lora_B.requires_grad = True
                        except Exception as e:
                            logger.debug("LoRA copy failed for %s: %s", name, e)

                replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)
                replaced_count += 1
                self.compressed_layers.append(name)

                # FIX: use actual svd_layer.rank for parameter accounting.
                actual_rank = int(getattr(svd_layer, "rank", r))
                comp_params = actual_rank * (in_f + out_f)
                if getattr(svd_layer, "lora_r", 0) > 0:
                    comp_params += int(svd_layer.lora_r) * (in_f + out_f)
                total_comp_params += comp_params

            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("HybridSVD compression exception on %s: %s", name, e)
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                total_comp_params += orig_params

        # Post-compression forward sanity check (single attempt).
        forward_check_success = True
        try:
            self.model.eval()
            with torch.no_grad():
                first_p = next(self.model.parameters(), None)
                dev = first_p.device if first_p is not None else torch.device("cpu")
                cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
                vocab_size = getattr(cfg, "vocab_size", 1000)
                max_tok = min(100, vocab_size - 1) if vocab_size > 10 else 2
                min_tok = min(2, max_tok)
                dummy = torch.randint(min_tok, max_tok + 1, (1, 8), device=dev, dtype=torch.long)
                attn_mask = torch.ones_like(dummy)
                try:
                    self.model(input_ids=dummy, attention_mask=attn_mask)
                except Exception:
                    # Try hidden-states form.
                    first_linear = next(
                        (m for m in self.model.modules() if isinstance(m, (nn.Linear, SVDLinear))),
                        None,
                    )
                    in_dim = getattr(first_linear, "in_features", getattr(cfg, "hidden_size", 8))
                    dummy_float = torch.randn(
                        1,
                        8,
                        in_dim,
                        device=dev,
                        dtype=first_p.dtype if first_p is not None else torch.float32,
                    )
                    self.model(dummy_float)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("HybridSVD post-compression forward check warning: %s", e)
            forward_check_success = False

        effective_ratio = (total_comp_params / total_orig_params) if total_orig_params > 0 else 1.0

        try:
            self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        except Exception:
            self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        avg_err = (
            (sum(self.layer_errors.values()) / max(1, len(self.layer_errors)))
            if self.layer_errors
            else 0.0
        )
        stats.update(
            {
                "replaced_layers_count": replaced_count,
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "svd_rank": self.svd_rank,
                "lora_r": self.lora_r,
                "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "effective_parameter_ratio": round(effective_ratio, 4),
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "method": "hybrid_svd",
            }
        )
        return self.model, stats

Weight-based SVD compressor for Linear / Conv1D layers.

def compress(self) ‑> tuple[torch.nn.modules.module.Module, dict[str, typing.Any]]
Expand source code 
def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Decompose linear layers via SVD and replace with SVDLinear."""
    replaced_count = 0
    self.compressed_layers = []
    self.fallback_layers = []
    self.skipped_layers = []
    self.layer_errors = {}
    seen_weights: set = set()

    total_orig_params = 0
    total_comp_params = 0

    # Freeze non-target layers if attention-only.
    if self.target_attention_only:
        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
                continue
            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            if (is_linear or is_conv1d) and not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                self.skipped_layers.append(name)

    # Fallback: linear-layer SVD.
    for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
        if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
            continue

        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        if not hasattr(target_mod, "weight") or target_mod.weight is None:
            continue

        w_id = id(target_mod.weight)
        if w_id in seen_weights:
            self.skipped_layers.append(name)
            continue
        seen_weights.add(w_id)

        raw_w = target_mod.weight.data
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.float()
        out_f, in_f = W.shape
        orig_params = in_f * out_f
        total_orig_params += orig_params

        max_rank = min(in_f, out_f)
        bound_r = (in_f * out_f) // (in_f + out_f)
        if max_rank < 16 or bound_r < 4:
            self.fallback_layers.append(name)
            for p in module.parameters():
                p.requires_grad = False
            total_comp_params += orig_params
            continue

        r = min(self.svd_rank, bound_r, max_rank)

        try:
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            for escalation_step in range(4):
                sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
                U_r = (U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)).to(
                    dtype=raw_w.dtype, device=raw_w.device
                )
                V_r = (sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]).to(
                    dtype=raw_w.dtype, device=raw_w.device
                )

                W_recon = torch.matmul(U[:, :r] * S[:r].unsqueeze(0), Vt[:r, :])
                err = (
                    torch.norm(W - W_recon, p="fro") / torch.norm(W, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
                ).item()
                self.layer_errors[name] = float(err)

                if err > self.max_error and r < min(bound_r, max_rank):
                    r_new = min(int(r * 1.5), bound_r, max_rank)
                    if r_new > r:
                        logger.info(
                            "HybridSVD: %s err=%.4f at rank=%d, escalating to rank=%d",
                            name,
                            err,
                            r,
                            r_new,
                        )
                        r = r_new
                        continue

                if err > self.max_error:
                    logger.warning(
                        "HybridSVD: layer %s reconstruction error %.4f > max_error %.4f (final rank=%d)",
                        name,
                        err,
                        self.max_error,
                        r,
                    )
                    if self.fallback_to_original:
                        self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                        for p in module.parameters():
                            p.requires_grad = False
                        total_comp_params += orig_params
                        break
                break

            if name in self.fallback_layers:
                continue

            # Detect existing LoRA rank.
            existing_lora_A = getattr(module, "lora_A", None)
            existing_lora_B = getattr(module, "lora_B", None)
            effective_lora_r = self.lora_r
            if (
                existing_lora_A is not None
                and hasattr(existing_lora_A, "weight")
                and hasattr(existing_lora_A.weight, "shape")
            ):
                try:
                    effective_lora_r = int(existing_lora_A.weight.shape[0])
                except (AttributeError, IndexError, TypeError):
                    pass

            svd_layer = SVDLinear(
                in_f,
                out_f,
                r,
                bias=(getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None),
                dtype=raw_w.dtype,
                device=raw_w.device,
                lora_r=effective_lora_r,
                lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            )
            svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(U_r, V_r)
            if getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(target_mod.bias.data)

            if (
                existing_lora_A is not None
                and existing_lora_B is not None
                and getattr(svd_layer, "lora_A", None) is not None
            ):
                w_A = getattr(existing_lora_A, "weight", existing_lora_A)
                w_B = getattr(existing_lora_B, "weight", existing_lora_B)
                if hasattr(w_A, "data") and hasattr(w_B, "data"):
                    try:
                        svd_layer.lora_A.data.copy_(w_A.data)
                        svd_layer.lora_B.data.copy_(w_B.data)
                        svd_layer.lora_A.requires_grad = True
                        svd_layer.lora_B.requires_grad = True
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.debug("LoRA copy failed for %s: %s", name, e)

            replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)
            replaced_count += 1
            self.compressed_layers.append(name)

            # FIX: use actual svd_layer.rank for parameter accounting.
            actual_rank = int(getattr(svd_layer, "rank", r))
            comp_params = actual_rank * (in_f + out_f)
            if getattr(svd_layer, "lora_r", 0) > 0:
                comp_params += int(svd_layer.lora_r) * (in_f + out_f)
            total_comp_params += comp_params

        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("HybridSVD compression exception on %s: %s", name, e)
            self.fallback_layers.append(name)
            for p in module.parameters():
                p.requires_grad = False
            total_comp_params += orig_params

    # Post-compression forward sanity check (single attempt).
    forward_check_success = True
    try:
        self.model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            first_p = next(self.model.parameters(), None)
            dev = first_p.device if first_p is not None else torch.device("cpu")
            cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
            vocab_size = getattr(cfg, "vocab_size", 1000)
            max_tok = min(100, vocab_size - 1) if vocab_size > 10 else 2
            min_tok = min(2, max_tok)
            dummy = torch.randint(min_tok, max_tok + 1, (1, 8), device=dev, dtype=torch.long)
            attn_mask = torch.ones_like(dummy)
            try:
                self.model(input_ids=dummy, attention_mask=attn_mask)
            except Exception:
                # Try hidden-states form.
                first_linear = next(
                    (m for m in self.model.modules() if isinstance(m, (nn.Linear, SVDLinear))),
                    None,
                )
                in_dim = getattr(first_linear, "in_features", getattr(cfg, "hidden_size", 8))
                dummy_float = torch.randn(
                    1,
                    8,
                    in_dim,
                    device=dev,
                    dtype=first_p.dtype if first_p is not None else torch.float32,
                )
                self.model(dummy_float)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("HybridSVD post-compression forward check warning: %s", e)
        forward_check_success = False

    effective_ratio = (total_comp_params / total_orig_params) if total_orig_params > 0 else 1.0

    try:
        self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
    except Exception:
        self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

    stats = self.get_compression_stats()
    avg_err = (
        (sum(self.layer_errors.values()) / max(1, len(self.layer_errors)))
        if self.layer_errors
        else 0.0
    )
    stats.update(
        {
            "replaced_layers_count": replaced_count,
            "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
            "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
            "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
            "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
            "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
            "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
            "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
            "svd_rank": self.svd_rank,
            "lora_r": self.lora_r,
            "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
            "max_error": self.max_error,
            "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
            "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
            "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
            "effective_parameter_ratio": round(effective_ratio, 4),
            "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
            "method": "hybrid_svd",
        }
    )
    return self.model, stats

class OptimalSubspaceLoRALinear(nn.Module):
    """
    Frozen optimal-subspace-projected base weight + optional trainable LoRA.
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = False  # OSP-FIX-01

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        weight: torch.Tensor,
        bias: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features
        self.nf = out_features
        self.nx = in_features
        self.lora_r = int(lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.scaling = (self.lora_alpha / self.lora_r) if self.lora_r > 0 else 1.0

        target_device = device if device is not None else weight.device
        target_dtype = dtype if dtype is not None else weight.dtype

        # Normalize to (out, in)
        if (
            weight.shape[0] == in_features
            and weight.shape[1] == out_features
            and in_features != out_features
        ):
            weight = weight.t().contiguous()

        self.weight = nn.Parameter(
            weight.to(device=target_device, dtype=target_dtype),
            requires_grad=False,
        )
        if bias is not None:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(
                bias.to(device=target_device, dtype=target_dtype),
                requires_grad=False,
            )
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if self.lora_r > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(
                    (self.lora_r, in_features),
                    dtype=torch.float32,
                    device=target_device,
                )
            )
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(
                torch.zeros(
                    (out_features, self.lora_r),
                    dtype=torch.float32,
                    device=target_device,
                )
            )
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

    @classmethod
    def from_linear(
        cls,
        linear: nn.Module,
        weight: torch.Tensor,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
    ) -> OptimalSubspaceLoRALinear:
        base_layer = getattr(linear, "base_layer", linear)
        bias = (
            base_layer.bias.detach()
            if hasattr(base_layer, "bias") and base_layer.bias is not None
            else None
        )
        in_features = getattr(base_layer, "in_features", getattr(base_layer, "nx", None))
        out_features = getattr(base_layer, "out_features", getattr(base_layer, "nf", None))
        if in_features is None or out_features is None:
            out_features = out_features or weight.shape[0]
            in_features = in_features or weight.shape[1]

        existing_lora_r = getattr(linear, "r", getattr(linear, "lora_r", 0))
        eff_lora_r = existing_lora_r if existing_lora_r > 0 else lora_r
        eff_lora_alpha = getattr(linear, "lora_alpha", getattr(linear, "alpha", lora_alpha))

        layer = cls(
            in_features=in_features,
            out_features=out_features,
            weight=weight,
            bias=bias,
            lora_r=eff_lora_r,
            lora_alpha=eff_lora_alpha,
            device=weight.device,
            dtype=weight.dtype,
        )

        # Preserve existing LoRA weights if present
        if (
            hasattr(linear, "lora_A")
            and hasattr(linear, "lora_B")
            and layer.lora_A is not None
            and layer.lora_B is not None
        ):
            with torch.no_grad():
                src_A = (
                    linear.lora_A.weight.data
                    if hasattr(linear.lora_A, "weight")
                    else linear.lora_A.data
                )
                src_B = (
                    linear.lora_B.weight.data
                    if hasattr(linear.lora_B, "weight")
                    else linear.lora_B.data
                )
                if src_A.shape == layer.lora_A.shape and src_B.shape == layer.lora_B.shape:
                    layer.lora_A.copy_(src_A.to(layer.lora_A.device, dtype=layer.lora_A.dtype))
                    layer.lora_B.copy_(src_B.to(layer.lora_B.device, dtype=layer.lora_B.dtype))
            layer.lora_A.requires_grad = True
            layer.lora_B.requires_grad = True

        return layer

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        out = F.linear(x, self.weight, self.bias)
        if self.lora_r > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            out = out + self.scaling * F.linear(F.linear(x.float(), self.lora_A), self.lora_B).to(
                out.dtype
            )
        return out

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    out = F.linear(x, self.weight, self.bias)
    if self.lora_r > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.
