@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("optimal_subspace_projection")
class OptimalSubspaceProjectionCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    """
    Optimal Subspace Projection Compressor via activation-aware randomized SVD.
    """

    # OSP-FIX-01: this is a compressor, not attention.
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        calibration_loader: Any | None = None,
        rank_ratio: float = 0.5,
        power_iters: int = 2,
        tokenizer: Any | None = None,
        calibration_texts: list[str] | None = None,
        device: str | torch.device | None = None,
        max_error: float = 0.50,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        skip_attention_qkv: bool = True,
        min_rank: int = 4,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="optimal_subspace_projection", **kwargs)
        cfg = kwargs.get("comp_config", kwargs.get("cfg"))

        if cfg is not None:
            rank_ratio = _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "optimal_subspace_rank_ratio",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "rank_ratio", rank_ratio),
            )
            power_iters = _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "optimal_subspace_power_iters",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "power_iters", power_iters),
            )
            max_error = _cfg_get(
                cfg, "optimal_subspace_max_error", _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error)
            )
            fallback_to_original = _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "optimal_subspace_fallback_to_original",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original),
            )
            lora_r = _cfg_get(cfg, "lora_r", lora_r)
            lora_alpha = _cfg_get(cfg, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha)
            skip_attention_qkv = _cfg_get(cfg, "skip_attention_qkv", skip_attention_qkv)
            target_attention_only = _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only)
            min_rank = _cfg_get(cfg, "min_rank", min_rank)

        self.calibration_loader = calibration_loader
        self.rank_ratio = max(0.01, min(float(rank_ratio), 1.0))
        self.power_iters = max(0, int(power_iters))
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer or getattr(model, "tokenizer", None)
        self.calibration_texts = calibration_texts
        self.max_error = float(max_error)
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(fallback_to_original)
        self.target_attention_only = bool(target_attention_only)
        self.target_modules = target_modules
        self.lora_r = int(lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.skip_attention_qkv = bool(skip_attention_qkv)
        self.min_rank = max(1, int(min_rank))

        self.target_device = (
            torch.device(device)
            if device is not None
            else next(model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
        )
        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.skipped_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.layer_ranks: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.stats: dict[str, Any] = {}

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _is_gqa_asymmetric(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        if not self.skip_attention_qkv:
            return False
        cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
        num_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_attention_heads", None)
        num_kv_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_key_value_heads", None)
        if num_heads is not None and num_kv_heads is not None and num_kv_heads < num_heads:
            name_lower = name.lower()
            if any(
                p in name_lower
                for p in (
                    "q_proj",
                    "k_proj",
                    "v_proj",
                    "query",
                    "key",
                    "value",
                    "c_attn",
                )
            ):
                return True
        return False

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        # OSP-FIX-07: skip already-compressed modules.
        if isinstance(module, OptimalSubspaceLoRALinear):
            return False
        # Skip nested lora params.
        if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ("lora_A", "lora_B")):
            return False

        cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(module, nn.Linear)
            or "conv1d" in cls_name
            or hasattr(module, "nf")
            or hasattr(module, "base_layer")
        )
        if not is_linear_like:
            return False
        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False
        if self._is_gqa_asymmetric(name, module):
            return False
        if self.target_attention_only:
            if not any(k in name.lower() for k in _ATTN_NAMES):
                return False
        if self.target_modules is None:
            return True
        return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _compute_rank(d_out: int, d_in: int, rank_ratio: float, min_rank: int) -> int:
        """OSP-FIX-04: clean, correct rank formula."""
        max_rank = min(d_out, d_in)
        target = max(1, int(round(max_rank * rank_ratio)))
        rank = max(target, min_rank)
        rank = min(rank, max_rank)
        return rank

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress(self, **kwargs: Any) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # 1. Collect activation covariances (dedup by base weight id).
        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        act_covs: dict[int, torch.Tensor] = {}
        weight_id_to_scan_id: dict[int, int] = {}  # base weight id → cov key
        hooks = []

        def hook_fn(module: nn.Module, inputs: tuple[torch.Tensor, ...], output: Any) -> None:
            if not inputs or not isinstance(inputs[0], torch.Tensor):
                return
            base = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            w = getattr(base, "weight", None)
            if w is None:
                return
            w_id = id(w)
            key = weight_id_to_scan_id.get(w_id, w_id)

            x = inputs[0].detach()
            x_flat = x.reshape(-1, x.shape[-1]).float()
            cov = torch.matmul(x_flat.T, x_flat) / max(x_flat.shape[0], 1)
            if key not in act_covs:
                act_covs[key] = cov.cpu()
            else:
                act_covs[key] = act_covs[key] + cov.cpu()

        # OSP-FIX-06: register one hook per unique base weight.
        registered_ids: set[int] = set()
        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                continue
            base = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            w = getattr(base, "weight", None)
            if w is None:
                continue
            w_id = id(w)
            if w_id in registered_ids:
                continue
            registered_ids.add(w_id)
            weight_id_to_scan_id[w_id] = w_id
            hooks.append(module.register_forward_hook(hook_fn))

        calib_inputs = get_calibration_tensors(
            self.model,
            tokenizer=self.tokenizer,
            calibration_loader=self.calibration_loader,
            calibration_texts=self.calibration_texts,
            device=self.target_device,
        )

        with torch.no_grad():
            try:
                if isinstance(calib_inputs, dict):
                    self.model(**calib_inputs)
                elif isinstance(calib_inputs, torch.Tensor):
                    self.model(calib_inputs)
                elif isinstance(calib_inputs, (list, tuple)):
                    self.model(*calib_inputs)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("OSP activation collection failed: %s", e)

        for h in hooks:
            h.remove()

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # 2. Compress each target layer.
        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.skipped_layers = []
        self.layer_errors = {}
        self.layer_ranks = {}
        seen_weights: set[int] = set()
        total_orig_params = 0
        total_comp_params = 0

        for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
            if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                continue

            base_module = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            if not hasattr(base_module, "weight") or base_module.weight is None:
                continue

            w_id = id(base_module.weight)
            if w_id in seen_weights:
                logger.info("OSP: skipping %s (shared/tied weight)", name)
                self.skipped_layers.append(name)
                continue
            seen_weights.add(w_id)

            # OSP-FIX-03: count only BASE parameters.
            base_param_count = sum(p.numel() for p in base_module.parameters())
            total_orig_params += base_param_count

            raw_w = base_module.weight.data
            cls_name = base_module.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_module, "nf")

            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().clone().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.clone().float()
            d_out, d_in = W.shape
            device = raw_w.device
            dtype = raw_w.dtype

            # ---------------- Cholesky preconditioner ----------------
            C = act_covs.get(w_id)
            L = torch.eye(d_in, device=device)
            L_inv = torch.eye(d_in, device=device)

            if C is not None and C.shape[-1] == d_in:
                try:
                    C = C.to(device).float()
                    diag_mean = torch.diagonal(C).mean().item()
                    reg = max(1e-4 * max(diag_mean, 0.0), 1e-5)
                    C_reg = C + reg * torch.eye(d_in, device=device)
                    L = torch.linalg.cholesky(C_reg)
                    L_inv = torch.linalg.inv(L)
                    if not torch.isfinite(L).all() or not torch.isfinite(L_inv).all():
                        raise ValueError("Cholesky produced non-finite values")
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("OSP: Cholesky fallback for %s: %s", name, e)
                    L = torch.eye(d_in, device=device)
                    L_inv = torch.eye(d_in, device=device)

            # ---------------- Randomized subspace iteration --------
            M = torch.matmul(W, L)
            rank = self._compute_rank(d_out, d_in, self.rank_ratio, self.min_rank)

            # OSP-FIX-09: Omega on the same device as M.
            Omega = torch.randn(M.shape[1], rank, device=M.device, dtype=M.dtype)
            Y = torch.matmul(M, Omega)
            for _ in range(self.power_iters):
                Y = torch.matmul(M, torch.matmul(M.T, Y))

            try:
                Q, _ = torch.linalg.qr(Y)
                B = torch.matmul(Q.T, M)
                U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(B, full_matrices=False)
                r = min(rank, len(S))

                # OSP-FIX-10: empty rank → fallback.
                if r < 1:
                    raise ValueError("Empty SVD rank")

                U_r = U[:, :r]
                S_r = S[:r]
                Vt_r = Vt[:r, :]
                new_U = torch.matmul(Q, U_r)  # (d_out, r)
                new_V = torch.matmul(Vt_r, L_inv)  # (r, d_in)
                new_weight = torch.matmul(new_U * S_r.unsqueeze(0), new_V)  # (d_out, d_in)
                new_weight = torch.nan_to_num(new_weight, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

                # ---------------- Error check ----------------
                w_norm = torch.norm(W, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-12).item()
                err = (torch.norm(W - new_weight, p="fro") / w_norm).item()
                self.layer_errors[name] = float(err)
                self.layer_ranks[name] = r

                if not math.isfinite(err) or err > self.max_error:
                    logger.warning(
                        "OSP: reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded %.4f.",
                        err,
                        name,
                        self.max_error,
                    )
                    if self.fallback_to_original:
                        self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                        total_comp_params += base_param_count  # OSP-FIX-08
                        for p in module.parameters():
                            p.requires_grad = False
                        continue

                # ---------------- Commit ----------------
                W_target = new_weight.to(dtype=dtype, device=device)  # OSP-FIX-11
                compressed_layer = OptimalSubspaceLoRALinear.from_linear(
                    module,
                    weight=W_target,
                    lora_r=self.lora_r,
                    lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                )

                # OSP-FIX-02: preserve LoRA wrapper.
                if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                    module.base_layer = compressed_layer
                else:
                    replace_module(self.model, name, compressed_layer)

                self.compressed_layers.append(name)
                total_comp_params += sum(p.numel() for p in compressed_layer.parameters())

            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("OSP: SVD/decomposition failed on %s: %s", name, e)
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                self.layer_errors[name] = 1.0
                total_comp_params += base_param_count
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # 3. Forward sanity check.
        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        forward_check_success = True
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                dev = next(self.model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
                dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 8), device=dev, dtype=torch.long)
                # OSP-FIX-05: no `.float()` on int64 input ids.
                try:
                    self.model(input_ids=dummy)
                except Exception:
                    try:
                        self.model(dummy)
                    except Exception:
                        # Try embedding-level call as last resort.
                        cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
                        hidden = getattr(cfg, "hidden_size", None) if cfg else None
                        if hidden is None:
                            first_lin = next(
                                (m for m in self.model.modules() if isinstance(m, nn.Linear)),
                                None,
                            )
                            hidden = getattr(first_lin, "in_features", 8)
                        dummy_feat = torch.randn(
                            1,
                            8,
                            hidden,
                            device=dev,
                            dtype=next(self.model.parameters()).dtype,
                        )
                        self.model(dummy_feat)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("OSP forward sanity check failed: %s", e)
            forward_check_success = False

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # 4. Stats.
        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        avg_err = (
            sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
            / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
            if self.compressed_layers
            else 0.0
        )
        eff_ratio = total_comp_params / max(1, total_orig_params) if total_orig_params > 0 else 1.0
        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        stats.update(
            {
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "rank_ratio": self.rank_ratio,
                "power_iters": self.power_iters,
                "lora_r": self.lora_r,
                "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
                "effective_parameter_ratio": round(eff_ratio, 4),
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "method": "optimal_subspace_projection",
            }
        )
        self.stats = stats
        return self.model, stats

class ResSVDCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    """
    Spectral Residual LoRA (Res-SVD) Compressor.

    Replaces standard Linear layers with ResSVDLinear modules, truncating base weights
    at rank r (frozen) and initializing adapter matrices A, B directly from the residual error tail.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        base_rank: int = 32,
        residual_rank: int = 8,
        scaling: float = 1.0,
        factorize_base: bool = True,
        freeze_base: bool = True,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="res_svd", **kwargs)
        cfg = kwargs.get("comp_config", kwargs.get("cfg"))
        if cfg is not None:
            base_rank = getattr(cfg, "base_rank", getattr(cfg, "rank", base_rank))
            residual_rank = getattr(cfg, "residual_rank", residual_rank)
            scaling = getattr(cfg, "scaling", getattr(cfg, "lora_alpha", scaling))
            factorize_base = getattr(cfg, "factorize_base", factorize_base)
            freeze_base = getattr(cfg, "freeze_base", freeze_base)

        if "rank" in kwargs and kwargs["rank"] is not None:
            base_rank = kwargs["rank"]

        self.base_rank = base_rank
        self.residual_rank = residual_rank
        self.scaling = scaling
        self.factorize_base = factorize_base
        self.freeze_base = freeze_base
        self.target_modules = target_modules
        self.replaced_layers: list[str] = []

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        """Check whether module is an eligible nn.Linear layer matching filter."""
        if not isinstance(module, nn.Linear):
            return False
        if isinstance(module, ResSVDLinear):
            return False
        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False
        if self.target_modules is None:
            return True
        return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)

    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Compress target linear layers into ResSVDLinear modules.

        Returns:
            Tuple[nn.Module, Dict[str, Any]]: Compressed model and compression statistics.
        """
        self.replaced_layers = []

        # Find target linear layers
        target_layers = [
            (name, mod)
            for name, mod in self.model.named_modules()
            if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
        ]

        if not target_layers:
            logger.warning("No compressible linear layers found in model.")
            return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

        for name, old_linear in target_layers:
            res_layer = ResSVDLinear.from_linear(
                linear=old_linear,
                base_rank=self.base_rank,
                residual_rank=self.residual_rank,
                scaling=self.scaling,
                factorize_base=self.factorize_base,
                freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            )
            replace_module(self.model, name, res_layer)
            self.replaced_layers.append(name)

        # Calculate statistics
        total_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        trainable_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters() if p.requires_grad)
        frozen_params = total_params - trainable_params

        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        stats["trainable_parameters"] = trainable_params
        stats["frozen_parameters"] = frozen_params
        stats["trainable_percentage"] = (
            (trainable_params / total_params * 100.0) if total_params > 0 else 0.0
        )
        stats["base_rank"] = self.base_rank
        stats["residual_rank"] = self.residual_rank
        stats["replaced_layers"] = self.replaced_layers

        return self.model, stats

def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Compress target linear layers into ResSVDLinear modules.

    Returns:

            linear=old_linear,

class ResSVDLinear(nn.Module):
    """
    Spectral Residual LoRA (Res-SVD) Linear Layer.

    Executes forward pass as:
    y = x @ W_base^T + scaling * (x @ A^T) @ B^T + bias
    where W_base is either factorized as (base_V, base_U) with requires_grad=False,
    and (lora_A, lora_B) are trainable adapter matrices initialized from the residual tail.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        base_rank: int = 32,
        residual_rank: int = 8,
        scaling: float = 1.0,
        factorize_base: bool = True,
        bias: bool = True,
        dtype: torch.dtype = torch.float32,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features
        max_possible_rank = min(in_features, out_features)

        # Bounds validation
        self.base_rank = max(1, min(base_rank, max_possible_rank))
        remaining_rank = max(0, max_possible_rank - self.base_rank)
        self.residual_rank = max(0, min(residual_rank, remaining_rank))
        self.scaling = float(scaling)
        self.factorize_base = factorize_base

        # Base parameters (Frozen)
        if self.factorize_base:
            self.base_V = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(self.base_rank, in_features, dtype=dtype, device=device),
                requires_grad=False,
            )
            self.base_U = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(out_features, self.base_rank, dtype=dtype, device=device),
                requires_grad=False,
            )
        else:
            self.base_weight = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(out_features, in_features, dtype=dtype, device=device),
                requires_grad=False,
            )

        # Trainable Residual LoRA Adapters
        if self.residual_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(self.residual_rank, in_features, dtype=dtype, device=device),
                requires_grad=True,
            )
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(out_features, self.residual_rank, dtype=dtype, device=device),
                requires_grad=True,
            )
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features, dtype=dtype, device=device))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        self.reset_parameters()

    def reset_parameters(self) -> None:
        """Kaiming uniform parameter initialization for base and zero for adapters."""
        if self.factorize_base:
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.base_V, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.base_U, a=math.sqrt(5))
        else:
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.base_weight, a=math.sqrt(5))

        if self.residual_rank > 0:
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)

        if self.bias is not None:
            fan_in = self.in_features
            bound = 1 / math.sqrt(fan_in) if fan_in > 0 else 0
            nn.init.uniform_(self.bias, -bound, bound)

    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Reconstructed effective weight matrix for compatibility."""
        if self.factorize_base:
            base = torch.matmul(self.base_U, self.base_V)
        else:
            base = self.base_weight
        if self.residual_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            res = self.scaling * torch.matmul(self.lora_B, self.lora_A)
            return base + res
        return base

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Forward execution:
        y = Base(x) + scaling * (x @ A^T) @ B^T + bias
        """
        # RESEARCH FIX: Two-stage factorized forward for frozen base to minimize memory footprint
        if self.factorize_base:
            base_intermediate = F.linear(x, self.base_V)
            base_out = F.linear(base_intermediate, self.base_U)
        else:
            base_out = F.linear(x, self.base_weight)

        # Trainable spectral residual LoRA adapter forward
        if self.residual_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            res_intermediate = F.linear(x, self.lora_A)
            res_out = self.scaling * F.linear(res_intermediate, self.lora_B)
            out = base_out + res_out
        else:
            out = base_out

        if self.bias is not None:
            out = out + self.bias

        return out

    @classmethod
    def from_linear(
        cls,
        linear: nn.Linear,
        base_rank: int = 32,
        residual_rank: int = 8,
        scaling: float = 1.0,
        factorize_base: bool = True,
        freeze_base: bool = True,
    ) -> ResSVDLinear:
        """
        Construct ResSVDLinear by decomposing a pretrained nn.Linear weight:
        1. Base factors U_r, V_r from the dominant top-r singular components.
        2. Residual adapter factors A, B directly initialized from the residual tail (r+1 to r+k).

        Args:
            linear: Source dense linear layer.
            base_rank: Rank r for the frozen base matrix.
            residual_rank: Rank k for the trainable residual adapter.
            scaling: Multiplier alpha / k for adapter output.
            factorize_base: Whether to store base weights as factorized U, V matrices.
            freeze_base: Whether to freeze base factors (requires_grad=False).

        Returns:
            ResSVDLinear: Spectral residual LoRA module.
        """
        in_f = linear.in_features
        out_f = linear.out_features
        max_rank = min(in_f, out_f)

        r = max(1, min(base_rank, max_rank))
        rem = max(0, max_rank - r)
        k = max(0, min(residual_rank, rem))

        new_layer = cls(
            in_features=in_f,
            out_features=out_f,
            base_rank=r,
            residual_rank=k,
            scaling=scaling,
            factorize_base=factorize_base,
            bias=linear.bias is not None,
            dtype=linear.weight.dtype,
            device=linear.weight.device,
        )

        W = linear.weight.data.float()  # (out_f, in_f)

        # SVD decomposition of source weight
        U, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)

        # 1. Base Truncation (Dominant Top-r Singular Components)
        U_base = U[:, :r] * torch.sqrt(S[:r]).unsqueeze(0)  # (out_f, r)
        V_base = torch.sqrt(S[:r]).unsqueeze(1) * Vh[:r, :]  # (r, in_f)

        if factorize_base:
            new_layer.base_U.data.copy_(U_base.to(dtype=linear.weight.dtype))
            new_layer.base_V.data.copy_(V_base.to(dtype=linear.weight.dtype))
            new_layer.base_U.requires_grad = not freeze_base
            new_layer.base_V.requires_grad = not freeze_base
        else:
            W_base = (U[:, :r] * S[:r].unsqueeze(0)) @ Vh[:r, :]
            new_layer.base_weight.data.copy_(W_base.to(dtype=linear.weight.dtype))
            new_layer.base_weight.requires_grad = not freeze_base

        # 2. Residual Tail Initialization (Singular directions r to r+k)
        # RESEARCH FIX: Analytical spectral initialization of LoRA adapter weights (A, B) directly
        # from the residual tail E = W - U_r V_r minimizes initial reconstruction loss to zero-shot optimality.
        if k > 0:
            S_tail = S[r : r + k]
            U_tail = U[:, r : r + k]
            Vh_tail = Vh[r : r + k, :]

            # Scale S_tail by adapter scaling factor gamma: (B @ A) * gamma = E_tail
            scale_factor = max(1e-8, float(scaling))
            S_tail_scaled = S_tail / scale_factor

            sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S_tail_scaled, min=0.0))
            lora_A_init = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vh_tail  # (k, in_f)
            lora_B_init = U_tail * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)  # (out_f, k)

            new_layer.lora_A.data.copy_(lora_A_init.to(dtype=linear.weight.dtype))
            new_layer.lora_B.data.copy_(lora_B_init.to(dtype=linear.weight.dtype))
            new_layer.lora_A.requires_grad = True
            new_layer.lora_B.requires_grad = True

        # Copy bias if present
        if linear.bias is not None:
            new_layer.bias.data.copy_(linear.bias.data)

        return new_layer

    def merge_weights(self) -> nn.Linear:
        """
        Merge frozen base weights and trainable residual adapter weights into a dense nn.Linear.

        Returns:
            nn.Linear: Standard dense linear layer with merged weights.
        """
        device = next(self.parameters()).device
        dtype = self.base_U.dtype if self.factorize_base else self.base_weight.dtype

        merged_linear = nn.Linear(
            self.in_features,
            self.out_features,
            bias=self.bias is not None,
            device=device,
            dtype=dtype,
        )

        with torch.no_grad():
            if self.factorize_base:
                W_base = self.base_U.float() @ self.base_V.float()
            else:
                W_base = self.base_weight.float()

            if self.residual_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
                delta_W = self.scaling * (self.lora_B.float() @ self.lora_A.float())
                W_total = W_base + delta_W
            else:
                W_total = W_base

            merged_linear.weight.data.copy_(W_total.to(dtype=dtype))
            if self.bias is not None:
                merged_linear.bias.data.copy_(self.bias.data)

        return merged_linear

    def get_residual_error_norm(self, original_weight: torch.Tensor | None = None) -> float:
        """
        Calculate relative Frobenius norm reconstruction error:
        || W_orig - (W_base + scaling * B @ A) ||_F / || W_orig ||_F
        """
        with torch.no_grad():
            if self.factorize_base:
                W_approx = self.base_U.float() @ self.base_V.float()
            else:
                W_approx = self.base_weight.float()

            if self.residual_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
                W_approx = W_approx + self.scaling * (self.lora_B.float() @ self.lora_A.float())

            if original_weight is not None:
                W_orig = original_weight.float().to(W_approx.device)
                error = torch.norm(W_orig - W_approx, p="fro") / (
                    torch.norm(W_orig, p="fro") + 1e-12
                )
                return float(error.item())

            return 0.0

    def extra_repr(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"in_features={self.in_features}, out_features={self.out_features}, "
            f"base_rank={self.base_rank}, residual_rank={self.residual_rank}, "
            f"scaling={self.scaling}, factorize_base={self.factorize_base}"
        )

Spectral Residual LoRA (Res-SVD) Linear Layer.

Executes forward pass as: y = x @ W_base^T + scaling * (x @ A^T) @ B^T + bias where W_base is either factorized as (base_V, base_U) with requires_grad=False, and (lora_A, lora_B) are trainable adapter matrices initialized from the residual tail.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

Static methods

def from_linear(linear: nn.Linear,
base_rank: int = 32,
residual_rank: int = 8,
scaling: float = 1.0,
factorize_base: bool = True,
freeze_base: bool = True) ‑> ResSVDLinear

Construct ResSVDLinear by decomposing a pretrained nn.Linear weight: 1. Base factors U_r, V_r from the dominant top-r singular components. 2. Residual adapter factors A, B directly initialized from the residual tail (r+1 to r+k).

Args
-----=
linear
Source dense linear layer.
base_rank
Rank r for the frozen base matrix.
residual_rank
Rank k for the trainable residual adapter.
scaling
Multiplier alpha / k for adapter output.
factorize_base
Whether to store base weights as factorized U, V matrices.
freeze_base
Whether to freeze base factors (requires_grad=False).
Returns
-----=
ResSVDLinear
Spectral residual LoRA module.

Instance variables

prop weight : torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
@property
def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Reconstructed effective weight matrix for compatibility."""
    if self.factorize_base:
        base = torch.matmul(self.base_U, self.base_V)
    else:
        base = self.base_weight
    if self.residual_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
        res = self.scaling * torch.matmul(self.lora_B, self.lora_A)
        return base + res
    return base

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    """
    Forward execution:
    y = Base(x) + scaling * (x @ A^T) @ B^T + bias
    """
    # RESEARCH FIX: Two-stage factorized forward for frozen base to minimize memory footprint
    if self.factorize_base:
        base_intermediate = F.linear(x, self.base_V)
        base_out = F.linear(base_intermediate, self.base_U)
    else:
        base_out = F.linear(x, self.base_weight)

    # Trainable spectral residual LoRA adapter forward
    if self.residual_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
        res_intermediate = F.linear(x, self.lora_A)
        res_out = self.scaling * F.linear(res_intermediate, self.lora_B)
        out = base_out + res_out
    else:
        out = base_out

    if self.bias is not None:
        out = out + self.bias

    return out

Forward execution: y = Base(x) + scaling * (x @ A^T) @ B^T + bias

def get_residual_error_norm(self, original_weight: torch.Tensor | None = None) ‑> float
Expand source code 
def get_residual_error_norm(self, original_weight: torch.Tensor | None = None) -> float:
    """
    Calculate relative Frobenius norm reconstruction error:
    || W_orig - (W_base + scaling * B @ A) ||_F / || W_orig ||_F
    """
    with torch.no_grad():
        if self.factorize_base:
            W_approx = self.base_U.float() @ self.base_V.float()
        else:
            W_approx = self.base_weight.float()

        if self.residual_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            W_approx = W_approx + self.scaling * (self.lora_B.float() @ self.lora_A.float())

        if original_weight is not None:
            W_orig = original_weight.float().to(W_approx.device)
            error = torch.norm(W_orig - W_approx, p="fro") / (
                torch.norm(W_orig, p="fro") + 1e-12
            )
            return float(error.item())

        return 0.0

Calculate relative Frobenius norm reconstruction error: || W_orig - (W_base + scaling * B @ A) ||_F / || W_orig ||_F

def merge_weights(self) ‑> torch.nn.modules.linear.Linear
Expand source code 
def merge_weights(self) -> nn.Linear:
    """
    Merge frozen base weights and trainable residual adapter weights into a dense nn.Linear.

    Returns:
        nn.Linear: Standard dense linear layer with merged weights.
    """
    device = next(self.parameters()).device
    dtype = self.base_U.dtype if self.factorize_base else self.base_weight.dtype

    merged_linear = nn.Linear(
        self.in_features,
        self.out_features,
        bias=self.bias is not None,
        device=device,
        dtype=dtype,
    )

    with torch.no_grad():
        if self.factorize_base:
            W_base = self.base_U.float() @ self.base_V.float()
        else:
            W_base = self.base_weight.float()

        if self.residual_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            delta_W = self.scaling * (self.lora_B.float() @ self.lora_A.float())
            W_total = W_base + delta_W
        else:
            W_total = W_base

        merged_linear.weight.data.copy_(W_total.to(dtype=dtype))
        if self.bias is not None:
            merged_linear.bias.data.copy_(self.bias.data)

    return merged_linear

Merge frozen base weights and trainable residual adapter weights into a dense nn.Linear.

Returns
-----=
nn.Linear
Standard dense linear layer with merged weights.
def reset_parameters(self) ‑> None
Expand source code 
def reset_parameters(self) -> None:
    """Kaiming uniform parameter initialization for base and zero for adapters."""
    if self.factorize_base:
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.base_V, a=math.sqrt(5))
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.base_U, a=math.sqrt(5))
    else:
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.base_weight, a=math.sqrt(5))

    if self.residual_rank > 0:
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
        nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)

    if self.bias is not None:
        fan_in = self.in_features
        bound = 1 / math.sqrt(fan_in) if fan_in > 0 else 0
        nn.init.uniform_(self.bias, -bound, bound)

Kaiming uniform parameter initialization for base and zero for adapters.

@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("residual_svd_compressor")
class ResidualSVDCompressor(ResSVDCompressor):
    pass

Spectral Residual LoRA (Res-SVD) Compressor.

Replaces standard Linear layers with ResSVDLinear modules, truncating base weights at rank r (frozen) and initializing adapter matrices A, B directly from the residual error tail.

Expand source code 
@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("saes_svd_v2")
class SAESSVDCompressorV2(BaseCompressor):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    """
    SAES-SVD v2 Compressor with iterative spectrum-aware residual correction,
    Frobenius validation, and safe fallback.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        rank: int | None = None,
        rank_ratio: float = 0.5,
        correcting_steps: int = 2,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        max_error: float = 0.60,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,  # Fix 1: Default changed to True
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="saes_svd_v2", **kwargs)
        cfg = kwargs.get("comp_config", kwargs.get("cfg"))
        if cfg is not None:
            rank = _cfg_get(cfg, "saes_rank", _cfg_get(cfg, "rank", rank))
            rank_ratio = _cfg_get(cfg, "saes_rank_ratio", _cfg_get(cfg, "rank_ratio", rank_ratio))
            correcting_steps = _cfg_get(cfg, "correcting_steps", correcting_steps)
            max_error = _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error)
            fallback_to_original = _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original)
            target_attention_only = _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only)

        if "rank" in kwargs and kwargs["rank"] is not None:
            rank = kwargs["rank"]
        if "fallback_to_original" in kwargs:
            fallback_to_original = bool(kwargs["fallback_to_original"])

        self.rank = rank
        self.rank_ratio = float(rank_ratio)
        self.correcting_steps = int(correcting_steps)
        self.target_modules = target_modules
        self.target_attention_only = bool(target_attention_only)
        self.max_error = float(max_error)
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(fallback_to_original)
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.layer_ranks: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.replaced_layers: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        """
        Fix 6: Unified critical-layer protection delegated to should_compress_linear.
        """
        name_l = name.lower()
        if self.target_attention_only:
            attn_keywords = [
                "attn",
                "attention",
                "q_proj",
                "k_proj",
                "v_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "c_attn",
                "c_proj",
                "query",
                "key",
                "value",
            ]
            if not any(k in name_l for k in attn_keywords):
                return False

        base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        # Fix 3: Reliable Conv1D check using class name and hasattr(nf)
        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(base_mod, nn.Linear) or "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
        )
        if not is_linear_like:
            return False
        if isinstance(base_mod, SVDLinear):
            return False
        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False
        if self.target_modules is None:
            return True
        return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)

    def compress_linear(
        self,
        layer: nn.Module,
        rank: int | None = None,
        name: str = "",
    ) -> SVDLinear | None:
        """Decompose a single linear or Conv1D layer with spectrum-aware error suppression."""
        base_layer = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
        raw_w = base_layer.weight.data
        cls_name = base_layer.__class__.__name__.lower()
        # Fix 3: Conv1D detection by class name or nf attribute
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_layer, "nf")

        if is_conv1d:
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
            out_f = W.shape[0]
            in_f = W.shape[1]
        else:
            W = raw_w.float()
            out_f = getattr(base_layer, "out_features", W.shape[0])
            in_f = getattr(base_layer, "in_features", W.shape[1])

        max_r = min(out_f, in_f)
        # Cap rank to guarantee parameter reduction
        param_cap = max(1, (in_f * out_f) // (in_f + out_f) - 1)
        effective_max_r = min(max_r, param_cap)

        if rank is not None:
            r = max(1, min(rank, effective_max_r))
        else:
            r = max(1, min(int(round(max_r * self.rank_ratio)), effective_max_r))

        device = base_layer.weight.device
        dtype = base_layer.weight.dtype

        # Compute full SVD in FP32
        try:
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("SAES-SVD SVD failure for %s: %s", name, e)
            return None

        # Initial symmetric square-root singular value allocation
        sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
        A = U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)  # [out_features, r]
        B = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]  # [r, in_features]

        # Iterative residual error suppression
        eps_eye = 1e-6 * torch.eye(r, device=W.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        for _ in range(self.correcting_steps):
            E = W - A @ B
            # Update A towards residual gradient
            BBt = B @ B.t().contiguous() + eps_eye
            A = A + E @ B.t().contiguous() @ torch.linalg.pinv(BBt)

            # Update B towards residual gradient
            AtA = A.t().contiguous() @ A + eps_eye
            B = B + torch.linalg.pinv(AtA) @ A.t().contiguous() @ (W - A @ B)

        # Compute relative Frobenius reconstruction error
        W_rec = A @ B
        norm_orig = torch.norm(W, p="fro")
        rel_err = (torch.norm(W - W_rec, p="fro") / (norm_orig + 1e-8)).item()

        # Fix 2: Always log a warning when reconstruction error exceeds max_error
        if rel_err > self.max_error:
            logger.warning(
                "SAES-SVD reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f%s",
                rel_err,
                name,
                self.max_error,
                (
                    ". Keeping original layer."
                    if self.fallback_to_original
                    else ". Proceeding with compressed layer."
                ),
            )
            if self.fallback_to_original:
                return None

        has_bias = getattr(base_layer, "bias", None) is not None
        has_lora = hasattr(layer, "lora_A") and hasattr(layer, "lora_B")
        lora_r = getattr(layer, "r", 0) if has_lora else 0

        svd_layer = SVDLinear(
            in_features=in_f,
            out_features=out_f,
            rank=r,
            bias=has_bias,
            dtype=dtype,
            device=device,
            lora_r=lora_r,
        )
        svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(
            U_factor=A.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
            V_factor=B.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
        )
        # Fix 5: Ensure bias is copied correctly for both Linear and Conv1D
        if has_bias and svd_layer.bias is not None:
            svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(base_layer.bias.data.to(dtype=dtype, device=device))

        if has_lora and svd_layer.use_lora:
            try:
                if hasattr(layer.lora_A, "weight"):
                    svd_layer.lora_A.weight.data.copy_(layer.lora_A.weight.data)
                if hasattr(layer.lora_B, "weight"):
                    svd_layer.lora_B.weight.data.copy_(layer.lora_B.weight.data)
            except Exception as e_lora:
                logger.warning("Could not preserve LoRA adapters in SAES-SVD: %s", e_lora)

        # Store metadata on module
        svd_layer.reconstruction_error = rel_err
        svd_layer.rank = r
        return svd_layer

    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Compress all linear and Conv1D layers in the model."""
        self.layer_errors = {}
        self.layer_ranks = {}
        self.replaced_layers = []

        target_layers = [
            (name, mod)
            for name, mod in self.model.named_modules()
            if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
        ]

        if not target_layers:
            logger.warning("SAES-SVD v2: No compressible linear layers found.")
            return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

        for name, module in target_layers:
            svd_layer = self.compress_linear(module, rank=self.rank, name=name)
            if svd_layer is not None:
                err = getattr(svd_layer, "reconstruction_error", 0.0)
                r = getattr(svd_layer, "rank", self.rank)
                self.layer_errors[name] = err
                self.layer_ranks[name] = r
                # Fix 4: Include per-layer rank and error in returned stats
                self.replaced_layers.append({"name": name, "rank": r, "error": err})
                replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)
            else:
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False

        self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        # Fix 4 & Fix 6: Detailed per-layer stats, errors, ranks, and fallback status
        stats.update(
            {
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
                "replaced_layers": self.replaced_layers,
                "compressed_layers": len(self.replaced_layers),
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "correcting_steps": self.correcting_steps,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
            }
        )
        return self.model, stats

Base class for all compressors. Not a nn.Module to avoid parameter storage.

def compress_linear(
    self,
    layer: nn.Module,
    rank: int | None = None,
    name: str = "",

    """Decompose a single linear or Conv1D layer with spectrum-aware error suppression."""
    base_layer = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
    raw_w = base_layer.weight.data
    cls_name = base_layer.__class__.__name__.lower()

    else:

    dtype = base_layer.weight.dtype

    W_rec = A @ B
    norm_orig = torch.norm(W, p="fro")
    rel_err = (torch.norm(W - W_rec, p="fro") / (norm_orig + 1e-8)).item()

    # Fix 2: Always log a warning when reconstruction error exceeds max_error

        logger.warning(
            "SAES-SVD reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f%s",
            rel_err,
            name,
            self.max_error,
            (
                ". Keeping original layer."
                if self.fallback_to_original
                else ". Proceeding with compressed layer."
            ),
        )
        if self.fallback_to_original:
            return None

    has_lora = hasattr(layer, "lora_A") and hasattr(layer, "lora_B")
    lora_r = getattr(layer, "r", 0) if has_lora else 0

        in_features=in_f,
        out_features=out_f,
        rank=r,
        bias=has_bias,
        dtype=dtype,
        device=device,
        lora_r=lora_r,
    )
    svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(
        U_factor=A.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
        V_factor=B.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
    )
    # Fix 5: Ensure bias is copied correctly for both Linear and Conv1D
    if has_bias and svd_layer.bias is not None:
        svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(base_layer.bias.data.to(dtype=dtype, device=device))

    if has_lora and svd_layer.use_lora:
        try:
            if hasattr(layer.lora_A, "weight"):
                svd_layer.lora_A.weight.data.copy_(layer.lora_A.weight.data)
            if hasattr(layer.lora_B, "weight"):
                svd_layer.lora_B.weight.data.copy_(layer.lora_B.weight.data)
        except Exception as e_lora:
            logger.warning("Could not preserve LoRA adapters in SAES-SVD: %s", e_lora)

    # Store metadata on module
    svd_layer.reconstruction_error = rel_err
    svd_layer.rank = r
    return svd_layer

@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("saes_svd_v2")
class SAESSVDCompressorV2(BaseCompressor):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    """
    SAES-SVD v2 Compressor with iterative spectrum-aware residual correction,
    Frobenius validation, and safe fallback.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        rank: int | None = None,
        rank_ratio: float = 0.5,
        correcting_steps: int = 2,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        max_error: float = 0.60,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,  # Fix 1: Default changed to True
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="saes_svd_v2", **kwargs)
        cfg = kwargs.get("comp_config", kwargs.get("cfg"))
        if cfg is not None:
            rank = _cfg_get(cfg, "saes_rank", _cfg_get(cfg, "rank", rank))
            rank_ratio = _cfg_get(cfg, "saes_rank_ratio", _cfg_get(cfg, "rank_ratio", rank_ratio))
            correcting_steps = _cfg_get(cfg, "correcting_steps", correcting_steps)
            max_error = _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error)
            fallback_to_original = _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original)
            target_attention_only = _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only)

        if "rank" in kwargs and kwargs["rank"] is not None:
            rank = kwargs["rank"]
        if "fallback_to_original" in kwargs:
            fallback_to_original = bool(kwargs["fallback_to_original"])

        self.rank = rank
        self.rank_ratio = float(rank_ratio)
        self.correcting_steps = int(correcting_steps)
        self.target_modules = target_modules
        self.target_attention_only = bool(target_attention_only)
        self.max_error = float(max_error)
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(fallback_to_original)
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.layer_ranks: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.replaced_layers: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        """
        Fix 6: Unified critical-layer protection delegated to should_compress_linear.
        """
        name_l = name.lower()
        if self.target_attention_only:
            attn_keywords = [
                "attn",
                "attention",
                "q_proj",
                "k_proj",
                "v_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "c_attn",
                "c_proj",
                "query",
                "key",
                "value",
            ]
            if not any(k in name_l for k in attn_keywords):
                return False

        base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        # Fix 3: Reliable Conv1D check using class name and hasattr(nf)
        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(base_mod, nn.Linear) or "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
        )
        if not is_linear_like:
            return False
        if isinstance(base_mod, SVDLinear):
            return False
        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False
        if self.target_modules is None:
            return True
        return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)

    def compress_linear(
        self,
        layer: nn.Module,
        rank: int | None = None,
        name: str = "",
    ) -> SVDLinear | None:
        """Decompose a single linear or Conv1D layer with spectrum-aware error suppression."""
        base_layer = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
        raw_w = base_layer.weight.data
        cls_name = base_layer.__class__.__name__.lower()
        # Fix 3: Conv1D detection by class name or nf attribute
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_layer, "nf")

        if is_conv1d:
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
            out_f = W.shape[0]
            in_f = W.shape[1]
        else:
            W = raw_w.float()
            out_f = getattr(base_layer, "out_features", W.shape[0])
            in_f = getattr(base_layer, "in_features", W.shape[1])

        max_r = min(out_f, in_f)
        # Cap rank to guarantee parameter reduction
        param_cap = max(1, (in_f * out_f) // (in_f + out_f) - 1)
        effective_max_r = min(max_r, param_cap)

        if rank is not None:
            r = max(1, min(rank, effective_max_r))
        else:
            r = max(1, min(int(round(max_r * self.rank_ratio)), effective_max_r))

        device = base_layer.weight.device
        dtype = base_layer.weight.dtype

        # Compute full SVD in FP32
        try:
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("SAES-SVD SVD failure for %s: %s", name, e)
            return None

        # Initial symmetric square-root singular value allocation
        sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
        A = U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)  # [out_features, r]
        B = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]  # [r, in_features]

        # Iterative residual error suppression
        eps_eye = 1e-6 * torch.eye(r, device=W.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        for _ in range(self.correcting_steps):
            E = W - A @ B
            # Update A towards residual gradient
            BBt = B @ B.t().contiguous() + eps_eye
            A = A + E @ B.t().contiguous() @ torch.linalg.pinv(BBt)

            # Update B towards residual gradient
            AtA = A.t().contiguous() @ A + eps_eye
            B = B + torch.linalg.pinv(AtA) @ A.t().contiguous() @ (W - A @ B)

        # Compute relative Frobenius reconstruction error
        W_rec = A @ B
        norm_orig = torch.norm(W, p="fro")
        rel_err = (torch.norm(W - W_rec, p="fro") / (norm_orig + 1e-8)).item()

        # Fix 2: Always log a warning when reconstruction error exceeds max_error
        if rel_err > self.max_error:
            logger.warning(
                "SAES-SVD reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f%s",
                rel_err,
                name,
                self.max_error,
                (
                    ". Keeping original layer."
                    if self.fallback_to_original
                    else ". Proceeding with compressed layer."
                ),
            )
            if self.fallback_to_original:
                return None

        has_bias = getattr(base_layer, "bias", None) is not None
        has_lora = hasattr(layer, "lora_A") and hasattr(layer, "lora_B")
        lora_r = getattr(layer, "r", 0) if has_lora else 0

        svd_layer = SVDLinear(
            in_features=in_f,
            out_features=out_f,
            rank=r,
            bias=has_bias,
            dtype=dtype,
            device=device,
            lora_r=lora_r,
        )
        svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(
            U_factor=A.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
            V_factor=B.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
        )
        # Fix 5: Ensure bias is copied correctly for both Linear and Conv1D
        if has_bias and svd_layer.bias is not None:
            svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(base_layer.bias.data.to(dtype=dtype, device=device))

        if has_lora and svd_layer.use_lora:
            try:
                if hasattr(layer.lora_A, "weight"):
                    svd_layer.lora_A.weight.data.copy_(layer.lora_A.weight.data)
                if hasattr(layer.lora_B, "weight"):
                    svd_layer.lora_B.weight.data.copy_(layer.lora_B.weight.data)
            except Exception as e_lora:
                logger.warning("Could not preserve LoRA adapters in SAES-SVD: %s", e_lora)

        # Store metadata on module
        svd_layer.reconstruction_error = rel_err
        svd_layer.rank = r
        return svd_layer

    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Compress all linear and Conv1D layers in the model."""
        self.layer_errors = {}
        self.layer_ranks = {}
        self.replaced_layers = []

        target_layers = [
            (name, mod)
            for name, mod in self.model.named_modules()
            if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
        ]

        if not target_layers:
            logger.warning("SAES-SVD v2: No compressible linear layers found.")
            return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

        for name, module in target_layers:
            svd_layer = self.compress_linear(module, rank=self.rank, name=name)
            if svd_layer is not None:
                err = getattr(svd_layer, "reconstruction_error", 0.0)
                r = getattr(svd_layer, "rank", self.rank)
                self.layer_errors[name] = err
                self.layer_ranks[name] = r
                # Fix 4: Include per-layer rank and error in returned stats
                self.replaced_layers.append({"name": name, "rank": r, "error": err})
                replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)
            else:
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False

        self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        # Fix 4 & Fix 6: Detailed per-layer stats, errors, ranks, and fallback status
        stats.update(
            {
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
                "replaced_layers": self.replaced_layers,
                "compressed_layers": len(self.replaced_layers),
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "correcting_steps": self.correcting_steps,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
            }
        )
        return self.model, stats

Base class for all compressors. Not a nn.Module to avoid parameter storage.

Expand source code 
class SVDLinear(nn.Module):
    """
    Low-rank factorized linear layer:
        y = (x @ V.T) @ U.T + LoRA(x) * scaling + b
    Parameter count: r·(d_in + d_out) + LoRA params.
    """

    # AASVD-FIX-01
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        rank: int,
        bias: bool = True,
        use_fused_kernel: bool = False,
        dtype: torch.dtype = torch.float32,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        lora_r: int | None = None,
        freeze_base: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = int(in_features)
        self.out_features = int(out_features)
        self.rank = max(1, min(rank, self.in_features, self.out_features))
        self.use_fused_kernel = bool(use_fused_kernel)

        # Accept both lora_rank and legacy lora_r
        eff_lora = int(lora_r) if (lora_r is not None and lora_r > 0) else max(0, int(lora_rank))
        self.lora_rank = eff_lora
        self.lora_r = eff_lora
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.lora_scaling = (self.lora_alpha / self.lora_rank) if self.lora_rank > 0 else 1.0
        self.scaling = self.lora_scaling

        requires_grad = not (self.lora_rank > 0 or freeze_base)
        fk = {"dtype": dtype, "device": device}

        self.V = nn.Parameter(
            torch.empty(self.rank, self.in_features, **fk), requires_grad=requires_grad
        )
        self.U = nn.Parameter(
            torch.empty(self.out_features, self.rank, **fk), requires_grad=requires_grad
        )

        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(self.out_features, **fk), requires_grad=requires_grad
            )
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.in_features, **fk))
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.out_features, self.lora_rank, **fk))
            with torch.no_grad():
                nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        # AASVD-FIX-07: input mean is not tracked; remove the buffer.
        self._input_mean: torch.Tensor | None = None

        self.reset_parameters()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.get_weight()

    def get_weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        w = torch.matmul(self.U, self.V)
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            w = w + torch.matmul(self.lora_B, self.lora_A) * self.lora_scaling
        return w

    @property
    def use_lora(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None)

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        if self.V.requires_grad:
            self.V.requires_grad = False
        if self.U.requires_grad:
            self.U.requires_grad = False
        if self.bias is not None and self.bias.requires_grad:
            self.bias.requires_grad = False
        if self.lora_A is not None:
            self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
        if self.lora_B is not None:
            self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def reset_parameters(self) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            for param in (self.V, self.U):
                if param.dtype in (torch.float16, torch.bfloat16):
                    tmp = torch.empty_like(param, dtype=torch.float32, device="cpu")
                    _orthogonal_init(tmp)
                    param.copy_(tmp.to(dtype=param.dtype, device=param.device))
                else:
                    _orthogonal_init(param)
            if self.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(self.bias)
            if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
                nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
                nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        if self._input_mean is not None:
            x = x - self._input_mean.to(x.device, x.dtype)

        if self.use_fused_kernel and (self.lora_rank == 0 or self.lora_A is None):
            try:
                from neuralops.attentions.fused_svd_kernel import fused_svd_linear

                return fused_svd_linear(x=x, V=self.V, U=self.U, bias=self.bias, use_rms_norm=False)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("SVDLinear fused kernel failed (%s); using F.linear.", e)

        latent = F.linear(x, self.V)
        out = F.linear(latent, self.U)

        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            a = self.lora_A.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_A.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_A
            b = self.lora_B.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_B.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_B
            lora_latent = F.linear(x, a)
            out = out + F.linear(lora_latent, b) * self.lora_scaling

        if self.bias is not None:
            out = out + self.bias.to(out.dtype)
        return out

    def set_low_rank_factors(self, U_factor: torch.Tensor, V_factor: torch.Tensor) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            r = min(self.rank, U_factor.size(1), V_factor.size(0))
            self.U.data.zero_()
            self.V.data.zero_()
            self.U.data[:, :r].copy_(U_factor[:, :r].to(self.U.device, self.U.dtype))
            self.V.data[:r, :].copy_(V_factor[:r, :].to(self.V.device, self.V.dtype))

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    if self._input_mean is not None:
        x = x - self._input_mean.to(x.device, x.dtype)

    if self.use_fused_kernel and (self.lora_rank == 0 or self.lora_A is None):
        try:
            from neuralops.attentions.fused_svd_kernel import fused_svd_linear

            return fused_svd_linear(x=x, V=self.V, U=self.U, bias=self.bias, use_rms_norm=False)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("SVDLinear fused kernel failed (%s); using F.linear.", e)

    latent = F.linear(x, self.V)
    out = F.linear(latent, self.U)

    if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
        a = self.lora_A.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_A.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_A
        b = self.lora_B.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_B.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_B
        lora_latent = F.linear(x, a)
        out = out + F.linear(lora_latent, b) * self.lora_scaling

    if self.bias is not None:
        out = out + self.bias.to(out.dtype)
    return out

class AdaptiveSVDLinear (in_features: int,
out_features: int,
rank: int,

dtype: torch.dtype = torch.float32,
device: torch.device | None = None,

lora_r: int | None = None,
freeze_base: bool = False)
Expand source code 
class SVDLinear(nn.Module):
    """
    Low-rank factorized linear layer:
        y = (x @ V.T) @ U.T + LoRA(x) * scaling + b
    Parameter count: r·(d_in + d_out) + LoRA params.
    """

    # AASVD-FIX-01
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        rank: int,
        bias: bool = True,
        use_fused_kernel: bool = False,
        dtype: torch.dtype = torch.float32,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        lora_r: int | None = None,
        freeze_base: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = int(in_features)
        self.out_features = int(out_features)
        self.rank = max(1, min(rank, self.in_features, self.out_features))
        self.use_fused_kernel = bool(use_fused_kernel)

        # Accept both lora_rank and legacy lora_r
        eff_lora = int(lora_r) if (lora_r is not None and lora_r > 0) else max(0, int(lora_rank))
        self.lora_rank = eff_lora
        self.lora_r = eff_lora
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.lora_scaling = (self.lora_alpha / self.lora_rank) if self.lora_rank > 0 else 1.0
        self.scaling = self.lora_scaling

        requires_grad = not (self.lora_rank > 0 or freeze_base)
        fk = {"dtype": dtype, "device": device}

        self.V = nn.Parameter(
            torch.empty(self.rank, self.in_features, **fk), requires_grad=requires_grad
        )
        self.U = nn.Parameter(
            torch.empty(self.out_features, self.rank, **fk), requires_grad=requires_grad
        )

        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(self.out_features, **fk), requires_grad=requires_grad
            )
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.in_features, **fk))
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.out_features, self.lora_rank, **fk))
            with torch.no_grad():
                nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        # AASVD-FIX-07: input mean is not tracked; remove the buffer.
        self._input_mean: torch.Tensor | None = None

        self.reset_parameters()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.get_weight()

    def get_weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        w = torch.matmul(self.U, self.V)
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            w = w + torch.matmul(self.lora_B, self.lora_A) * self.lora_scaling
        return w

    @property
    def use_lora(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None)

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        if self.V.requires_grad:
            self.V.requires_grad = False
        if self.U.requires_grad:
            self.U.requires_grad = False
        if self.bias is not None and self.bias.requires_grad:
            self.bias.requires_grad = False
        if self.lora_A is not None:
            self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
        if self.lora_B is not None:
            self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def reset_parameters(self) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            for param in (self.V, self.U):
                if param.dtype in (torch.float16, torch.bfloat16):
                    tmp = torch.empty_like(param, dtype=torch.float32, device="cpu")
                    _orthogonal_init(tmp)
                    param.copy_(tmp.to(dtype=param.dtype, device=param.device))
                else:
                    _orthogonal_init(param)
            if self.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(self.bias)
            if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
                nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
                nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        if self._input_mean is not None:
            x = x - self._input_mean.to(x.device, x.dtype)

        if self.use_fused_kernel and (self.lora_rank == 0 or self.lora_A is None):
            try:
                from neuralops.attentions.fused_svd_kernel import fused_svd_linear

                return fused_svd_linear(x=x, V=self.V, U=self.U, bias=self.bias, use_rms_norm=False)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("SVDLinear fused kernel failed (%s); using F.linear.", e)

        latent = F.linear(x, self.V)
        out = F.linear(latent, self.U)

        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            a = self.lora_A.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_A.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_A
            b = self.lora_B.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_B.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_B
            lora_latent = F.linear(x, a)
            out = out + F.linear(lora_latent, b) * self.lora_scaling

        if self.bias is not None:
            out = out + self.bias.to(out.dtype)
        return out

    def set_low_rank_factors(self, U_factor: torch.Tensor, V_factor: torch.Tensor) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            r = min(self.rank, U_factor.size(1), V_factor.size(0))
            self.U.data.zero_()
            self.V.data.zero_()
            self.U.data[:, :r].copy_(U_factor[:, :r].to(self.U.device, self.U.dtype))
            self.V.data[:r, :].copy_(V_factor[:r, :].to(self.V.device, self.V.dtype))

class SVDLinearCompressor (model: nn.Module,
rank: int = 192,
