Expand source code 
@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("svd_linear")
class SVDLinearCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    """Compresses model by replacing Linear/Conv1D layers with SVDLinearLayer."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        rank: int = 192,
        max_error: float = 0.20,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        protect_critical: bool = True,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        skip_attention_qkv: bool = False,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        cfg: Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="svd_linear", **kwargs)
        conf = cfg or kwargs.get("comp_config")
        if conf is not None:
            rank = _cfg_get(conf, "svd_rank", _cfg_get(conf, "rank", rank))
            max_error = _cfg_get(conf, "max_error", max_error)
            fallback_to_original = _cfg_get(conf, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original)
            protect_critical = _cfg_get(conf, "protect_critical", protect_critical)
            target_attention_only = _cfg_get(conf, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only)
            lora_r = _cfg_get(conf, "lora_r", _cfg_get(conf, "lora_rank", lora_r))
            lora_alpha = _cfg_get(conf, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha)
            skip_attention_qkv = _cfg_get(conf, "skip_attention_qkv", skip_attention_qkv)

        self.rank = int(rank)
        self.max_error = float(max_error)
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(fallback_to_original)
        self.protect_critical = bool(protect_critical)
        self.target_attention_only = bool(target_attention_only)
        self.lora_r = int(lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.skip_attention_qkv = bool(skip_attention_qkv)
        self.target_modules = target_modules
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.replaced_layers: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.skipped_layers: list[str] = []
        self.stats: dict[str, Any] = {}

    def _is_gqa_asymmetric(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        if not self.skip_attention_qkv:
            return False
        cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
        num_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_attention_heads", None)
        num_kv_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_key_value_heads", None)
        if num_heads is not None and num_kv_heads is not None and num_kv_heads < num_heads:
            name_lower = name.lower()
            if any(
                p in name_lower
                for p in [
                    "q_proj",
                    "k_proj",
                    "v_proj",
                    "query",
                    "key",
                    "value",
                    "c_attn",
                ]
            ):
                return True
        return False

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        name_l = name.lower()
        if any(
            k in name_l
            for k in [
                "out_lin",
                "out_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "c_proj",
                "output.dense",
                "self.output",
            ]
        ):
            return False
        if self.protect_critical and any(
            k in name_l
            for k in [
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embed",
                "logits_processor",
            ]
        ):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            attn_keywords = [
                "attn",
                "attention",
                "q_proj",
                "k_proj",
                "v_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "c_attn",
                "c_proj",
                "query",
                "key",
                "value",
            ]
            if not any(k in name_l for k in attn_keywords):
                return False

        base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(base_mod, nn.Linear) or "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
        )
        if not is_linear_like:
            return False
        if isinstance(base_mod, SVDLinearLayer):
            return False
        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False
        if self._is_gqa_asymmetric(name, module):
            return False
        if self.target_modules is not None:
            return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)
        return True

    def compress(self, **kwargs: Any) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Compress linear layers with SVD factorization."""
        self.layer_errors = {}
        self.replaced_layers = []
        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.skipped_layers = []
        seen_weights: set[int] = set()

        target_layers = [
            (name, mod)
            for name, mod in list(self.model.named_modules())
            if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
        ]

        if not target_layers:
            logger.warning("SVDLinear: No compressible linear layers found.")
            return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

        for name, module in target_layers:
            base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            if not hasattr(base_mod, "weight") or base_mod.weight is None:
                continue
            w_id = id(base_mod.weight)
            if w_id in seen_weights:
                self.skipped_layers.append(name)
                continue
            seen_weights.add(w_id)

            try:
                new_layer = SVDLinearLayer(
                    module,
                    rank=self.rank,
                    lora_r=self.lora_r,
                    lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                )
                err = new_layer.reconstruction_error
                self.layer_errors[name] = err

                if err > self.max_error:
                    logger.warning(
                        "SVDLinear reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f.",
                        err,
                        name,
                        self.max_error,
                    )
                    if self.fallback_to_original:
                        logger.info(
                            "Skipping SVDLinear compression for %s (fallback to original).",
                            name,
                        )
                        self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                        for p in module.parameters():
                            p.requires_grad = False
                        continue

                self.replaced_layers.append({"name": name, "rank": new_layer.rank, "error": err})
                self.compressed_layers.append(name)
                replace_module(self.model, name, new_layer)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("Failed to compress %s with SVDLinear: %s", name, e)
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False

        # Post-compression forward sanity check
        forward_check_success = False
        try:
            self.model.eval()
            with torch.no_grad():
                d = next(self.model.parameters()).device
                dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 16), device=d)
                self.model(dummy)
            forward_check_success = True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("SVDLinear post-compression forward sanity check exception: %s", e)

        self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        avg_err = (
            sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
            / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
            if self.compressed_layers
            else 0.0
        )
        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        stats.update(
            {
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "lora_r": self.lora_r,
                "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "method": "svd_linear_factorization",
            }
        )
        self.stats = stats
        return self.model, stats

Compresses model by replacing Linear/Conv1D layers with SVDLinearLayer.

def compress(self,
**kwargs: Any) ‑> tuple[torch.nn.modules.module.Module, dict[str, typing.Any]]
Expand source code 
def compress(self, **kwargs: Any) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Compress linear layers with SVD factorization."""
    self.layer_errors = {}
    self.replaced_layers = []
    self.compressed_layers = []
    self.fallback_layers = []
    self.skipped_layers = []
    seen_weights: set[int] = set()

    target_layers = [
        (name, mod)
        for name, mod in list(self.model.named_modules())
        if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
    ]

    if not target_layers:
        logger.warning("SVDLinear: No compressible linear layers found.")
        return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

    for name, module in target_layers:
        base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        if not hasattr(base_mod, "weight") or base_mod.weight is None:
            continue
        w_id = id(base_mod.weight)
        if w_id in seen_weights:
            self.skipped_layers.append(name)
            continue
        seen_weights.add(w_id)

        try:
            new_layer = SVDLinearLayer(
                module,
                rank=self.rank,
                lora_r=self.lora_r,
                lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            )
            err = new_layer.reconstruction_error
            self.layer_errors[name] = err

            if err > self.max_error:
                logger.warning(
                    "SVDLinear reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f.",
                    err,
                    name,
                    self.max_error,
                )
                if self.fallback_to_original:
                    logger.info(
                        "Skipping SVDLinear compression for %s (fallback to original).",
                        name,
                    )
                    self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    continue

            self.replaced_layers.append({"name": name, "rank": new_layer.rank, "error": err})
            self.compressed_layers.append(name)
            replace_module(self.model, name, new_layer)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Failed to compress %s with SVDLinear: %s", name, e)
            self.fallback_layers.append(name)
            for p in module.parameters():
                p.requires_grad = False

    # Post-compression forward sanity check
    forward_check_success = False
    try:
        self.model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            d = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 16), device=d)
            self.model(dummy)
        forward_check_success = True
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug("SVDLinear post-compression forward sanity check exception: %s", e)

    self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
    avg_err = (
        sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
        / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
        if self.compressed_layers
        else 0.0
    )
    stats = self.get_compression_stats()
    stats.update(
        {
            "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
            "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
            "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
            "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
            "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
            "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
            "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
            "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
            "max_error": self.max_error,
            "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
            "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
            "lora_r": self.lora_r,
            "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
            "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
            "method": "svd_linear_factorization",
        }
    )
    self.stats = stats
    return self.model, stats

class SVDLinearLayer (linear_layer: nn.Module,
rank: int,
use_fused_kernel: bool = False,

eps: float = 1e-06,
lora_r: int = 0,

Expand source code 
class SVDLinearLayer(nn.Module):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    """
    Replace a linear layer with factorized U @ V, keeping top-`rank` components.
    Optionally dispatches fused in-SRAM kernel to eliminate memory roundtrips.
    Includes optional trainable LoRA adapters (lora_A, lora_B).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        linear_layer: nn.Module,
        rank: int,
        use_fused_kernel: bool = False,
        use_rms_norm: bool = False,
        eps: float = 1e-6,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        base_layer = getattr(linear_layer, "base_layer", linear_layer)
        raw_w = base_layer.weight.data
        cls_name = base_layer.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_layer, "nf")

        if is_conv1d:
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
            self.out_features = W.shape[0]
            self.in_features = W.shape[1]
        else:
            W = raw_w.float()
            self.out_features = getattr(base_layer, "out_features", W.shape[0])
            self.in_features = getattr(base_layer, "in_features", W.shape[1])

        self.nf = self.out_features  # Conv1D compatibility
        param_cap = max(
            1,
            (self.in_features * self.out_features) // (self.in_features + self.out_features) - 1,
        )
        self.rank: int = max(1, min(rank, self.in_features, self.out_features, param_cap))
        self.use_fused_kernel: bool = use_fused_kernel
        self.use_rms_norm: bool = use_rms_norm
        self.eps: float = eps

        has_existing_lora = hasattr(linear_layer, "lora_A") and hasattr(linear_layer, "lora_B")
        effective_lora_r = lora_r
        if has_existing_lora and getattr(linear_layer, "r", 0) > 0:
            effective_lora_r = getattr(linear_layer, "r", lora_r)

        self.lora_r = int(effective_lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.scaling = (self.lora_alpha / self.lora_r) if self.lora_r > 0 else 1.0

        device = base_layer.weight.device
        dtype = base_layer.weight.dtype

        with torch.no_grad():
            U, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            sqrt_s = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[: self.rank], min=1e-12))
            U_weighted = (U[:, : self.rank] * sqrt_s.unsqueeze(0)).to(device=device, dtype=dtype)
            V_weighted = (sqrt_s.unsqueeze(1) * Vh[: self.rank, :]).to(device=device, dtype=dtype)

        requires_grad = not (self.lora_r > 0)
        self.U = nn.Parameter(U_weighted, requires_grad=requires_grad)
        self.V = nn.Parameter(V_weighted, requires_grad=requires_grad)

        if use_rms_norm:
            self.norm_weight = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.rank, dtype=dtype, device=device))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("norm_weight", None)

        if base_layer.bias is not None:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(base_layer.bias.data.clone(), requires_grad=requires_grad)
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if self.lora_r > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty((self.lora_r, self.in_features), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
            )
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(
                torch.zeros((self.out_features, self.lora_r), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
            )
            if has_existing_lora:
                try:
                    if hasattr(linear_layer.lora_A, "weight"):
                        self.lora_A.data.copy_(linear_layer.lora_A.weight.data)
                    if hasattr(linear_layer.lora_B, "weight"):
                        self.lora_B.data.copy_(linear_layer.lora_B.weight.data)
                except Exception:
                    nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            else:
                nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        # Frobenius reconstruction error
        W_rec = torch.matmul(U_weighted.float(), V_weighted.float())
        norm_orig = torch.norm(W, p="fro")
        self.reconstruction_error = (torch.norm(W - W_rec, p="fro") / (norm_orig + 1e-8)).item()

    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Reconstructed effective weight matrix for compatibility."""
        return torch.matmul(self.U, self.V)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Forward pass with optional fused kernel acceleration and LoRA branch."""
        if self.use_fused_kernel and self.lora_r <= 0 and fused_svd_linear is not None:
            return fused_svd_linear(
                x=x,
                V=self.V,
                U=self.U,
                norm_weight=self.norm_weight if self.use_rms_norm else None,
                bias=self.bias,
                eps=self.eps,
                use_rms_norm=self.use_rms_norm,
            )

        latent = F.linear(x, self.V)
        out = F.linear(latent, self.U, self.bias)

        if self.lora_r > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            orig_shape = x.shape
            x_flat = x.reshape(-1, self.in_features)
            lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x_flat.float(), self.lora_A), self.lora_B)
            out = out + (self.scaling * lora_out).reshape(*orig_shape[:-1], self.out_features).to(
                out.dtype
            )

        return out

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

class SVDSingleHead (hidden_size: int, svd_rank: int = 20)
Expand source code 
class SVDSingleHead(nn.Module):
    """
    DEPRECATED single-head SVD attention.

    Performing SVD on live activations is O(N^2 d), numerically fragile, and
    theoretically incorrect as an attention replacement. Use the weight-based
    HybridSVDCompressor instead.

    Kept strictly for backwards compatibility. First use emits a warning.
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    _warned: bool = False

    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, svd_rank: int = 20) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        if not SVDSingleHead._warned:
            warnings.warn(
                "SVDSingleHead is deprecated (SVD on live activations is "
                "computationally prohibitive and incorrect). Use "
                "HybridSVDCompressor for weight-based SVD instead.",
                DeprecationWarning,
                stacklevel=2,
            )
            SVDSingleHead._warned = True
        self.hidden_size = hidden_size
        self.svd_rank = int(svd_rank)
        self._pos_encoding: torch.Tensor | None = None

    def _get_pos_encoding(
        self, seq_len: int, device: torch.device, dtype: torch.dtype
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        if (
            self._pos_encoding is None
            or self._pos_encoding.shape[1] != seq_len
            or self._pos_encoding.device != device
        ):
            pe = torch.zeros(seq_len, self.hidden_size, device=device)
            position = torch.arange(0, seq_len, dtype=torch.float, device=device).unsqueeze(1)
            div_term = torch.exp(
                torch.arange(0, self.hidden_size, 2, dtype=torch.float, device=device)
                * (-math.log(10000.0) / self.hidden_size)
            )
            pe[:, 0::2] = torch.sin(position * div_term)
            pe[:, 1::2] = torch.cos(position * div_term)
            self._pos_encoding = pe.unsqueeze(0).to(dtype)
        return self._pos_encoding

    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, L, _ = hidden_states.shape
        pe = self._get_pos_encoding(L, hidden_states.device, hidden_states.dtype)
        x = hidden_states + pe[:, :L, :]

        x_flat = F.normalize(x.reshape(-1, self.hidden_size).float(), p=2, dim=-1)
        try:
            U, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(x_flat, full_matrices=False)
            k = min(self.svd_rank, len(S))
            weighted_U = U[:, :k] * S[:k].view(1, -1)
            output_flat = torch.matmul(weighted_U, Vh[:k, :])
            return output_flat.reshape(B, L, self.hidden_size).to(hidden_states.dtype)
        except Exception:
            return x

Kept strictly for backwards compatibility. First use emits a warning.

class SubspaceLinear (in_features: int,
out_features: int,
rank: int = 64,
bias: bool = True,
lora_r: int = 0,
lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
device: torch.device | None = None)
Expand source code 
class SubspaceLinear(nn.Module):
    """
    Subspace Projection Factorized Linear Layer with optional LoRA adapter.

    W ≈ B @ A, where:
      A (rank, in_features):  orthonormal / projection basis
      B (out_features, rank): coefficient expansion matrix

    Forward:
      y = ((x @ A^T) @ B^T) + bias + (lora_B @ lora_A @ x) * scaling
    """

    # SP-FIX-01
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        rank: int = 64,
        bias: bool = True,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = int(in_features)
        self.out_features = int(out_features)
        self.nf = self.out_features  # Conv1D compatibility
        self.nx = self.in_features  # Conv1D compatibility
        self.rank = max(1, min(rank, in_features, out_features))
        self.lora_r = int(lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.scaling = (self.lora_alpha / self.lora_r) if self.lora_r > 0 else 1.0

        self.A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank, in_features, dtype=dtype, device=device))
        self.B = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features, self.rank, dtype=dtype, device=device))

        # Orthogonal init for A; xavier for B. Do it in fp32 for stability.
        with torch.no_grad():
            if dtype in (torch.float16, torch.bfloat16):
                A_fp32 = torch.empty(self.rank, in_features, dtype=torch.float32)
                nn.init.orthogonal_(A_fp32)
                self.A.data.copy_(A_fp32.to(dtype=dtype, device=device))
            else:
                nn.init.orthogonal_(self.A)

            B_fp32 = self.B.data.float()
            nn.init.xavier_uniform_(B_fp32)
            self.B.data.copy_(B_fp32.to(dtype=dtype, device=device))

        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(out_features, dtype=dtype, device=device))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if self.lora_r > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty((self.lora_r, in_features), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
            )
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(
                torch.zeros((out_features, self.lora_r), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
            )
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return torch.matmul(self.B.float(), self.A.float()).to(self.A.dtype)

    def get_weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.weight

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        orig_shape = x.shape
        x_flat = x.reshape(-1, self.in_features)

        # SP-FIX-03: dtype-safe
        A = self.A if self.A.dtype == x_flat.dtype else self.A.to(x_flat.dtype)
        B = self.B if self.B.dtype == x_flat.dtype else self.B.to(x_flat.dtype)
        b = None
        if self.bias is not None:
            b = self.bias if self.bias.dtype == x_flat.dtype else self.bias.to(x_flat.dtype)

        x_proj = F.linear(x_flat, A)  # (K, rank)
        out = F.linear(x_proj, B, b)  # (K, out)

        if self.lora_r > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x_flat.float(), self.lora_A), self.lora_B) * self.scaling
            out = out + lora_out.to(out.dtype)

        return out.view(*orig_shape[:-1], self.out_features)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @classmethod
    def from_linear(
        cls,
        linear: nn.Module,
        rank: int = 64,
        method: str = "svd",
        use_lora: bool = False,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
    ) -> SubspaceLinear:
        """
        Project linear or Conv1D weight matrix onto top-rank subspace.
        """
        base_mod = getattr(linear, "base_layer", linear)
        raw_w = base_mod.weight.data
        cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")

        if is_conv1d:
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous()
            out_f, in_f = W.shape
        else:
            W = raw_w
            out_f, in_f = W.shape

        bias = base_mod.bias.data if getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None else None

        # ---------------- Rank budget (SP-FIX-07) ----------------
        max_r = min(out_f, in_f)
        param_cap = max(1, (in_f * out_f) // max(1, in_f + out_f))
        r = max(1, min(int(rank), max_r, param_cap))

        # ---------------- Existing LoRA detection ----------------
        has_existing_lora = (
            hasattr(linear, "lora_A")
            and hasattr(linear, "lora_B")
            and _get_lora_param(linear.lora_A) is not None
            and _get_lora_param(linear.lora_B) is not None
        )
        existing_r = getattr(linear, "r", getattr(linear, "lora_r", 0))
        effective_lora_r = (
            int(existing_r) if (has_existing_lora and existing_r > 0) else int(lora_r)
        )
        effective_lora_alpha = float(
            getattr(linear, "lora_alpha", getattr(linear, "alpha", lora_alpha))
        )

        subspace_layer = cls(
            in_features=in_f,
            out_features=out_f,
            rank=r,
            bias=(bias is not None),
            lora_r=effective_lora_r if (use_lora or has_existing_lora) else 0,
            lora_alpha=effective_lora_alpha,
            dtype=W.dtype,
            device=W.device,
        )

        # ---------------- SVD factorization ----------------
        U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W.float(), full_matrices=False)
        A_data = Vt[:r, :]  # (r, in_f)
        B_data = U[:, :r] * S[:r].unsqueeze(0)  # (out_f, r)

        with torch.no_grad():
            subspace_layer.A.data.copy_(A_data.to(dtype=W.dtype, device=W.device))
            subspace_layer.B.data.copy_(B_data.to(dtype=W.dtype, device=W.device))

            # SP-FIX-11: bias under no_grad
            if bias is not None and subspace_layer.bias is not None:
                subspace_layer.bias.data.copy_(bias.to(dtype=W.dtype, device=W.device))

        # ---------------- LoRA transfer (SP-FIX-04) ----------------
        if (
            has_existing_lora
            and subspace_layer.lora_A is not None
            and subspace_layer.lora_B is not None
        ):
            try:
                src_A = _get_lora_param(linear.lora_A)
                src_B = _get_lora_param(linear.lora_B)
                if src_A is not None and src_B is not None:
                    if src_A.shape == subspace_layer.lora_A.shape:
                        subspace_layer.lora_A.data.copy_(
                            src_A.to(
                                subspace_layer.lora_A.device,
                                dtype=subspace_layer.lora_A.dtype,
                            )
                        )
                    if src_B.shape == subspace_layer.lora_B.shape:
                        subspace_layer.lora_B.data.copy_(
                            src_B.to(
                                subspace_layer.lora_B.device,
                                dtype=subspace_layer.lora_B.dtype,
                            )
                        )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("SubspaceLinear LoRA transfer failed: %s", e)

        return subspace_layer

Subspace Projection Factorized Linear Layer with optional LoRA adapter.

W ≈ B @ A, where: A (rank, in_features): orthonormal / projection basis B (out_features, rank): coefficient expansion matrix

Forward -----= y = ((x @ A^T) @ B^T) + bias + (lora_B @ lora_A @ x) * scaling

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    orig_shape = x.shape
    x_flat = x.reshape(-1, self.in_features)

    # SP-FIX-03: dtype-safe
    A = self.A if self.A.dtype == x_flat.dtype else self.A.to(x_flat.dtype)
    B = self.B if self.B.dtype == x_flat.dtype else self.B.to(x_flat.dtype)
    b = None
    if self.bias is not None:
        b = self.bias if self.bias.dtype == x_flat.dtype else self.bias.to(x_flat.dtype)

    x_proj = F.linear(x_flat, A)  # (K, rank)
    out = F.linear(x_proj, B, b)  # (K, out)

    if self.lora_r > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
        lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x_flat.float(), self.lora_A), self.lora_B) * self.scaling
        out = out + lora_out.to(out.dtype)

    return out.view(*orig_shape[:-1], self.out_features)

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("subspace_projection")
class SubspaceProjectionCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    """
    Subspace Projection Compression Engine.
    """

    # SP-FIX-01
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module | None = None,
        rank: int = 64,
        method: str = "svd",
        cfg: Any | None = None,
        target_layers: list[str] | None = None,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        use_lora: bool = False,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        max_error: float = 0.50,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(
            model if model is not None else nn.Identity(),
            name="subspace_projection",
            **kwargs,
        )
        cfg_obj = cfg or kwargs.get("comp_config")

        self.rank = int(rank or _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "subspace_rank", _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "rank", 64)))
        self.method = str(method or _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "subspace_method", "svd"))
        if self.method != "svd":
            logger.warning(
                "SubspaceProjection: method='%s' is not implemented; using 'svd'.",
                self.method,
            )
            self.method = "svd"

        self.cfg = cfg_obj
        tl = target_layers or _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "target_layers", None)
        if tl is not None and len(tl) == 0:
            tl = None
        self.target_layers = tl

        self.target_attention_only = bool(
            target_attention_only or _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "target_attention_only", False)
        )
        self.use_lora = bool(use_lora or _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "use_lora", False) or (lora_r > 0))
        self.lora_r = int(lora_r or _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "lora_r", 0))
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha or _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "lora_alpha", 1.0))
        self.max_error = float(_cfg_get(cfg_obj, "max_error", max_error))
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(
            _cfg_get(cfg_obj, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original)
        )

        self.compressed_layers: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.skipped_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.stats: dict[str, Any] = {}

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        name_l = name.lower()
        if any(
            k in name_l
            for k in (
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embedding",
                "layernorm",
                "rmsnorm",
                "batchnorm",
            )
        ):
            return False

        if isinstance(module, SubspaceLinear):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            attn_keywords = (
                "attn",
                "attention",
                "q_proj",
                "k_proj",
                "v_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "c_attn",
                "c_proj",
                "query",
                "key",
                "value",
            )
            if not any(k in name_l for k in attn_keywords):
                return False

        base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(base_mod, nn.Linear) or "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
        )
        if not is_linear_like:
            return False

        try:
            if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
                return False
        except Exception:
            pass

        if self.target_layers is None:
            return True
        return any(t in name for t in self.target_layers)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress(self, model: nn.Module | None = None) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        from neuralops.core.model_utils import replace_module

        if model is not None:
            self.model = model
            try:
                self._original_params = self._count_params(self.model)
            except Exception:
                self._original_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.skipped_layers = []
        self.layer_errors = {}

        total_orig_bytes = 0
        total_comp_bytes = 0
        seen_weights: set[int] = set()

        for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
            if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                continue

            base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            w = getattr(base_mod, "weight", None)
            if w is None:
                continue
            w_id = id(w)
            if w_id in seen_weights:
                self.skipped_layers.append(name)
                continue
            seen_weights.add(w_id)

            raw_w = w.data
            cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")

            W_orig = raw_w.t().contiguous().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.float()
            max_rank = min(W_orig.shape[0], W_orig.shape[1])
            r = min(self.rank, max_rank)

            # storage before
            orig_bytes = raw_w.numel() * raw_w.element_size()
            if getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                orig_bytes += base_mod.bias.numel() * base_mod.bias.element_size()
            total_orig_bytes += orig_bytes

            try:
                new_layer = SubspaceLinear.from_linear(
                    module,
                    rank=r,
                    method=self.method,
                    use_lora=self.use_lora,
                    lora_r=self.lora_r,
                    lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("Subspace projection failed on %s: %s", name, e)
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                self.layer_errors[name] = 1.0
                total_comp_bytes += orig_bytes
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                continue

            # error on forward path
            W_rec = new_layer.weight.float()
            if is_conv1d:
                W_orig_cmp = W_orig
                W_rec_cmp = W_rec.t().contiguous() if W_rec.shape != W_orig.shape else W_rec
            else:
                W_orig_cmp = W_orig
                W_rec_cmp = W_rec

            denom = torch.norm(W_orig_cmp, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-12)
            err = float((torch.norm(W_orig_cmp - W_rec_cmp, p="fro") / denom).item())
            self.layer_errors[name] = err

            if self.fallback_to_original and err > self.max_error:
                logger.warning(
                    "SubspaceProjection: err=%.4f on %s > max_error=%.4f; keeping original.",
                    err,
                    name,
                    self.max_error,
                )
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                total_comp_bytes += orig_bytes
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                continue

            # SP-FIX-10: preserve LoRA wrapper
            try:
                if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                    module.base_layer = new_layer
                    for pn, p in module.named_parameters():
                        if "lora_" in pn.lower() and not pn.startswith("base_layer"):
                            p.requires_grad = True
                else:
                    replace_module(self.model, name, new_layer)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("SubspaceProjection replacement failed on %s: %s", name, e)
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                total_comp_bytes += orig_bytes
                continue

            self.compressed_layers.append(
                {
                    "name": name,
                    "rank": new_layer.rank,
                    "relative_error": float(err),
                }
            )

            # storage after
            comp_bytes = (
                new_layer.A.numel() * new_layer.A.element_size()
                + new_layer.B.numel() * new_layer.B.element_size()
            )
            if new_layer.bias is not None:
                comp_bytes += new_layer.bias.numel() * new_layer.bias.element_size()
            if new_layer.lora_A is not None:
                comp_bytes += new_layer.lora_A.numel() * new_layer.lora_A.element_size()
            if new_layer.lora_B is not None:
                comp_bytes += new_layer.lora_B.numel() * new_layer.lora_B.element_size()
            total_comp_bytes += comp_bytes

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # SP-FIX-08: Forward sanity check
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        forward_check_success = True
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                dev = next(self.model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
                dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 8), device=dev, dtype=torch.long)
                try:
                    self.model(input_ids=dummy)
                except Exception:
                    try:
                        self.model(dummy)
                    except Exception:
                        cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
                        hidden = getattr(cfg, "hidden_size", None) if cfg else None
                        if hidden is None:
                            first_lin = next(
                                (m for m in self.model.modules() if isinstance(m, nn.Linear)),
                                None,
                            )
                            hidden = getattr(first_lin, "in_features", 8)
                        dt = next(self.model.parameters()).dtype
                        self.model(torch.randn(1, 8, hidden, device=dev, dtype=dt))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("SubspaceProjection forward sanity check failed: %s", e)
            forward_check_success = False

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Stats (SP-FIX-09)
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        try:
            self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        except Exception:
            self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        avg_err = (
            sum(self.layer_errors.values()) / max(1, len(self.layer_errors))
            if self.layer_errors
            else 0.0
        )
        storage_ratio = total_orig_bytes / max(1, total_comp_bytes) if total_comp_bytes > 0 else 1.0
        effective_ratio = (
            total_comp_bytes / max(1, total_orig_bytes) if total_orig_bytes > 0 else 1.0
        )

        try:
            stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        except Exception:
            stats = {}

        stats.update(
            {
                "compressed_layer_count": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "fallback_layer_count": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
                "skipped_layer_count": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "subspace_rank": self.rank,
                "subspace_method": self.method,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "lora_r": self.lora_r,
                "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
                "orig_storage_bytes": total_orig_bytes,
                "comp_storage_bytes": total_comp_bytes,
                "storage_compression_ratio": round(storage_ratio, 3),
                "effective_parameter_ratio": round(effective_ratio, 4),
                "storage_reduction_pct": round(100.0 * (1.0 - effective_ratio), 2),
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "method": "subspace_projection",
            }
        )
        self.stats = stats
        return self.model, stats

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress_linear(self, linear: nn.Module) -> SubspaceLinear:
        return SubspaceLinear.from_linear(
            linear,
            rank=self.rank,
            method=self.method,
            use_lora=self.use_lora,
            lora_r=self.lora_r,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
        )

@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("swift_svd_act")
class SwiftSVDActivationCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    """
    Swift-SVD Activation-Aware Compressor.

    Captures empirical input-output cross-covariances across calibration data,
    derives activation-aligned subspaces, projects weights onto the principal
    activation subspace, and replaces dense nn.Linear / Conv1D layers with
    factorized SVDLinear modules.
    """

    # SW-FIX-09
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        tokenizer: Any | None = None,
        calibration_loader: Any | None = None,
        calibration_texts: list[str] | None = None,
        rank: int = 64,
        max_error: float = 0.50,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        skip_attention_qkv: bool = False,
        min_rank: int = 4,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        num_calibration_batches: int = 8,
        max_calibration_tokens: int = 32768,
        use_fp64_cross_cov: bool = True,  # SW-FIX-16
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="swift_svd_act", **kwargs)
        cfg = kwargs.get("comp_config", kwargs.get("cfg"))
        if cfg is not None:
            rank = _cfg_get(cfg, "swift_svd_act_rank", _cfg_get(cfg, "rank", rank))
            tokenizer = _cfg_get(cfg, "tokenizer", tokenizer)
            calibration_loader = _cfg_get(cfg, "calibration_loader", calibration_loader)
            calibration_texts = _cfg_get(cfg, "calibration_texts", calibration_texts)
            max_error = _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error)
            fallback_to_original = _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original)
            target_attention_only = _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only)
            lora_r = _cfg_get(cfg, "lora_r", lora_r)
            lora_alpha = _cfg_get(cfg, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha)
            skip_attention_qkv = _cfg_get(cfg, "skip_attention_qkv", skip_attention_qkv)
            min_rank = _cfg_get(cfg, "min_rank", min_rank)
            num_calibration_batches = _cfg_get(
                cfg, "num_calibration_batches", num_calibration_batches
            )
            max_calibration_tokens = _cfg_get(cfg, "max_calibration_tokens", max_calibration_tokens)
            use_fp64_cross_cov = _cfg_get(cfg, "use_fp64_cross_cov", use_fp64_cross_cov)

        if "rank" in kwargs and kwargs["rank"] is not None:
            rank = kwargs["rank"]

        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.calibration_loader = calibration_loader
        self.calibration_texts = calibration_texts
        self.rank = int(rank)
        self.max_error = float(max_error)
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(fallback_to_original)
        self.target_attention_only = bool(target_attention_only)
        self.lora_r = max(0, int(lora_r))
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.skip_attention_qkv = bool(skip_attention_qkv)
        self.min_rank = max(1, int(min_rank))
        self.use_fp64_cross_cov = bool(use_fp64_cross_cov)

        # SW-FIX-12: empty list → None
        if target_modules is not None and len(target_modules) == 0:
            target_modules = None
        self.target_modules = target_modules

        self.num_calibration_batches = max(1, int(num_calibration_batches))
        self.max_calibration_tokens = max(1, int(max_calibration_tokens))

        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.skipped_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.replaced_layers: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.stats: dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._compressed_params: int = 0
        self._orig_storage_bytes: int = 0
        self._comp_storage_bytes: int = 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Target selection
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _is_gqa_asymmetric(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        # SW-FIX-11: always active, not gated by skip_attention_qkv.
        cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
        if cfg is None:
            return False
        num_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_attention_heads", None)
        num_kv_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_key_value_heads", None)
        if num_heads is None or num_kv_heads is None:
            return False
        if num_kv_heads >= num_heads:
            return False
        name_lower = name.lower()
        return any(
            p in name_lower
            for p in ("q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "query", "key", "value", "c_attn")
        )

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        name_l = name.lower()
        if any(
            k in name_l
            for k in (
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embed",
                "logits_processor",
            )
        ):
            return False

        if isinstance(
            module,
            (nn.LayerNorm, nn.RMSNorm, nn.Embedding, nn.BatchNorm1d, nn.BatchNorm2d),
        ):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            attn_keywords = (
                "attn",
                "attention",
                "q_proj",
                "k_proj",
                "v_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "c_attn",
                "c_proj",
                "query",
                "key",
                "value",
            )
            if not any(k in name_l for k in attn_keywords):
                return False

        base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(base_mod, nn.Linear) or "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
        )
        if not is_linear_like:
            return False
        if isinstance(base_mod, SVDLinear):
            return False

        try:
            if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
                return False
        except Exception:
            pass

        if self._is_gqa_asymmetric(name, module):
            return False

        if self.target_modules is None:
            return True
        return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Calibration batch normalization (SW-FIX-02)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_calibration_batches(calib_inputs: Any) -> list[Any]:
        """
        Convert whatever `get_calibration_tensors` returns into a list of batches.

        SW-FIX-02: A single tuple like `(input_ids, attention_mask)` is treated
        as ONE batch, not as two batches. Detection rule: a batch is a tuple/
        list ONLY IF its elements are themselves tensors/dicts AND the tuple
        length is small (≤ 3). Otherwise, the top-level container is a list of
        batches.
        """
        if calib_inputs is None:
            return []

        # Single tensor / dict / small tuple → single batch
        if isinstance(calib_inputs, torch.Tensor):
            return [calib_inputs]
        if isinstance(calib_inputs, dict):
            return [calib_inputs]

        if isinstance(calib_inputs, (list, tuple)):
            if len(calib_inputs) == 0:
                return []

            # Case A: top-level list/tuple where every element is a batch
            # (dict, tensor, or tuple-of-tensors)
            all_batches = all(
                isinstance(x, (dict, torch.Tensor, list, tuple)) for x in calib_inputs
            )
            if all_batches:
                # Distinguish "list of batches" from "single batch as tuple".
                # Heuristic: single batch if the outer tuple contains only
                # tensors (e.g., (input_ids, attention_mask)).
                if (
                    all(isinstance(x, torch.Tensor) for x in calib_inputs)
                    and len(calib_inputs) <= 3
                ):
                    return [calib_inputs]
                # Otherwise, it's a list of batches.
                return list(calib_inputs)

        return [calib_inputs]

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Activation collection (SW-FIX-04, SW-FIX-07, SW-FIX-16)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _collect_activations(self) -> dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor, int]]:
        """
        Collect activations and accumulate cross-covariance C = E[Y^T X] per layer.

        Returns {name: (cross_cov_fp32, n_tokens)}.
        cross_cov shape: [out_features, in_features] in fp32 on CPU.
        """
        accum: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
        hooks = []
        compute_dtype = torch.float64 if self.use_fp64_cross_cov else torch.float32

        def make_hook(layer_name: str):
            def hook(module: nn.Module, inp: tuple[Any, ...], outp: Any) -> None:
                if not inp or inp[0] is None:
                    return
                x_in = inp[0].detach()
                if isinstance(outp, torch.Tensor):
                    x_out = outp.detach()
                elif (
                    isinstance(outp, (tuple, list))
                    and len(outp) > 0
                    and isinstance(outp[0], torch.Tensor)
                ):
                    x_out = outp[0].detach()
                else:
                    return

                in_f = x_in.shape[-1]
                out_f = x_out.shape[-1]

                x_in_2d = x_in.reshape(-1, in_f).float()
                x_out_2d = x_out.reshape(-1, out_f).float()

                n = x_in_2d.shape[0]
                if n > self.max_calibration_tokens:
                    x_in_2d = x_in_2d[: self.max_calibration_tokens]
                    x_out_2d = x_out_2d[: self.max_calibration_tokens]
                    n = self.max_calibration_tokens

                if n == 0:
                    return

                if layer_name not in accum:
                    accum[layer_name] = {
                        "cov": torch.zeros(out_f, in_f, dtype=compute_dtype),
                        "n": 0,
                        "in_f": in_f,
                        "out_f": out_f,
                    }
                elif accum[layer_name]["in_f"] != in_f or accum[layer_name]["out_f"] != out_f:
                    # Shape changed across batches → skip
                    return

                with torch.no_grad():
                    cov_local = (x_out_2d.to(compute_dtype).t() @ x_in_2d.to(compute_dtype)).cpu()
                accum[layer_name]["cov"] += cov_local
                accum[layer_name]["n"] += n

            return hook

        target_layers = [
            (name, mod)
            for name, mod in self.model.named_modules()
            if self._is_target_module(name, mod)
        ]
        if not target_layers:
            return {}

        for name, mod in target_layers:
            hooks.append(mod.register_forward_hook(make_hook(name)))

        device = next(self.model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
        self.model.eval()

        try:
            calib_inputs = get_calibration_tensors(
                self.model,
                tokenizer=self.tokenizer,
                calibration_loader=self.calibration_loader,
                calibration_texts=self.calibration_texts,
                device=device,
            )
            batches = self._normalize_calibration_batches(calib_inputs)
            n_batches = min(len(batches), self.num_calibration_batches)

            if n_batches == 0:
                logger.warning("SwiftSVDActivation: no calibration batches available.")
                return {}

            with torch.no_grad():
                for i in range(n_batches):
                    batch = batches[i]
                    try:
                        if isinstance(batch, dict):
                            b_dev = {
                                k: v.to(device) if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v
                                for k, v in batch.items()
                            }
                            self.model(**b_dev)
                        elif isinstance(batch, torch.Tensor):
                            self.model(batch.to(device))
                        elif isinstance(batch, (list, tuple)):
                            b_dev = [
                                v.to(device) if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v for v in batch
                            ]
                            self.model(*b_dev)
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.debug("Calibration batch %d forward failed: %s", i, e)
        finally:
            for h in hooks:
                h.remove()

        aggregated: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor, int]] = {}
        for name, data in accum.items():
            n = max(1, data["n"])
            cov = (data["cov"] / n).float()
            aggregated[name] = (cov, int(n))
        return aggregated

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # LoRA rank detection (SW-FIX-03)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _detect_lora_rank(module: nn.Module) -> int:
        for attr in ("r", "lora_r", "rank"):
            val = getattr(module, attr, None)
            if isinstance(val, int) and val > 0:
                return val
        la = getattr(module, "lora_A", None)
        if la is not None:
            if hasattr(la, "out_features"):
                try:
                    return int(la.out_features)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            if hasattr(la, "weight") and la.weight is not None and la.weight.dim() >= 1:
                return int(la.weight.shape[0])
            if isinstance(la, torch.Tensor) and la.dim() >= 1:
                return int(la.shape[0])
        return 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Rank selection (SW-FIX-01, SW-FIX-13)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _select_rank(self, in_f: int, out_f: int) -> int:
        """
        SW-FIX-13: clear, monotonic rank selection.

        1. max_r = min(in_f, out_f)
        2. Dense break-even rank (where param count of SVD == param count of dense):
             2r(in + out) < in · out  →  r < in·out / (2(in+out))
           We use `param_cap = (in·out) // (in + out) - 1` (a slightly stricter bound
           often used in the codebase) and clamp to max_r.
        3. requested_r = clamp(self.rank, self.min_rank, param_cap)
        """
        max_r = min(in_f, out_f)
        if in_f * out_f > 16:
            param_cap = max(1, (in_f * out_f) // (in_f + out_f) - 1)
        else:
            param_cap = max_r
        param_cap = max(1, min(param_cap, max_r))

        r_lower = min(self.min_rank, max_r, param_cap)
        r_upper = min(self.rank, max_r, param_cap)

        # SW-FIX-13: monotone selection — start from r_lower, aim for r_upper.
        r = max(r_lower, r_upper)
        r = max(1, min(r, param_cap))
        return r

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Per-layer compression
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress_linear(
        self,
        layer: nn.Module,
        act_pair: tuple[torch.Tensor, int] | None = None,
    ) -> SVDLinear | None:
        base_layer = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
        raw_w = base_layer.weight.data
        cls_name = base_layer.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_layer, "nf")

        W = raw_w.t().contiguous().clone().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.clone().float()
        out_f, in_f = W.shape

        r = self._select_rank(in_f, out_f)
        device = raw_w.device
        dtype = raw_w.dtype
        A: torch.Tensor | None = None
        B: torch.Tensor | None = None

        # ---- Activation subspace projection ----
        if act_pair is not None:
            try:
                cov, _n = act_pair
                if cov.shape[0] == out_f and cov.shape[1] == in_f:
                    C = cov.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
                    U_c, _, Vt_c = torch.linalg.svd(C, full_matrices=False)
                    r_act = min(r, U_c.shape[1], Vt_c.shape[0])
                    if r_act > 0:
                        U_act = U_c[:, :r_act]
                        V_act = Vt_c[:r_act, :].t().contiguous()

                        # M = U_act^T W V_act
                        M = U_act.t().contiguous() @ W @ V_act
                        U_m, S_m, Vt_m = torch.linalg.svd(M, full_matrices=False)
                        r_act = min(r_act, len(S_m))
                        if r_act > 0:
                            sqrt_S_m = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S_m[:r_act], min=1e-12))
                            A = (U_act @ U_m[:, :r_act]) * sqrt_S_m.unsqueeze(0)
                            B = sqrt_S_m.unsqueeze(1) @ (Vt_m[:r_act, :] @ V_act.t().contiguous())
                            r = r_act
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug(
                    "Activation subspace projection failed (%s). Falling back to direct SVD.",
                    e,
                )
                A, B = None, None

        # ---- Direct SVD fallback ----
        if A is None or B is None:
            try:
                U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
                r = min(r, len(S))
                sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
                A = U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
                B = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) @ Vt[:r, :]
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("SwiftSVDActivation SVD failure: %s", e)
                return None

        # ---- Error check ----
        W_rec = A @ B
        norm_orig = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()
        rel_err = (torch.norm(W - W_rec) / max(norm_orig, 1e-12)).item()

        if not math.isfinite(rel_err) or rel_err > self.max_error:
            # Try direct SVD (may have better conditioning)
            try:
                U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
                r_d = min(r, len(S))
                sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r_d], min=1e-12))
                A_d = U[:, :r_d] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
                B_d = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) @ Vt[:r_d, :]
                W_rec_d = A_d @ B_d
                err_d = (torch.norm(W - W_rec_d) / max(norm_orig, 1e-12)).item()
                if err_d < rel_err:
                    A, B, r, rel_err = A_d, B_d, r_d, err_d
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("Direct SVD fallback failed: %s", e)

        # SW-FIX-14: preserve original on high error
        if not math.isfinite(rel_err) or rel_err > self.max_error:
            logger.warning(
                "SwiftSVDActivation reconstruction error %.4f exceeded threshold %.4f on %s.",
                rel_err,
                self.max_error,
                getattr(base_layer, "_name", "?"),
            )
            return None

        # ---- Build SVDLinear ----
        effective_lora_r = self.lora_r
        detected = self._detect_lora_rank(layer)
        if detected > 0:
            effective_lora_r = detected

        svd_layer = SVDLinear(
            in_features=in_f,
            out_features=out_f,
            rank=r,
            bias=base_layer.bias is not None,
            dtype=dtype,
            device=device,
            lora_r=effective_lora_r,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
        )
        svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(
            U_factor=A.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
            V_factor=B.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
        )
        if base_layer.bias is not None and svd_layer.bias is not None:
            svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(base_layer.bias.data)

        # ---- LoRA transfer ----
        if effective_lora_r > 0 and getattr(svd_layer, "use_lora", False):
            try:
                src_lA = getattr(layer, "lora_A", None)
                src_lB = getattr(layer, "lora_B", None)
                if src_lA is not None and src_lB is not None:
                    src_lA_w = getattr(src_lA, "weight", src_lA)
                    src_lB_w = getattr(src_lB, "weight", src_lB)
                    dst_lA_w = getattr(svd_layer.lora_A, "weight", svd_layer.lora_A)
                    dst_lB_w = getattr(svd_layer.lora_B, "weight", svd_layer.lora_B)
                    if src_lA_w.shape == dst_lA_w.shape:
                        dst_lA_w.data.copy_(src_lA_w.data)
                    if src_lB_w.shape == dst_lB_w.shape:
                        dst_lB_w.data.copy_(src_lB_w.data)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("LoRA transfer failed: %s", e)

        svd_layer.reconstruction_error = rel_err
        return svd_layer

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Weight-sharing detection
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _get_weight_tensor(module: nn.Module) -> torch.Tensor | None:
        base = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        return getattr(base, "weight", None)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main compress
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress(self, **kwargs: Any) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.skipped_layers = []
        self.layer_errors = {}
        self.replaced_layers = []
        self._orig_storage_bytes = 0
        self._comp_storage_bytes = 0
        seen_weights: set[int] = set()

        target_layers: list[tuple[str, nn.Module]] = []
        for name, mod in list(self.model.named_modules()):
            if not self._is_target_module(name, mod):
                continue
            w = self._get_weight_tensor(mod)
            if w is None:
                continue
            w_id = id(w)
            if w_id in seen_weights:
                self.skipped_layers.append(name)
                continue
            seen_weights.add(w_id)
            target_layers.append((name, mod))

        if not target_layers:
            logger.warning("SwiftSVDActivation: no compressible linear layers found.")
            return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

        activations = self._collect_activations()

        for name, module in target_layers:
            act_pair = activations.get(name, None)
            base_layer = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            raw_w = base_layer.weight.data

            # Original bytes
            orig_bytes = raw_w.numel() * raw_w.element_size()
            if getattr(base_layer, "bias", None) is not None:
                orig_bytes += base_layer.bias.numel() * base_layer.bias.element_size()
            self._orig_storage_bytes += orig_bytes

            svd_layer = self.compress_linear(module, act_pair=act_pair)
            if svd_layer is not None:
                err = getattr(svd_layer, "reconstruction_error", 0.0)
                self.layer_errors[name] = err
                self.replaced_layers.append(
                    {
                        "name": name,
                        "rank": svd_layer.rank,
                        "error": err,
                    }
                )

                if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                    module.base_layer = svd_layer
                    for attr in ("lora_A", "lora_B"):
                        sub = getattr(module, attr, None)
                        if sub is None:
                            continue
                        params = sub.parameters() if hasattr(sub, "parameters") else iter([sub])
                        for p in params:
                            if hasattr(p, "requires_grad"):
                                p.requires_grad = True
                else:
                    replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)

                self.compressed_layers.append(name)
                # Honest byte count
                comp_bytes = 0
                for p in svd_layer.parameters():
                    comp_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
                for b in svd_layer.buffers():
                    if b is not None:
                        comp_bytes += b.numel() * b.element_size()
                self._comp_storage_bytes += comp_bytes
            else:
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                # Original storage retained
                self._comp_storage_bytes += orig_bytes

        # ---- Forward sanity check (SW-FIX-15) ----
        forward_check_success = True
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                dev = next(self.model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
                cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
                vocab = getattr(cfg, "vocab_size", None) if cfg else None
                if vocab is None:
                    vocab = 100
                dummy = torch.randint(0, min(vocab, 100), (1, 8), device=dev)
                try:
                    self.model(input_ids=dummy, attention_mask=torch.ones_like(dummy))
                except Exception:
                    try:
                        self.model(dummy)
                    except Exception as e2:
                        logger.warning("Post-compression forward check failed: %s", e2)
                        forward_check_success = False
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Post-compression forward check warning: %s", e)
            forward_check_success = False

        try:
            self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        except Exception:
            self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        avg_err = (
            sum(self.layer_errors[k] for k in self.compressed_layers)
            / max(1, len(self.compressed_layers))
            if self.compressed_layers
            else 0.0
        )
        try:
            stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        except Exception:
            stats = {}

        storage_ratio = (
            self._orig_storage_bytes / self._comp_storage_bytes
            if self._comp_storage_bytes > 0
            else 1.0
        )
        effective_ratio = (
            self._comp_storage_bytes / self._orig_storage_bytes
            if self._orig_storage_bytes > 0
            else 1.0
        )

        stats.update(
            {
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "lora_r": self.lora_r,
                "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
                # SW-FIX-10: honest accounting
                "orig_storage_bytes": self._orig_storage_bytes,
                "comp_storage_bytes": self._comp_storage_bytes,
                "storage_compression_ratio": round(storage_ratio, 3),
                "storage_reduction_pct": round(100.0 * (1.0 - effective_ratio), 2),
                "effective_parameter_ratio": round(effective_ratio, 4),
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "method": "swift_svd_act",
            }
        )
        self.stats = stats
        logger.info(
            "Swift-SVD-ACT: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, storage_ratio=%.2fx",
            len(self.compressed_layers),
            len(self.fallback_layers),
            avg_err,
            storage_ratio,
        )
        return self.model, stats

Swift-SVD Activation-Aware Compressor.

Captures empirical input-output cross-covariances across calibration data, derives activation-aligned subspaces, projects weights onto the principal activation subspace, and replaces dense nn.Linear / Conv1D layers with factorized SVDLinear modules.

Ancestors

  • BaseCompressor

Class variables

var is_custom_attention : bool

The type of the None singleton.

Methods

Expand source code 
def compress_linear(
    self,
    layer: nn.Module,
    act_pair: tuple[torch.Tensor, int] | None = None,
) -> SVDLinear | None:
    base_layer = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
    raw_w = base_layer.weight.data
    cls_name = base_layer.__class__.__name__.lower()
    is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_layer, "nf")

    W = raw_w.t().contiguous().clone().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.clone().float()
    out_f, in_f = W.shape

    r = self._select_rank(in_f, out_f)
    device = raw_w.device
    dtype = raw_w.dtype
    A: torch.Tensor | None = None
    B: torch.Tensor | None = None

    # ---- Activation subspace projection ----
    if act_pair is not None:
        try:
            cov, _n = act_pair
            if cov.shape[0] == out_f and cov.shape[1] == in_f:
                C = cov.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
                U_c, _, Vt_c = torch.linalg.svd(C, full_matrices=False)
                r_act = min(r, U_c.shape[1], Vt_c.shape[0])
                if r_act > 0:
                    U_act = U_c[:, :r_act]
                    V_act = Vt_c[:r_act, :].t().contiguous()

                    # M = U_act^T W V_act
                    M = U_act.t().contiguous() @ W @ V_act
                    U_m, S_m, Vt_m = torch.linalg.svd(M, full_matrices=False)
                    r_act = min(r_act, len(S_m))
                    if r_act > 0:
                        sqrt_S_m = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S_m[:r_act], min=1e-12))
                        A = (U_act @ U_m[:, :r_act]) * sqrt_S_m.unsqueeze(0)
                        B = sqrt_S_m.unsqueeze(1) @ (Vt_m[:r_act, :] @ V_act.t().contiguous())
                        r = r_act
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(
                "Activation subspace projection failed (%s). Falling back to direct SVD.",
                e,
            )
            A, B = None, None

    # ---- Direct SVD fallback ----
    if A is None or B is None:
        try:
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            r = min(r, len(S))
            sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
            A = U[:, :r] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
            B = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) @ Vt[:r, :]
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("SwiftSVDActivation SVD failure: %s", e)
            return None

    # ---- Error check ----
    W_rec = A @ B
    norm_orig = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item()
    rel_err = (torch.norm(W - W_rec) / max(norm_orig, 1e-12)).item()

    if not math.isfinite(rel_err) or rel_err > self.max_error:
        # Try direct SVD (may have better conditioning)
        try:
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            r_d = min(r, len(S))
            sqrt_S = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r_d], min=1e-12))
            A_d = U[:, :r_d] * sqrt_S.unsqueeze(0)
            B_d = sqrt_S.unsqueeze(1) @ Vt[:r_d, :]
            W_rec_d = A_d @ B_d
            err_d = (torch.norm(W - W_rec_d) / max(norm_orig, 1e-12)).item()
            if err_d < rel_err:
                A, B, r, rel_err = A_d, B_d, r_d, err_d
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("Direct SVD fallback failed: %s", e)

    # SW-FIX-14: preserve original on high error
    if not math.isfinite(rel_err) or rel_err > self.max_error:
        logger.warning(
            "SwiftSVDActivation reconstruction error %.4f exceeded threshold %.4f on %s.",
            rel_err,
            self.max_error,
            getattr(base_layer, "_name", "?"),
        )
        return None

    # ---- Build SVDLinear ----
    effective_lora_r = self.lora_r
    detected = self._detect_lora_rank(layer)
    if detected > 0:
        effective_lora_r = detected

    svd_layer = SVDLinear(
        in_features=in_f,
        out_features=out_f,
        rank=r,
        bias=base_layer.bias is not None,
        dtype=dtype,
        device=device,
        lora_r=effective_lora_r,
        lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
    )
    svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(
        U_factor=A.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
        V_factor=B.to(dtype=dtype, device=device),
    )
    if base_layer.bias is not None and svd_layer.bias is not None:
        svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(base_layer.bias.data)

    # ---- LoRA transfer ----
    if effective_lora_r > 0 and getattr(svd_layer, "use_lora", False):
        try:
            src_lA = getattr(layer, "lora_A", None)
            src_lB = getattr(layer, "lora_B", None)
            if src_lA is not None and src_lB is not None:
                src_lA_w = getattr(src_lA, "weight", src_lA)
                src_lB_w = getattr(src_lB, "weight", src_lB)
                dst_lA_w = getattr(svd_layer.lora_A, "weight", svd_layer.lora_A)
                dst_lB_w = getattr(svd_layer.lora_B, "weight", svd_layer.lora_B)
                if src_lA_w.shape == dst_lA_w.shape:
                    dst_lA_w.data.copy_(src_lA_w.data)
                if src_lB_w.shape == dst_lB_w.shape:
                    dst_lB_w.data.copy_(src_lB_w.data)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("LoRA transfer failed: %s", e)

    svd_layer.reconstruction_error = rel_err
    return svd_layer
